Result: 在两类偏差条件下，OpDis-PS 的重建精度均高于经典正则化方法与学习式预测器；在留出的心脏病理数据上提升幅度最大，说明其泛化性优于依赖算子精确性的基线。

Conclusion: 算子偏差不应被当作信号无关噪声；对偏差的信号依赖结构建模并结合固定先验采样，可提升 ECGI 在算子不确定条件下的鲁棒性与跨病理泛化能力。

Abstract: Electrocardiographic imaging (ECGI) reconstructs heart-surface potentials from body-surface potentials, an ill-posed inverse problem. Diffusion posterior samplers combine a learned prior with an explicit forward operator that they assume to be accurate. In ECGI this operator is only an estimate, because the body changes with respiration and posture, and body composition such as subcutaneous fat is rarely known for an individual patient. The resulting operator discrepancy is structured and depends on the heart signal. We introduce OpDis-PS, a diffusion posterior sampler that models this discrepancy instead of treating it as signal-independent noise. OpDis-PS learns how the discrepancy depends on the signal, folds this into a corrected forward operator, and reconstructs with a fixed prior across all operator conditions. We study two realistic sources of discrepancy, changing lung conductivity and an added subcutaneous-fat layer. Across both, OpDis-PS reconstructs more accurately than classical regularization and learned predictors, with its largest gains on held-out cardiac pathologies.

</details>


### [104] [Fuse-All MoE Inference Kernel with Load Balancing on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17857)
*Rupeng Liu,Bangsheng Tang,Zongwei Zhou,Chengji Yao,Yejin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fuse-All MoE 是一种面向 TPU 的 MoE 内核设计，通过融合专家计算、输出通信与负载均衡来优化 MoE 推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 架构仅激活部分专家，可在不按比例增加每 token 计算量的前提下扩展参数容量；但在 TPU 上，中间数据搬运和专家负载不均限制了 MoE 推理效率，亟需同时优化计算、通信与负载均衡的方案。

Method: 提出 Fuse-All MoE 内核，融合 token 收集、专家投影、门控激活与输出写回，并将专家计算中间结果保留在片上；采用固定 token-slot 目标地址，使调度与专家放置可变化而不改变路由语义。在 PD 分离服务中，prefill 阶段通过调度与专家重分布平衡 EP16 负载，decode 阶段采用对称 EP8TP2 在每颗芯片的两个 TensorCore 设备间共享活跃专家计算。

Result: 在偏斜 EP16 prefill 负载下，内核运行时间由均衡基准的 2.3 倍降至 1.09 倍；在 32–512 个 decode token 范围内，对称 EP8TP2 相比融合 EP16 将内核延迟最多降低 21.3%；配合服务栈优化，整体系统相比 Megablox EP16 基线将服务吞吐提升至多 34.5%。

Conclusion: 通过内核融合、片上中间结果保留、固定 slot 语义以及针对 PD 分离场景的负载均衡与 EP8TP2 并行策略，Fuse-All MoE 有效缓解了 TPU 上 MoE 推理的数据搬运与负载不均瓶颈，显著提升运行效率与服务吞吐。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures activate only a subset of experts for each token, allowing large language models (LLMs) to scale parameter capacity without a proportional increase in computation per token. However, intermediate data movement and uneven expert loads limit the efficiency of MoE inference on TPUs. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce Fuse-All MoE, a TPU kernel design that jointly optimizes expert computation, output communication, and load balancing. The kernel fuses token collection, expert projections, gated activation, and output writes, retaining expert computation intermediates on chip. Fixed token-slot destinations allow scheduling and expert placement to change while preserving routing semantics. In prefill-decode (PD) disaggregated serving, Fuse-All MoE balances EP16 prefill workloads through scheduling and expert redistribution, while symmetric EP8TP2 shares active expert computation between the two TensorCore devices within each chip during decode. Our experiments show that Fuse-All MoE reduces kernel runtime for a measured skewed EP16 prefill workload from 2.3x to 1.09x the same fixed reference time for a balanced workload. Across 32-512 decode tokens, symmetric EP8TP2 reduces kernel latency by up to 21.3% relative to fused EP16. With accompanying serving stack optimizations, the integrated system improves serving throughput by up to 34.5% over the Megablox EP16 baseline.

</details>


### [105] [Disentangling Forgetting from Utility: Lightweight LLM Unlearning via Orthogonal Null-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17864)
*Xiuyuan Wang,Weiming Liu,Zongxin Yang,Fan Wang,Liemiao Jin,Rubin Qin,Wei Chen,Bin Shi,Chaochao Chen,Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UUE框架，将大模型遗忘重构为可控模型编辑：把参数更新投影到保留知识表示的正交左零空间以保证通用能力最小干扰，并用凸的无目标闭式解把遗忘集嵌入推向对跖点，通过轻量可插拔适配器（含低秩变体UUE-L）实现高效遗忘，在TOFU与WMDP上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM保留隐私或危险信息带来安全风险，需要在不损害通用效用的前提下精确删除特定知识。现有基于微调的遗忘方法存在计算低效问题，且由于模型参数中知识相互纠缠，常导致非预期的通用能力下降，难以兼顾遗忘与效用。

Method: UUE将遗忘形式化为可控模型编辑：(1) 将参数更新投影到被保留知识表示的正交左零空间，严格保证编辑对通用能力干扰最小；(2) 不同于定向映射，设计凸的无目标优化目标，将遗忘集嵌入推向其反极点，并推导闭式解析解；(3) 以轻量可插拔适配器实现（含低秩变体UUE-L），不修改冻结主干即可高效部署。

Result: 在TOFU与WMDP基准上，UUE与UUE-L取得更优的遗忘效果，显著超过既有方法。

Conclusion: 以正交子空间约束解耦“遗忘”与“效用”，结合凸无目标闭式编辑与轻量适配器，可在不损伤通用能力的前提下高效实现LLM知识遗忘，为兼顾安全性与实用性提供了有理论依据的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pose significant security risks by retaining private or hazardous information, creating an urgent need for unlearning techniques that excise specific knowledge without compromising general utility. However, prevailing fine-tuning-based unlearning methods struggle to achieve this balance, often suffering from computational inefficiency and unintended utility degradation due to the entangled nature of knowledge within model parameters. To disentangle forgetting from utility, we propose UUE, a theoretically grounded framework that reformulates unlearning as controllable model editing. First, UUE projects parameter updates into the orthogonal left null space of retained knowledge representations, strictly ensuring that the editing process causes minimal interference to the model's general capabilities. Second, distinct from targeted mapping, we introduce a convex untargeted objective that derives a closed-form analytical solution to effectively disrupt undesired knowledge by pushing forget-set embeddings toward their antipodal points. We implement UUE via lightweight, pluggable adapters (including a low-rank variant, UUE-L), enabling efficient deployment without modifying the frozen backbone. Extensive experiments on TOFU and WMDP benchmarks demonstrate that UUE and UUE-L achieve superior unlearning efficacy, significantly outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [106] [Process Mining-Enabled Federated Anomaly Detection for the Industrial IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17869)
*Francesco Vitale,Salvatore Bramante,Francesco Flammini,Hangli Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于过程挖掘的相似度加权联邦聚合方法，用于缓解工业IoT非IID时序数据下的联邦异常检测误报问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦异常检测可整合多节点本地数据以降低误报，但工业IoT场景中不同工厂运行工况与环境现象差异大，导致时序数据高度异质（non-IID）。标准联邦学习无法进行考虑跨节点差异的细粒度聚合，反而抬高误报率。

Method: 以全局节点提供的参考为基准，采用过程挖掘方法评估各本地节点数据与参考的相似度；全局节点据此相似度指标对本地模型进行动态加权聚合，生成个性化更新并分发到各节点。

Result: 在来自不同运行条件制造工厂的工业IoT时序数据上评估：相比孤立本地训练基线F1相对提升46.52%，相比标准FedAvg相对提升11.99%；相比标准联邦聚合，误报相对减少51.24%。

Conclusion: 过程挖掘驱动的相似度加权聚合能有效应对非IID工业时序数据的异质性，在联邦异常检测中同时提升F1并显著降低误报，优于本地独立训练与标准FedAvg。

Abstract: While federated anomaly detection enables the integration of local data insights across diverse nodes to reduce false positives, it inherently struggles with non-IID data. In practice, industrial IoT plants often operate under varying conditions and experience diverse environmental phenomena, resulting in significant heterogeneity in the monitored time series. In such settings, standard federated learning approaches fail to perform nuanced aggregation that accounts for these cross-node differences, ultimately leading to increased false positive rates. In this paper, we propose a process mining approach to evaluate the similarity of local node data against a reference provided by a global node. The global node utilizes these similarity metrics to dynamically weight local models during aggregation, producing personalized updates distributed across the network. We evaluate our approach using industrial IoT time-series data collected from manufacturing plants operating under distinct conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that our process mining-enabled federated anomaly detection pipeline achieves superior performance, yielding a 46.52% and 11.99% relative improvement in F1-score compared to isolated local training baselines and standard FedAvg, respectively, alongside a 51.24% relative reduction in false positives over standard federated aggregation.

</details>


### [107] [Protection-Oriented Assessment of Machine Learning-Based Fault Detection in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17872)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Changhun Kim,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Johann Jäger,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在PROTECT-90数据集上对三种机器学习故障检测器与一个序分量电流参考方法进行保护导向评估：尽管AUC-ROC均超过0.999，但在10⁻³安全预算下漏跳率仍达0.007%–1.99%，清除后误跳率约0.12%–0.16%/窗口，11.3%–19.1%的故障事件存在滋扰性动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力系统保护用ML故障检测器多以聚合指标（如AUC）报告性能，掩盖了误跳与漏跳的实际后果，缺乏面向保护功能的评估框架。

Method: 构建PROTECT-90数据集（90 kV双回线路、9,022个仿真故障事件）；采用五种窗口条件、因果窗口末端标注、按事件分组的交叉验证、固定安全预算下的工作点选择；评估梯度提升树、卷积神经网络、逻辑回归三个ML检测器与序电流参考；报告安全性、可靠性、检测时延、决策裕度及事件级滋扰断言，并分析物理描述量关联性。

Result: 三个ML检测器AUC-ROC均>0.999，但10⁻³安全预算下已发展故障漏跳率0.007%–1.99%；清除后误跳率约0.12%–0.16%/窗口，事件级滋扰断言占11.3%–19.1%；漏跳最一致地与更高故障电阻相关；波形电流仅部分复现解析电流代理的故障电流梯度（秩相关0.62）；无单一拓扑状态或故障相占主导。

Conclusion: 聚合指标会掩盖保护后果，应采用保护导向的评估协议；PROTECT-90仅含故障事件，安全性由故障前与清除后窗口评估，尚未覆盖独立非故障扰动，需后续扩展。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML)-based fault detectors are increasingly proposed for power system protection, yet aggregate metrics can obscure the consequences of false and missed trips. We present a protection-oriented assessment of three ML detectors and a sequence-current reference on PROTECT-90, comprising 9,022 simulated fault episodes on a 90 kV double-circuit line. The assessment combines five window conditions, causal window-end labelling, episode-grouped cross-validation, and operating-point selection under a fixed security budget. It reports security, dependability, detection timing, decision margins, and episode-level nuisance assertions. Gradient-boosted trees, a convolutional neural network, and logistic regression achieve AUC-ROC values above 0.999, yet at a security budget of $10^{-3}$ their developed-fault missed-trip rates range from 0.007% to 1.99%. Post-clearing false-trip rates of approximately 0.12-0.16% per window coexist with nuisance assertions in 11.3-19.1% of episodes. Among the analysed physical descriptors, missed trips are most consistently associated with higher fault resistance within the sampled range. An analytical current proxy suggests a fault-current gradient only partly reproduced by waveform-derived current (rank correlation 0.62). No single topology state or faulted phase consistently dominates within the sampled domain. Because PROTECT-90 contains fault episodes only, security is assessed on pre-fault and post-clearing windows rather than independent non-fault disturbances.

</details>


### [108] [Delayed Effects of Temporary Weight Decay in Muon Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17876)
*Md Rifat Arefin,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Ernie Chang,Chinnadhurai Sankar,Rylan Conway,Aristide Baratin,Irina Rish,Adithya Sagar,Patrick Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 临时优化干预（如限时权重衰减）在移除时可能升高验证损失，但在最终训练预算处反而降低损失；早期衰减会留下持久的权重范数缺口，使相对更新步长长期偏大，因此必须按最终预算而非移除时点来评估此类调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践通常依据干预移除时（或移除后不久）的验证损失来判断临时优化调度（如权重衰减窗口、范数约束等）的有效性。作者怀疑这种评估时点有误导性：临时干预可能产生延迟效应，移除时的观测并不能反映其在完整训练预算下的真实收益，因此需要系统检验"移除时更差、最终更好"这一反转现象是否真实存在、是否跨规模稳定、以及其机制为何。

Method: 在仅解码器 Transformer（162M/405M/1.4B/3.4B）上用 Muon 优化器训练，施加固定时长的临时权重衰减窗口，并系统地把窗口从训练早期移至后期进行比较；在 162M 上考察窗口时长在总收缩量近似匹配时的效应；将 162M 选定的调度不经调参直接迁移到 1.4B，与无衰减和最优采样常数衰减基线比较；进一步做固定范数重放（fixed-norm replay）、范数重置实验，并构建近似范数动力学递推方程来解释相对步长的缓慢收敛；另以 Gram 干预检验同类移除后动态。

Result: ≤1.4B 模型上，把固定时长衰减窗口从早期移到后期会反转其最终效果。162M 中，在总收缩近似匹配时改变窗口时长仍改变最终损失。162M 选定调度迁移到 1.4B：移除后比无衰减差 0.26 nats，训练进行到 73% 时才反超，最终好 0.028 nats，并优于最优采样常数衰减基线。早期衰减造成持续的权重范数缺口，使干预结束后相对步长偏大；近似范数动力学递推能刻画相对步长的缓慢收敛，固定范数重放复现了性能排序与相近的损失差。162M/405M 的范数重置实验表明，衰减窗口末端较小的权重范数解释了大部分最终损失改善。Gram 干预呈现类似的移除后范数与相对步长动态。

Conclusion: 临时优化调度具有显著且持久的延迟效应，移除时的损失不能作为其价值的判据；评估必须在预期的最终训练预算处进行。权重范数与相对步长的慢收敛动力学是这一现象的主要机制，范数重置实验进一步支持范数是关键中介变量。这也提示超参数迁移与调度搜索若按移除时点评估，可能系统性低估或误判临时干预的作用。

Abstract: A temporary optimization intervention can increase validation loss when it ends yet reduce it at the final training budget. We demonstrate this with temporary weight decay in Muon-trained decoder-only Transformers with 162M, 405M, 1.4B, and 3.4B parameters. For models up to 1.4B parameters, moving a fixed-duration decay window from early to late training reverses its final effect. At 162M, changing window duration alters final loss even at approximately matched total shrinkage. Most strikingly, a schedule selected at 162M and transferred without retuning to 1.4B is 0.26 nats worse than no decay when removed, overtakes it only after 73% of training, and ultimately finishes 0.028 nats better while also outperforming the best sampled constant-decay baseline. Early decay leaves a persistent weight-norm deficit, which keeps relative steps larger after the intervention ends. An approximate norm-dynamics recurrence captures the slow convergence of these relative steps, and fixed-norm replays of the measured trajectories reproduce the performance ordering and a similar loss difference. At 162M and 405M, norm-reset experiments show that smaller weight norms at the end of the decay window explain most of the final loss improvement. A separate Gram intervention exhibits related post-removal norm and relative-step dynamics. These results show that temporary optimization schedules can have delayed effects long after removal and should therefore be evaluated at the intended final training budget, not only at removal.

</details>


### [109] [From Local Prediction Error to Final Outputs: A Theory of Expert Sharing in Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17879)
*Tiantong Wang,Zhiren Gong,Yixin Wang,Tiantong Wu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究 MoE 中跨层/跨计算步共享专家带来的局部误差降低能否在最终输出上保留：在可解析的线性回归模型下给出精确的共享与合并判据，并构造出"局部风险下降但最终输出风险上升"的反转案例，同时给出通用路由计算的条件端到端误差界与专家操作归因所需证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型通过跨层或跨计算步共享专家来汇集不同上下文的信息，从而降低局部预测误差；但多步系统的评价标准是最终输出，而非中间步骤。因此需要明确：局部收益在组合（composition）之后是否仍然存在，以及仅凭正确预测与路由标签能否把可复用的操作归因到单个专家。

Method: 1) 在指定的线性回归模型中，针对目标行为不同的"角色"之间共享专家，权衡方差降低与共享引入的偏差，推导出精确的共享与合并判据；2) 在固定线性传播映射下，刻画保留误差方向与相关性的最终输出期望平方风险；3) 构造局部平均误差降低而最终输出误差上升的解析与受控实验反例；4) 给出正交全池化加各向同性噪声条件下局部改进可完整传递到输出的条件；5) 对通用路由计算推导条件端到端误差界，计入分布偏移、周边组件误差与专家选择变化；6) 设计干预实验检验归因，并在低数据符号任务上训练 Transformer 比较 teacher-forcing 单步收益与自由运行最终输出收益。

Result: 理论上构造出严格的"局部到最终"风险反转案例，即共享降低平均局部误差却提高最终输出误差；明确了正交全池化+各向同性噪声下局部改进在最终输出被保留的条件；给出涵盖分布偏移、组件误差与路由变化的条件端到端误差界。实验上：受控实验实现该风险反转；低数据符号任务中 Transformer 呈现统计显著的 teacher-forcing 单步增益，但自由运行的最终输出增益在统计上无法分辨；干预测试可区分成功的多层动作、单专家归因与对未见转移的泛化。

Conclusion: 局部估计改善与最终输出改善并不等价，共享专家的收益取决于角色间目标一致性与传播结构；正确预测加路由标签不足以证明单个专家实现了所归因的操作，需要通过干预与反事实测试提供证据。研究据此划分了三类问题：共享何时改善局部估计、这些收益何时能传递到最终输出、以及归因可复用操作到单个专家需要何种证据。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can share experts across layers or computational steps, pooling training information from different contexts. This can reduce local prediction error, but multi-step systems are ultimately evaluated by their final outputs. We study when these local gains survive composition. In specified linear regression models, we derive exact sharing and merging criteria by balancing variance savings against bias from sharing across roles with different target behavior. Under a common fixed linear propagation map, we characterize expected squared final-output risk, retaining error directions and correlations. We construct cases where sharing lowers average local error yet increases final-output error. Within this setting, we also identify when orthogonal full pooling with isotropic noise preserves every local improvement at the final output. For general routed computations, we derive conditional end-to-end error bounds that account for distribution shifts, errors in surrounding components, and changes in expert selection. Separately, we show that correct predictions and route labels alone do not establish that individual experts implement the attributed operations. Controlled experiments realize a strict local-to-final risk reversal. In low-data symbolic settings, trained Transformers exhibit statistically supported teacher-forced one-step gains without statistically resolved free-running final-output gains. Separate intervention tests distinguish successful multi-layer actions from single-expert attribution and generalization to unseen transitions. Together, these results distinguish when sharing improves local estimation, when those gains survive to the final output, and what evidence is needed to attribute reusable operations to individual experts.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum Graph Neural Networks: Architectures, Trainability, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17886)
*Zeinab Sharifi,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量子图神经网络综述：提出统一形式化框架（编码器—图条件化线路—可观测量—经典头）与排列等变性条件及资源模型，将文献划分为五类架构并系统评估证据，核心结论是设计空间丰富、对称性是最有支撑的归纳偏置，但实验证据薄弱（多为小图模拟、与经典模型持平），尚无优于经典图任务的实证优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2019年首个基于哈密顿量的模型以来，QGNN领域已积累大量架构、应用与性能主张，但支撑这些主张的证据从未被系统评估；本文旨在填补这一空白，用统一框架梳理领域并审计证据质量。

Method: 提出统一形式化，将任一QGNN描述为编码器、图条件化量子线路、可观测量与可选经典头；给出线路层面的排列等变充分条件与资源成本模型；将文献归为卷积、等变、消息传递与循环、哈密顿量、混合五类架构，按量子比特开销、对称性、可扩展性与证据强度比较；并综述对称与几何归纳偏置（排列、旋转、洛伦兹）、表达能力、可训练性、噪声与可扩展性结果，以及高能物理、化学、金融、医疗、地球观测和交通等应用；最后审计数据集与评测实践，提出最小报告协议。

Result: 设计空间丰富且对称性为最有证据支撑的归纳偏置；但证据整体薄弱：结果多为模拟、小规模图、与经典模型持平或仅以更少参数达成，尚无在经典图数据任务上确立的量子优势。数据集与评测实践不一致，导致结论可比性不足。

Conclusion: 提出五项开放挑战与研究议程：可证明优势、等变与几何设计、容错实现、共享基准与最小报告协议；综述覆盖至2026年并侧重近期工作。

Abstract: Quantum graph neural networks (QGNNs) apply parametrized quantum circuits to graph-structured data, with the aim of overcoming limits of classical graph neural networks in scalability, expressivity, and long-range information flow. Since the first Hamiltonian-based model in 2019, the field has produced many architectures, applications, and claims, but the evidence behind them has not been assessed systematically. This survey introduces a unified formalism that describes every QGNN as an encoder, a graph-conditioned circuit, observables, and an optional classical head, together with a circuit-level condition for permutation equivariance and a resource model for comparing costs. We organize the literature into five architecture families (convolutional, equivariant, message-passing and recurrent, Hamiltonian, and hybrid) and compare them by qubit cost, symmetry, scalability, and evidence. We then review symmetry and geometric inductive biases (permutation, rotation, and Lorentz), the results on expressivity, trainability, noise, and scalability, and applications in high-energy physics, chemistry, finance, healthcare, earth observation, and transportation. An audit of datasets and evaluation practices leads to a minimum reporting protocol. Our main finding is that the design space is rich and symmetry is the best-supported inductive bias, whereas the evidence remains thin: results are mostly simulated, on small graphs, at parity with classical models or with fewer parameters, and no advantage has yet been established on tasks over classical graph data. We identify five open challenges and a research agenda covering provable advantage, equivariant and geometric designs, fault-tolerant implementations, and shared benchmarks. The coverage extends through 2026 and emphasizes recent work.

</details>


### [111] [TIDE: Reclaiming Lost Performance In On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17924)
*Mathew Jacob,Morgan Borjigin-Wang,Kan Zhu,Po Hao Chen,Yi Pan,Andy Ruan,Nayani Modugula,Ethan Lavi,Luis Ceze,Malte Schwarzkopf,Akshay Narayan,Deepti Raghavan,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIDE是一个RL后训练系统，通过动态回收长尾rollout所占GPU用于训练、并在推理引擎间迁移rollout提升解码吞吐，在保持on-policy语义的前提下恢复资源利用率，取得最高1.36x端到端加速、达到最优吞吐的97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前同步、同址（colocated）的LLM强化学习后训练因长尾rollout导致严重的资源闲置：推理引擎要么空等长尾rollout完成，要么以低效的小batch进行解码，整体利用率低下。

Method: TIDE采用两项互补技术：(1) 在长尾rollout尚未完成时，动态将rollout所用GPU改作训练用途；(2) 在推理引擎之间动态迁移rollout以提升解码吞吐。系统通过自适应迁移策略自动决定回收rollout资源的时机，无需针对性手工配置即可泛化到多种应用与硬件配置。

Result: 评估中，相对同址系统端到端加速最高达1.36x，优于所有对比的on-policy基线，并达到最优吞吐的97%。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度资源复用与rollout迁移，TIDE在不牺牲on-policy训练语义的情况下显著缓解长尾造成的利用率损失，具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: Synchronous, colocated RL post-training of large language models (LLMs) today suffers from severe under-utilization due to long-tail rollouts. Inference engines fall idle waiting for long-tail rollouts or decode at low, inefficient batch sizes. To address this, we present TIDE, an RL training system that preserves on-policy training semantics while recovering resource utilization. TIDE does so by two complementary techniques: (1) dynamically repurposing rollout GPUs for training while long-tail rollouts complete and (2) dynamically migrating rollouts between inference engines to boost decoding throughput. TIDE automatically determines the right time to repurpose rollout resources through an adaptive migration policy, obtaining performance improvements that generalize across multiple applications and hardware configurations without requiring bespoke configuration. In our evaluation, TIDE provides end-to-end speedups of up to 1.36x over colocated systems, outperforming all evaluated on-policy baselines and achieving up to 97% of optimal throughput.

</details>


### [112] [Differentially Private Federated Class-Incremental Learning for EEG Mental State Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17944)
*Baran Can Gül,Ali Gülümser,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将差分隐私联邦学习引入“小规模客户端联盟”的可穿戴脑电场景，提出FedEEG（突触智能巩固+滞后自适应FedProx）与头作用域客户端级DP，在5客户端联邦上实现类别增量学习，兼顾防遗忘、可复现的类别范围诊断与更低的ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习与差分隐私研究多面向大客户端群体，依赖子采样和跨客户端平均来实现隐私放大与噪声平滑；而可穿戴脑电等个人生物信号应用通常只有少数受试者构成的小型联邦，该体制下客户端级DP的实际行为与效用—隐私权衡缺乏刻画。同时，类别增量流式训练会带梯度退化与负监督缺失问题，并在小联盟中加剧灾难性遗忘。

Method: 1) 构建K=5客户端联邦，混合公开脑电数据集与自建四通道MUSE 2采集数据；2) 设计掩码式逐类训练协议，避免类别增量流中的退化梯度与负监督缺失；3) 提出FedEEG，将Synaptic Intelligence巩固机制与滞后自适应FedProx正则项结合；4) 采用端上平衡批回放进一步降低遗忘；5) 进行类别作用域（class-scoping）诊断，用6个随机种子评估稳定性；6) 提出头作用域（head-scoped）客户端级DP，仅对分类头加噪保护并与更少通信轮次配合。

Result: FedEEG相对FedAvg、FedProx及联邦版Learning-without-Forgetting基线降低灾难性遗忘；端上平衡批回放使平均遗忘再降56.7%。类别作用域诊断取得50.5%±3.6%准确率（6种子），为随机水平的2.02倍。头作用域客户端级DP在相同轮次预算下可将可达ε降低200倍，结合更少通信轮次达到ε≈10.4，且效用与非隐私训练无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 小联盟体制下的联邦学习与客户端级DP具有区别于大群体的独特特性，不能直接沿用子采样隐私放大的假设；通过针对性的巩固与回放策略可有效抑制类别增量遗忘，而将DP保护范围收缩至分类头是提升隐私—效用—通信效率权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) under differential privacy (DP) is typically evaluated on large client cohorts, where subsampling and cross-client averaging amplify privacy and smooth injected noise. Personal biosignal applications such as wearable EEG instead involve small federations of a handful of subjects, a regime whose interaction with formal client-level DP is largely uncharacterized. We study differentially private federated class-incremental learning in this small-cohort regime, combining a public EEG dataset with a custom four-channel MUSE 2 recording set into a K=5-client federation, and give a masked per-class training protocol that avoids degenerate gradients and missing negative supervision under class-incremental streaming. We propose FedEEG, combining Synaptic-Intelligence consolidation with a lagged adaptive FedProx term, and show it reduces catastrophic forgetting relative to FedAvg, FedProx, and a federated Learning-without-Forgetting baseline; on-device balanced-batch replay reduces mean forgetting by a further 56.7%. A class-scoping diagnosis yields $50.5\%\pm3.6\%$ accuracy with 6 seeds and 2.02 times chance. We also introduce head-scoped client-level DP, which protects the classification head and alone reduces the achievable $\varepsilon$ by 200 times at a matched round budget; combined with fewer communication rounds it reaches $\varepsilon\!\approx\!10.4$ with utility not statistically distinguishable from non-private training.

</details>


### [113] [Reasoning Models Confabulate to Defend Injected Beliefs About Their Own Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17958)
*Yang Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：一个在提示文本中不可见的激活编辑（注入"形成轨迹"）可以切断思维链（CoT）监控所依赖的关键锚点——模型对自身输入请求的指代。注入的主题会取代真实请求，成为模型推理、自我报告与最终答案的对象，且警告、注意力与电路归因都难以揭示这一偏移，构成对CoT可监控性的具体限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI监督方案（CoT监控与自我报告）隐含一个假设：模型对"自己在做什么"的叙述锚定于"它被问了什么"。若该锚点可被切断，则CoT监控与自我报告的可靠性基础不成立。作者意在检验这一锚点的稳健性，并揭示其失效机理。

Method: 采用激活层编辑（activation edit）在提示文本中不可见地注入"偏离主题概念的forming-trace"，覆盖6个在规模、架构与训练配方上各异的推理模型；通过强制引用回读（quote-back）、最终答案、警告实验（含直接点名真实主题）、纯文本给出真相等方式测试行为影响；并结合注意力分析与电路归因（circuit attribution）考察内部机制。

Result: 强制回读在48个"模型-概念"单元中的47个返回注入主题；在4个最终输出干净haiku的模型上，注入答案在99%的生成中出现。警告几乎无效：即便点名真实主题，真实主题答案占比仍低于5%。即使以干净token给出真相，模型仍编造用户解释（如"用户可能搞混后自我更正"）并继续按注入作答，内外行为同时被污染。机制上：模型编辑后对真实请求的注意力反而更强，但该注意力因果惰性；注入内容沿完整任务电路传导，故注意力图与电路归因均显示"在任务上"；同一编辑还会改写请求的语言与格式，而非仅主题。注入真正触及的是模型对自身输入的指代，激发的是为输入作辩护的倾向。

Conclusion: CoT监控与自我报告所依赖的"请求锚定"可被不可见的激活编辑切断，且现有可解释性手段（注意力图、电路归因）无法暴露该偏移。这构成对CoT可监控性的具体硬限制：模型会自信地就错误任务推理、报告并作答。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring and self-report are proposed as levers for AI oversight, on the assumption that a model's account of what it is doing is anchored to what it was asked. We show this anchor can be cut. An activation edit invisible in the prompt text, the injected forming-trace of an off-topic concept, overwrites a reasoning model's belief about its own request: across six models spanning scale, architecture, and recipe, the injected topic becomes the task it reasons about, reports back, and answers: the forced quote-back returns the injected topic in 47 of 48 model-concept cells, and on the four models with clean final haiku the injected answer follows in 99% of generations. Warnings barely move it: even one naming the true topic yields under 5% true-topic answers. Handed the truth in clean tokens, the model confabulates a story about the user ("maybe they got confused and corrected themselves") and answers with the injection anyway, its behavior and chain-of-thought corrupted together: a concrete limit on CoT monitorability. Opening the model up offers no reprieve: it attends to the true request even harder after edits, yet that attention is causally inert, and the injected content rides intact task circuits, so attention maps and circuit attributions read as on-task too; the same edit redirects the request's language and format, not only its topic. What the injection reaches is the model's reference to its own input; what it elicits is its defense of it.

</details>


### [114] [DeltaVisionX: A Low-Cost Vision-Guided Automation System Using YOLOv11 and PLC-Based Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17959)
*Lara Sobh,Marc Nahhas,Francisco Katach El Halabi,Michel Owayjan,Fadi Elias Sleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出低成本工业自动化系统DeltaVisionX，将YOLOv11实时视觉检测与Delta DVP-SE PLC控制的气动分选执行相结合，实现传送带目标的分类与分拣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低对人工分拣的依赖、提升制造效率，并采用本地可获得的低成本硬件替代昂贵商用方案。

Method: 自建数据集（标注与增强）训练YOLOv11进行传送带目标检测；检测结果通过通信发送至Delta DVP-SE PLC驱动气动活塞完成分选；采用基于编码器的跟踪保证检测与执行的同步。

Result: mAP达0.97，单帧推理约100 ms，吞吐量达80个/分钟，检测与执行同步可靠。

Conclusion: 低成本本地化开发的分选自动化系统可行，可减少人工依赖并提升效率；创新点在于编码器同步的视觉-PLC集成而非检测模型本身；后续将引入高速Delta机器人与基于EtherCAT的伺服控制进行高级抓取放置。

Abstract: This paper presents DeltaVisionX, a low-cost industrial automation system that integrates real-time computer vision with PLC-based actuation for object classification and sorting. The system uses the YOLOv11 (You Only Look Once) deep learning model to detect objects on an operating conveyor belt and communicates detection results to a Delta DVP-SE PLC, which actuates pneumatic pistons for sorting. A custom dataset was created, labeled, and augmented to train the YOLOv11 model, achieving a mean average precision of 0.97 with real-time inference of 100 ms per frame. Encoder-based tracking ensures reliable synchronization between detection and actuation, supporting a throughput of 80 objects per minute. Results show that affordable, locally developed automation can reduce labor dependency and improve manufacturing efficiency. The novelty of the proposed system lies in its encodersynchronized vision-PLC integration rather than in the detection model itself. Future work includes integrating a highspeed Delta robot with EtherCAT-based servo control for advanced pick-and-place operations.

</details>


### [115] [Analyzing Learning-Dynamics Across Loss and Prediction Choices for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17967)
*Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统解耦了去噪生成模型中"预测空间"与"损失空间"的作用：证明对固定预测目标，改变损失空间等价于时间步重加权；并从秩-m线性瓶颈给出噪声/速度预测存在 D-m 的不可约误差下界，而干净数据预测可利用低维数据结构，由此得到瓶颈容量的必要条件，实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪生成模型可预测 x、ε 或 v，损失也可在这三种空间度量，共九种组合虽代数等价，但去噪精度与样本质量差异显著，现有工作缺乏对"预测空间"与"损失空间"各自作用的清晰拆分，也缺少对架构瓶颈与预测选择交互的定量理解。

Method: (1) 解耦预测空间与损失空间：对固定预测目标，证明更换损失空间严格等价于时间步重加权，并给出该重加权影响生成质量的启发式分析；(2) 分析秩-m 线性瓶颈压缩 D 维含噪输入时的误差下界，结合与预测目标相关的误差转换，导出不同预测目标下对瓶颈容量的必要条件；(3) 在 CIFAR-10、Imagenette、ImageNet-256 上用 U-Net 与 JiT 进行验证。

Result: 实验支持理论预测：在压缩条件下，预测目标的选择显著影响去噪精度与样本质量；噪声预测的误差与理论下界高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究给出了去噪生成模型中架构瓶颈与预测/损失选择如何相互作用的定量刻画：损失空间仅通过时间步重加权起作用，而瓶颈容量约束对噪声/速度预测是刚性的、对干净数据预测则较宽松。

Abstract: Denoising generative models predict clean data ($x$), noise ($ε$), or velocity ($v$), while training losses can be measured in any of these spaces. Although the resulting nine combinations are algebraically related, they can yield substantially different denoising accuracy and sample quality. We disentangle the effects of prediction and loss spaces. For a fixed prediction target, we show that changing the loss space is exactly equivalent to timestep reweighting and provide a heuristic analysis of how this reweighting affects generation quality. We then show that a rank-$m$ linear bottleneck compressing a $D$-dimensional noisy input imposes an irreducible squared-error floor of $D-m$ on noise and velocity prediction, whereas clean-data prediction can exploit low-dimensional data structure. Combining this bound with prediction-dependent error conversion yields distinct necessary conditions on bottleneck capacity. Experiments with U-Net and JiT across CIFAR-10, Imagenette, and ImageNet-256 support these theoretical predictions. Under compression, prediction choice strongly affects both denoising accuracy and sample quality, with noise-prediction errors closely matching the theoretical lower bound. Our results provide a quantitative understanding of how architectural bottlenecks interact with prediction and loss choices in denoising generative models.

</details>


### [116] [When Does Oversampling Help? A Decision Framework for Imbalanced Data Streams Under Label Delay and Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17981)
*Ahmed Awad,Anirudh Sengar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在“类别不平衡+概念漂移+标签延迟”的流式场景中，系统性比较流式过采样与连续重评估，发现重评估已能挽回大部分延迟损失，过采样的边际价值有限且高度依赖数据流特性，方法选择应随流动态重估而非沿用合成基准结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式过采样器通常在“即时标签、先测后训”假设下评估，而在标签延迟条件下的行为（尤其是相对连续重评估的增量价值）缺乏系统研究；实践者面临一个现实决策问题：是否值得额外引入流式过采样，还是仅靠对早期预测的持续重评估即可抵消延迟带来的代价。

Method: 在联合类别不平衡、概念漂移与标签延迟的条件下开展大规模实验对比。构造一个决策框架，围绕三条轴组织：漂移密度、少数类可学习性、延迟相对于精化窗口的比例；并将其操作化为“恢复通道（重评估）”与“少数类覆盖通道（过采样）”之间的竞争。实验覆盖 15 个分类器、6 个合成流与 6 个真实流。

Result: 1）重评估可在漂移活跃流上恢复延迟造成的损失，但在平稳流上无作用；2）过采样相对重评估的边际贡献仅在一种真实数据配置下具有统计可检测性；3）合成流得到的成本—性能 Pareto 推荐无法迁移到真实数据，真实数据上的 Pareto 前沿按漂移机制分区。

Conclusion: 在标签延迟的不平衡流中，连续重评估是延迟代价的主要补偿机制，过采样的额外收益有限且条件性强。方法选择应依据数据流自身剖面（漂移密度、少数类可学习性、延迟/精化窗口比）进行条件化决策，并随流演化持续重估，而非直接继承合成基准的结论。

Abstract: For imbalanced data streams with delayed labels, is it worth adding a streaming oversampler, or does continuous re-evaluation of earlier predictions already recover the cost of the delay? We address this through an extensive experimental comparison of streaming oversampling and continuous re-evaluation under joint class imbalance, concept drift, and label delay. Streaming oversamplers are typically evaluated under the test-then-train assumption of immediate labels; their behaviour under these conditions, assessed through re-evaluation, has not been systematically studied. We close this gap with a decision framework that predicts when oversampling adds value over re-evaluation, organised around three axes: drift density, minority-class learnability, and delay relative to the refinement window, and operationalised as a competition between a recovery channel (re-evaluation) and a minority-coverage channel (oversampling). Evaluating $15$ classifiers on $6$ synthetic and $6$ real streams, we find: re-evaluation recovers delay-induced loss on drift-active streams but is silent on stationary ones; oversampling's marginal contribution over re-evaluation is statistically detectable in only one real-data configuration; and cost--performance Pareto recommendations from synthetic streams do not transfer to real data, where fronts partition by drift regime. Method selection should therefore be conditioned on the stream's profile and re-estimated as it evolves, not inherited from synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [117] [RiverScan: Learning to Accumulate on River Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18005)
*He Zhu,Zhiyi Gao,Youhuan Yang,Han Quan,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RiverScan，一种基于面积加权选择性状态空间递归的河网空间算子，按拓扑序递归累积上游信息，在 608 个站点的多瑙河流域径流预测中优于时序与图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度流域径流预测需同时刻画异质性子流域的局部水文响应及其向下游的累积效应。河网连通性提供天然结构，但有效利用它需解决如何组合不同大小子流域贡献的问题，即以"学习如何累积"为核心挑战。

Method: 将问题建模为学习式累积，提出 RiverScan：以子流域面积占比作为权重、以选择性状态空间递归为骨架的空间算子；按河网拓扑序，将局部时间表示与累积的上游状态递归组合，并通过可学习的保留（retention）机制控制上游信息向下游的持续程度。采用结构控制与消融实验验证。

Result: 在多瑙河流域 608 个站点的实验中，RiverScan 整体预测性能优于所评估的时序与图基线；结构与消融实验表明，按水文累积方式组合异质子流域表示可显著提升径流预测，且正确拓扑带来的收益沿河网层级递增。

Conclusion: 物理结构化的累积机制是一种有效的水文建模归纳偏置，可支撑更细粒度、完全分布式的 AI 水文模型。

Abstract: Accurate runoff prediction across large river basins requires models to capture heterogeneous local hydrological responses and their accumulation downstream. River-network connectivity provides a natural structure for this task, but using it effectively requires learning how to combine contributions from subcatchments of different sizes. We frame this challenge as learning to accumulate and introduce RiverScan, a spatial operator based on area-weighted selective state-space recurrence. RiverScan recursively combines local temporal representations with accumulated upstream states in topological order. Contributing-area fractions account for subcatchment size, while learned retention controls how upstream information persists downstream. Experiments on 608 stations in the Danube basin show that RiverScan outperforms the evaluated temporal and graph baselines in overall predictive performance. Structural controls and ablations demonstrate that composing heterogeneous subcatchment representations in a manner aligned with hydrological accumulation substantially improves runoff prediction, with the benefits of correct topology growing along the river-network hierarchy. These findings establish physically structured accumulation as an effective inductive bias for hydrological modeling and motivate finer-grained, fully distributed AI-based hydrological models.

</details>


### [118] [Sample-Efficient Control of Magnetoconvective Flows via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18008)
*Jannis Becktepe,Dmitry Krasnov,Nils Thuerey,Jörg Schumacher,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微分、GPU加速的MHD求解器φ-PICT，用于主动流动控制，实现传热提升>25%与逆设计精度10³倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MHD流体（如聚变包层冷却）中流动控制对优化传热至关重要；机器学习推动的主动流动控制（AFC）发展迅速，但其在MHD流动中的应用几乎空白，缺少高效可微分的仿真基础工具。

Method: 构建面向AFC的可微分、GPU加速MHD求解器φ-PICT，并用MHD标准算例验证其正确性与计算效率；将其集成到FluidGym平台，从而支持基于梯度的优化算法（采样高效）进行流动控制与逆设计。

Result: φ-PICT通过MHD基准验证且计算效率高；集成后梯度算法使传热提升超过25%，逆设计精度比黑盒方法高约10³倍；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 可微分GPU MHD求解器为MHD主动流动控制提供了高效、可扩展的基础设施，显著优于无梯度黑盒方法，并已公开可用以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) fluid flows are central to demanding engineering applications, most prominently fusion blanket cooling, where flow control is a promising tool to optimize key properties such as heat transfer. While active flow control (AFC) has recently advanced through machine learning, its application to MHD flows remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present $φ$-PICT, a novel differentiable GPU-accelerated MHD solver targeted at AFC. We validate $φ$-PICT against MHD benchmarks and demonstrate its high computational efficiency. Integrated into FluidGym, $φ$-PICT enables sample-efficient gradient-based algorithms to improve heat transfer by $>25$% and allows optimizers to achieve $10^3\times$ higher accuracy in inverse design than black-box methods. $φ$-PICT is publicly available at https://github.com/upb-sciml/phiPICT.

</details>


### [119] [Conflict, Feasibility, Faithfulness, Displacement: Four Quantities That Come Apart in Federated Dual-Cone Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18014)
*Grzegorz Gwardys,Karol Wojciechowski,Mateusz Gwardys,Bartosz {Ś}widerski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文检验FedFV、FedGH、FedMGDA+等"多目标梯度聚合消除客户端冲突"的合理性，用UPGrad（一阶不增加任何客户端损失）作为只改变FedAvg更新方向的对照。结果表明：冲突、加权均值可行性、传输更新对客户端梯度的忠实性、更新位移这四个量并不等价；UPGrad的增益仅在完全参与下出现、依赖数据划分与留出数据的使用，均匀权重（对冲突不敏感）即可获得同等或更大增益，所谓公平性效应经多重校正后不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedFV、FedGH、FedMGDA+等将"客户端更新间冲突"作为核心动机，采用多目标梯度聚合器处理冲突，但该论证从未被隔离检验：增益究竟来自消除冲突，还是来自其他同时被改变的因素（权重方式、更新方向、数据划分）尚不清楚。

Method: 以UPGrad（一阶上不增加任何客户端损失）作为干预手段，仅改变FedAvg的更新方向，其余保持不变。系统分离并分别度量被原论证混为一体的四个量：客户端更新间冲突、其加权均值的可行性、传输更新对客户端梯度的忠实性、更新位移。实验覆盖CIFAR-10与Oxford-IIIT Pet，考察Dirichlet划分浓度、完全/部分参与、未用数据是否保留，并设置两个对照：冲突不敏感的均匀客户端权重，以及匹配的随机旋转。

Result: Dirichlet划分下UPGrad在CIFAR-10上提升+0.78 pp（15个随机种子中14个，p<0.001），但仅在完全参与时成立；Oxford-IIIT Pet上+0.52 pp的增益在保留协议未用数据后消失（+0.07 pp）。仅改变Dirichlet浓度时，增益随异构性增强而增大，但冲突最大的划分反而无增益。均匀客户端权重（对冲突盲）即可匹配CIFAR-10增益并超过Pet增益；匹配的随机旋转则两种效果均未复现。无任何公平性效应在多重比较校正后存活，且CIFAR-10上该保证未延伸至客户端梯度。

Conclusion: "消除客户端冲突"不足以解释聚合器带来的增益，冲突、均值可行性、传输忠实性与更新位移应分开建模与评估。增益对参与度、数据留出与权重方案高度敏感，且公平性主张不稳健。作者据此提出该类工作评估时应报告的具体项目。

Abstract: FedFV, FedGH and FedMGDA+ aggregate client updates with multi-objective gradient aggregators to remove conflict between them. We examine this rationale with UPGrad, which increases no client's loss to first order, in a comparison that changes only the direction of FedAvg's update. Four quantities the rationale treats as one -- conflict between client updates, feasibility of their weighted mean, faithfulness of the transmitted updates to the clients' gradients, and displacement of the update -- come apart. Under Dirichlet partitioning UPGrad improves CIFAR-10 accuracy by +0.78 pp (14/15 seeds, p < 0.001), only with full participation; on Oxford-IIIT Pet its +0.52 pp gain vanishes (+0.07 pp) once the data our protocol left unused are kept. Varying only the Dirichlet concentration, the gain grows with heterogeneity, yet the split with the most conflict shows none. Uniform client weights, blind to conflict, match the CIFAR-10 gain and exceed the Pets gain; a matched random rotation does neither. No fairness effect survives multiplicity correction, and on CIFAR-10 the guarantee does not reach the clients' gradients. We state what evaluations should report.

</details>


### [120] [RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$: On-Device Reinforcement Learning Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18035)
*Zexin Li,Xinwei Gao,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RL^RT 是一个面向端侧强化学习的运行时系统，将 actor 与 learner 之间的权重耦合从"停机等待或策略漂移"的二选一，改造为可调参数：通过 refresh bound 限制两次刷新之间 actor 的发布次数，并用带 slack 感知准入的 GPU permit 优先保障 actor、把空闲时间用于训练。在两块 Jetson 板卡、真实刷新开销下，使 16.8M 参数策略的截止期错过率低于 1%（无该机制时为 85%~100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧 RL 在单块嵌入式 GPU 上需与其他负载共享资源，周期执行的 actor 依赖 learner 产出的权重。现有设计只能二选一：停机等待刷新（牺牲 actor 时效性）或放任策略漂移（牺牲正确性），缺少对两者权衡的显式控制手段。

Method: 提出运行时 RL^RT：1）refresh bound 作为策略新鲜度与扰动的调节旋钮，限制两次刷新间 actor 的发布次数；2）GPU permit 机制叠加在任意基础调度器之上，采用 slack 感知的准入控制，优先调度 actor，并利用剩余空闲时间填充训练任务。

Result: 在两块 Jetson 板卡上按真实权重刷新开销评测，16.8M 参数策略的截止期错过率由 85%~100% 降至 1% 以下。

Conclusion: 将 actor–learner 耦合显式参数化并用轻量准入控制实现，可在共享 GPU 的端侧平台上同时保证 actor 的实时性与训练进度，无需改动基础调度器。

Abstract: On-device reinforcement learning runs a periodic actor and a learner on one embedded GPU shared with other workloads. Because the learner produces the actor's weights, existing designs stall the actor or let its policy drift. We present RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$, an on-device runtime that makes this coupling a tunable parameter: a refresh bound limits actor releases between refreshes, and a GPU permit with slack-aware admission prioritises the actor and fills spare time with training atop any base scheduler. Under the real refresh cost on two Jetson boards, RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$ keeps a 16.8 M-parameter policy's deadline misses below 1%, against 85 to 100% without it.

</details>


### [121] [Enrichment-Oriented Virtual Screening of Perovskite Solar Cells through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18043)
*Yezeyuan Zheng,Ziyang Hu,Maojun Sun. Ruijian Han,Jian Huang,Guanhua Chen,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出代理模型引导的强化学习框架，将钙钛矿太阳能电池配方生成视为虚拟筛选，在890个已测器件的邻域内富集高PCE候选配方；top-10选择把平均PCE从19.68%提升至23.74%，PPO/RPPO在top-100短名单的邻域支持命中率达23%/24%，约为纯随机的8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池的实验优化受吸收层组分、器件层结构与工艺组合多样性制约，实验成本高，故希望在制备前通过虚拟筛选优先排序配方；但纯代理模型筛选缺乏对实验可行性与邻域支撑的保障。

Method: 构建代理模型引导的强化学习框架：冻结的TabPFN作为代理器为配方打分并引导策略生成配方；以890个实测器件构成参考池做邻域（相似度）评估；在1,846个器件上做五折评估；将3种学习策略与6种基线在每次运行3,000次代理评估的预算下比较，并统计邻域支持的命中率与相似度匹配比例。

Result: top-10选择使平均PCE由19.68%升至23.74%；K=100短名单中PPO与RPPO的邻域支持命中率为23%与24%（纯随机2.9%的7.9倍与8.3倍），最高的基线L-SHADE与Ax BO仅10%（3.4倍）；9,000个配方中65.4%在相似度0.80下匹配某组分家族，0.90时降至11.7%；增益与弱支持比例并存。

Conclusion: 结果支持以“富集导向”的候选优先排序，但不支持实验性能提升、随机种子鲁棒性或等预算下的优势，说明该框架可作筛选前的排序工具而非性能验证手段。

Abstract: Experimental optimization of perovskite solar cells is constrained by combinatorial diversity in absorber compositions, device layers, and processing, which makes virtual screening attractive for prioritizing recipes before fabrication. We develop a surrogate-guided reinforcement-learning framework that casts recipe generation as virtual screening, enriching candidate shortlists near high-performing experimental neighbors. A frozen Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) guides recipe generation, while a reference pool of 890 measured devices enables neighborhood-based assessment. Five-fold evaluation on 1,846 devices shows top-ten selection raises mean power conversion efficiency (PCE) from 19.68\% to 23.74\%. Three learned policies are compared with six baselines under 3,000 surrogate evaluations per run. In top-100 shortlists, Proximal policy optimization (PPO) and recurrent PPO (RPPO) achieve neighborhood-supported hit rates of 23\% and 24\% (7.9- and 8.3-fold Pure Random's 2.9\%), versus 10\% (3.4-fold) for the baselines with the highest supported hit rate (L-SHADE and Ax BO, tied) at $K = 100$. Gains coexist with weak-support fractions. Of 9,000 recipes, 65.4\% match a composition family at similarity 0.80, falling to 11.7\% at 0.90. Results support enrichment-oriented prioritization but not experimental performance, seed robustness, or matched-budget advantage.

</details>


### [122] [Learning nonlinear interactions and cross-feature couplings with product-unit residual networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18074)
*Ziyuan Li,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出复数乘积单元残差网络（CV-PURe），以显式建模特征间非线性交互，并在合成与真实数据上系统验证其预测精度、交互恢复能力与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLP只能隐式捕获非线性特征交互，导致表征纠缠，削弱鲁棒性与可解释性，因此需要显式建模跨特征耦合的架构。

Method: 将乘性乘积单元与残差连接结合构成复数域网络CV-PURe，以显式表达交叉特征耦合并稳定优化；在交互驱动的合成基准与多组真实数据集上，采用可复现协议评估预测性能与输入级交互恢复，并做配对消融、噪声与学习曲线实验及推理开销测量。

Result: 在5次数据划分×5个训练种子上，CV-PURe在6个科学基准中4个取得最低平均RMSE，Friedman误差较MLP降低53.9%；其交互图将60.7%的总强度分配给已知交互对（MLP为31.0%）；含三阶、四阶乘积及20个干扰项的合成任务上RMSE由0.1376降至0.0797，并在所有重复中恢复全部9个真实成对投影；Bike Sharing与Parkinsons Telemonitoring的消融分析将性能提升归因于时间、日历与季节上下文；噪声与学习曲线实验显示持续增益。

Conclusion: 显式乘性交互与残差稳定化相结合可同时提升预测精度与交互可解释性，且在扰动输入与有限监督下保持优势，计算开销可控。

Abstract: Understanding nonlinear feature interactions is crucial in science and engineering, yet standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) often capture such interactions only implicitly, leading to entangled representations that can impair robustness and interpretability. We investigate complex-valued product-unit residual networks (CV-PURe) that integrate multiplicative product units with residual connections to explicitly model cross-feature couplings while stabilizing optimization. We conduct a systematic evaluation on an interaction-driven synthetic benchmark and real-world datasets and evaluate prediction together with input-level interaction recovery under a reproducible protocol. Across five data splits and five training seeds, CV-PURe achieves the lowest mean RMSE on four of six scientific benchmarks, reducing Friedman error by 53.9% relative to MLP. Its Friedman interaction maps assign 60.7% of total strength to the known interacting pair, compared with 31.0% for MLP. On a synthetic task containing third- and fourth-order products and 20 distractors, CV-PURe reduces RMSE from 0.1376 to 0.0797 relative to a complex-valued MLP and recovers all nine true pairwise projections in every training/sampling repetition. Bike Sharing experiments connect improved prediction to the contribution of temporal, calendar, and seasonal context through paired ablations of both PURe variants. Parkinsons Telemonitoring extends the context analysis to repeated measurements. Noise and learning-curve evaluations demonstrate sustained predictive gains under perturbed inputs and limited supervision, while inference measurements quantify computational cost. This article extends our ICCS 2026 paper, Modeling Nonlinear Feature Interactions with Product-Unit Residual Networks (doi:10.1007/978-3-032-29921-5_6).

</details>


### [123] [Deep Latents, Long Memory: Latent persistence explains temporal correlations in transformer activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18089)
*Francesco Cagnetta,Santiago Acevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用层激活的自相关函数定量刻画LLM隐藏层的"抽象"程度：定义自相关为间隔lag的两个上下文位置激活的平均点积，发现慢衰减尾部随层数增加而增长，且该增长源于对长程持续潜在变量的编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "隐藏层表示的抽象程度随深度增加"这一说法缺少可操作的抽象度量，难以定量检验。作者转而依据"序列的相关性反映生成过程结构"这一原则，寻找可测量的替代指标。

Method: 1) 定义层的自相关函数：lag分隔的两个上下文位置激活的平均点积；2) 沿上下文持久编码的潜在变量会在自相关中产生慢衰减尾部；3) 在具有受控层级潜在结构的合成数据与自然数据上测量跨层自相关；4) 在真实文本中用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征作为潜在变量的代理，考察其持续性与自相关的关系；5) 通过消融持续性的SAE特征检验因果性。

Result: 合成与自然数据中自相关尾部均随层数增长；在受控层级潜在结构的合成数据中，该增长源于对支配长段输入的抽象潜在变量的编码，潜在编码的持续性可预测自相关的定性结构并解释大部分经验度量；真实文本中SAE特征的持续性与自相关强度在跨数据集、跨模型上强相关，持续时间和自相关均随深度增加，说明更深层潜在特征在更长上下文跨度上持续；显式消融持续性SAE特征后自相关下降。

Conclusion: 自相关函数可作为抽象的操作化度量：深层潜在特征持续更长上下文跨度，对应更强的长程自相关；SAE特征持续性—自相关关联及消融结果支持该机制解释。

Abstract: The hidden layers of Large Language Models (LLMs) are believed to encode representations of the data whose abstraction increases with depth. However, in the absence of an operational notion of abstraction, this statement is hard to test quantitatively. Here, we propose an alternative view, building on the principle that the correlations of a sequence reflect the structure of the process that generated it. We define the autocorrelation function of a layer as the average dot product between its activations at two context positions separated by a lag. Latent variables encoded persistently along the context create slowly decaying tails in the autocorrelation, and we find these tails to grow across layers in both synthetic and natural data. In synthetic data with controlled hierarchical latent structure, this growth stems from the encoding of abstract latent variables, which govern long stretches of the input. Indeed, the persistence of latent codes predicts the qualitative structure of autocorrelations and accounts for most of the empirical measures. In real text, we take sparse-autoencoder features as proxies for latent variables, and find that their persistence correlates strongly with the magnitude of autocorrelations across datasets and LLMs. In particular, the persistence time increases with depth, as do autocorrelations, implying that deeper latent features persist for longer context spans. We complement this analysis by explicitly ablating the persistent sparse-autoencoder features and observing a consequent decrease in the autocorrelations.

</details>


### [124] [Efficient Poincaré Convolutions through Patch-Statistics Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18125)
*Lukáš Jochymek,Markéta Vašinková,Petr Gajdoš*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出HNN++中Poincaré卷积的得分仅依赖每个图像块的平方范数及与滤波器的内积这两个统计量，因此可用普通卷积加单通道box filter直接计算，从而免去将重叠图像块映射到Poincaré球的操作；在保持输出与梯度精度的同时，训练更新提速1.5–1.8倍、峰值显存降低38–55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发布的Poincaré卷积实现（HNN++）需先将每个重叠图像块映射到Poincaré球再打分，带来额外的数据搬运与计算开销，成为双曲卷积网络训练的瓶颈；作者希望在不改变所定义评分的前提下降低这一开销。

Method: 理论分析表明，截断后的评分对每个图像块仅通过两个统计量依赖：其平方范数，以及与各滤波器的内积；这两个量可由一次native卷积与一次单通道box filter直接得到。据此提出因子化（factorized）层，不构造ball-valued图像块而精确求值原评分。

Result: 单精度输出与梯度与双精度评估的一致程度不劣于显式参考实现。RTX A6000（两种JAX版本）上，Poincaré U-Net与Poincaré ResNet-20完整训练更新比两种显式实现中较快者快1.5–1.8倍（相对跳过映射的显式实现快1.2–1.4倍）；V100上ResNet-20加宽8倍后仍保持优势。每任务15次配对训练中，更新分别快2.0与1.8倍，最终分割/分类质量无一致变化；U-Net扩展实验中峰值显存下降38–55%；替换官方PyTorch Hyperbolic U-Net的卷积后，训练步提速1.4倍且显存减半。

Conclusion: 通过图像块统计量来求值已有层，可使Poincaré卷积网络在测试的模型与软件栈上训练成本更低，且不牺牲精度与最终任务表现。

Abstract: Published implementations of the Poincaré convolution in Hyperbolic Neural Networks++ (HNN++) map every overlapping image patch to the Poincaré ball before evaluating its score. We show that the clipped score depends on each patch only through two statistics, its squared norm and its inner products with the filters, which a native convolution and a single-channel box filter compute directly. The factorized layer therefore evaluates the specified score exactly, without constructing ball-valued patches, and its single-precision outputs and gradients agree with double-precision evaluations at least as closely as those of the explicit reference. On an RTX A6000 under two versions of JAX, complete training updates of a Poincaré U-Net and a Poincaré ResNet-20 are 1.5 to 1.8 times faster than the faster of two explicit implementations that map patches to the ball (1.2 to 1.4 times against an explicit implementation that skips this mapping); on a V100, the gain over the gather-based one persists as the ResNet-20 is widened eightfold. In 15 paired training runs per task, updates are 2.0 and 1.8 times faster than a reference that follows the published execution order, with no consistent change in final segmentation or classification quality, and peak memory falls by 38 to 55% in a U-Net scaling study. Replacing the convolutions of the official PyTorch Hyperbolic U-Net makes its training step 1.4 times faster with half the memory. Evaluating an existing layer from its patch statistics thus makes Poincaré convolutional networks cheaper to train in the tested models and software stacks.

</details>


### [125] [When Copying Is Hard to Beat: A Negative Result in Clinical Assessment Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18142)
*Marius Fracarolli,Stefan Riezler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个负面结果：在次日临床评估预测任务中，所评估的模型相对强持续性（persistence）基线没有一致优势；结论受文档流程、输入截断、观测掩码与解码规则的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次日临床评估预测常被认为有价值，但临床医生可直接查看前一日问卷答案，形成"文档持续性"这一强基线。作者希望检验现有模型是否真正优于该基线，并厘清以往结论可能被哪些评测设定所左右。

Method: 在29,715个ICU日对上以患者不重叠（patient-disjoint）划分预测45个条目。构建生理信息驱动的门控模型，与单行临床规则及持续性基线对比。设置三类消融/敏感性分析：将生理窗口截断于上一表单参考时间；在评分中剔除68.5%由父条目衍生答案构成的单元格；二者合并。另评估三个模型家族的四个检查点及一个微调模型，并比较类别读出、仅状态读出与转移矩阵。

Result: 门控模型相对单行临床规则在item-macro-F1上提升+0.0019 [+0.0006,+0.0031]，但准确率下降；将生理窗口截断至上一表单参考时间后，macro-F1优势消失（+0.0001 [-0.0006,+0.0008]）；剔除衍生答案单元格后亦无优势；两项合并则出现小幅劣势。四个检查点均不如持续性基线，微调模型仅在两个单元格上与之不同。类别读出使变化单元格准确率提升约八倍，但仅状态读出的总体得分高于仅预测解码，并与转移矩阵相当。

Conclusion: 该负面结果说明，关于记录型评估预测能力的结论高度依赖于文档流程、输入截断时点、观测掩码与解码规则；在控制这些因素后，模型并未稳定超越持续性基线。

Abstract: We report a negative result in next-day clinical assessment forecasting: the evaluated models show no consistent advantage over strong persistence-based baselines. Clinicians can view the previous day's questionnaire answers, a plausible source of documentation persistence. Predicting 45 items on 29,715 ICU day-pairs with patient-disjoint splits, a physiology-informed gated model improves item-macro-F1 over a one-line clinical rule by +0.0019 [+0.0006,+0.0031], while losing accuracy. No macro-F1 advantage is established when the physiology window ends at the previous form's reference time (+0.0001 [-0.0006,+0.0008]), or when scoring excludes the 68.5% of cells derived from parent answers; combining both changes yields a small disadvantage. Four checkpoints across three families underperform persistence, and a fine-tuned one differs from it on only two cells. A categorical read-out raises changed-cell accuracy eightfold, but a state-only read-out scores higher overall than forecast-only decoding and matches a transition matrix. This negative result highlights how the documentation process, input cutoff, observation mask and decoding rule shape conclusions about forecasting recorded assessments.

</details>


### [126] [STAMP: Manufacturing-Informed Generative Design and Shape Optimization of Sheet Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18144)
*Jihoon Kim,Jun Hyeok Song,Wonho Ha,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP 将冲压成形制造约束直接嵌入钣金面板的形状表示（材料布局 + 参考曲面深度图），使生成与优化结果天然满足几何可制造性，并与扩散生成和可微曲面优化结合用于汽车引擎盖内板设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成形钣金件的生成式设计需要同时满足几何制造条件（避免参考面倒扣、保持标称厚度等），同时探索多样布局并提升性能；依赖罚项或数据规模难以保证可行性。

Method: STAMP 表示：每块面板由材料布局与参考曲面构成，参考曲面在垂直于拉延方向的平面内每个被占据位置仅有一个高度值（深度图形式），沿法向给定标称厚度；该表示支持曲面、加强筋和开孔，从表示层面排除参考面倒扣且厚度固定，无需制造罚项。生成采用边界条件扩散模型产生候选布局，再用预测器梯度引导曲面优化，并保留外轮廓、开孔与指定固定区域。在 CarHoods10k 汽车引擎盖内板上验证。

Result: 在相同存储值数量下，深度图重建曲面精度优于点云与体素基线；仅用 5 个训练设计时，三次训练中一次即产生新颖且多样的布局；用 500 个设计（训练集 6.5%）训练的生成器已能达到全数据模型的布局多样性。在 11 个留出设计族上，基于预测器的筛选对现有设计得到相对最优 FEA 变形的平均差距 0.18 mm，随机筛选期望为 1.59 mm，且 11 个族全部优于随机。在 10% 深度变化约束下，未参与搜索的网络对 6 个生成与现有面板预测平均变形降低 24.8%，几何计算的平均质量增加 3.2%。

Conclusion: 将制造条件内嵌于形状表示，可使任何规模训练下生成与优化的面板均满足制造几何约束；工程师可在该制造约束下的设计空间中高效探索与优化面板。

Abstract: Generative design of formed sheet panels must preserve geometric manufacturing conditions while exploring layouts and improving performance. STAMP embeds these conditions directly in the shape representation. Each panel is described by its material layout and a reference surface with one height at each occupied position in a plane perpendicular to the drawing direction. Nominal thickness is prescribed along the normal. The representation allows curved surfaces, ribs, and openings. It excludes reference-surface undercuts and keeps nominal thickness fixed without manufacturing penalty terms. Because these conditions belong to the representation, every generated and optimized panel satisfies them regardless of training-set size. Boundary-conditioned diffusion generates alternative layouts, and predictor gradients guide a surface optimization that retains outlines, openings, and designated fixed regions. We demonstrate STAMP on automotive hood inner panels using CarHoods10k. With a comparable number of stored values, depth maps reconstruct surfaces more accurately than point-cloud and voxel baselines. From only five training designs, one of three training runs yields novel and varied layouts. A generator trained on 500 designs, 6.5% of the training set, already reaches the layout variety of the full-data model. Across 11 held-out design families, predictor-based selection among existing dataset designs yields an average gap of 0.18 mm to the best available FEA deformation, versus an expected 1.59 mm for random selection. It does better than random selection in all 11 families. At a 10% depth-change bound, a network not used in the search predicts an average deformation reduction of 24.8% for six generated and existing panels, with a 3.2% average mass increase computed from geometry. Engineers can thus explore and optimize panels within this manufacturing-informed design space.

</details>


### [127] [Which Model, For Which Variable? Per-Variable Forecast Combination in Sparse ICU Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18149)
*Marius Fracarolli,Stefan Riezler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在五个ICU数据库中，针对94个生理通道的24小时预测任务，按变量拟合权重的多模型组合在测试集上优于任何单一最优架构6.1–11.0%（均值8.7%），且增益集中在数据最稀疏的通道与检验值；无拟合权重的排序谐波加权可恢复约五分之四的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测文献通常只报告单一最优架构，但作者认为这一提问方式掩盖了变量层面的差异：不存在在所有变量上都最优的架构，胜出模型随变量而变。因此需要按变量考察模型选择与组合问题。

Method: 在MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV、eICU、HiRID与UMM（德国单中心ICU）五个数据库上，对94个通道做24小时预测，构建含14个模型的模型池，并采用按变量分别拟合权重的方式进行组合；进一步比较无拟合权重的排序谐波加权（每变量取前五模型）与等权平均；随后将模型池扩展为按通道的梯度提升树、ARIMA与ETS，再做同样的按变量组合。

Result: 组合模型在五个数据库上均优于最优单模型，MSE改善6.1–11.0%（均值8.7%）；增益集中于数据最稀疏处——最稀疏三分之一的通道提升11.9%、检验值提升7.5%，而最密集三分之一仅3.1%。排序谐波加权（无拟合参数）可恢复约80%的增益。扩展模型池后，梯度提升树成为五个数据库上经验证集选择的最优单模型，按变量组合仍在其上再提升2.9–5.9%（五个中四个显著），而同一成员的等权平均则无此效果。结论是学习式组合优于简单平均，但前提是权重受到约束。此外还给出了"何时偏好何模型"的指导，并显示该排序随数据库规模变化。

Conclusion: 临床多变量预测不应以单一最优架构为结论；模型优劣具有变量特异性，按变量约束权重的组合能稳定带来收益，且在数据稀疏场景收益最大。无参数的排序谐波加权可作为低成本近似，等权平均不足，模型偏好排序随数据规模改变。

Abstract: Clinical forecasting papers typically report a single best architecture. We show that on multivariate ICU physiology this question conceals variable-specific differences: no architecture is best on every variable, and which model wins changes from variable to variable. Across five ICU databases (MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, eICU, HiRID, and UMM (University Medicine Mannheim), a German single-center ICU) we forecast 94 channels over a 24-hour horizon with a pool of 14 models, and combine them with weights fitted per variable. On test, the combination beats the best single model on all five databases by 6.1-11.0% mean squared error (mean 8.7%). The gain is concentrated where clinical data is thinnest: the sparsest third of channels improves by 11.9% and laboratory values by 7.5%, against 3.1% for the densest third. Rank-based harmonic weighting of the best five models per variable, with no fitted weights at all, recovers about four-fifths of the gain. Extending the pool afterwards with a per-channel gradient-boosted tree, ARIMA and ETS makes the tree the validation-selected best single model on all five databases; per-variable combination still improves on it by 2.9-5.9% (significant on four of five), while equal weighting of the same members does not. As on univariate benchmarks, learned combination beats simple averaging - but only once the weights are constrained. We also report which model to prefer when, and show that the ordering changes with database size.

</details>


### [128] [On the Limits of Sufficient Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18163)
*Klaus Weinbauer,Peter F. Stadler,Thomas Gärtner,Sagar Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对结构化数据（图、字符串）分类器的充分性解释给出了统一的形式化刻画：定义由实例空间、分类器与选择关系构成的解释框架，证明充分解释器的存在性完全由分类器与选择关系的相互作用决定，并扩展到概率充分性与删除充分性，进而提出可界定任务最大可达充分性的统计量；在语言、蛋白质与分子数据集上发现多数任务无法达到完全充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 充分性解释（选取输入的一部分即可保证预测标签）已在图、字符串、图像等多种模态中被广泛采用，但缺乏统一的形式化分析，导致无法判断某任务上充分解释是否可能出现、解释器的充分性水平是否已达上限。

Method: （i）定义通用解释形式化框架：实例空间、分类器、选择关系（select relation，规定每个实例中可被选作解释的子实例或特征子集）；（ii）分析充分解释器存在性与分类器—选择关系相互作用的充要关系；（iii）将该分析推广到概率充分性与删除充分性等实用变体；（iv）基于形式化分析设计统计量，用于界定给定任务上可达到的最大充分性。

Result: 在语言、蛋白质与分子分类数据集上评估所提出的统计量，发现多数数据集上完全充分性不可达；由于这些上界对任意解释器均成立，可作为任务级参照，用于以绝对标准评判任一解释器的充分性。

Conclusion: 充分解释的存在性由分类器与选择关系的交互决定，可被统一形式化刻画；任务级充分性上界为解释器评估提供了绝对基准，并揭示了多数真实任务中完全充分性不可实现。

Abstract: We formally characterize the conditions under which sufficient explanations exist for classifiers on structured data such as graphs and strings. We focus on post-hoc explainers that select parts of the input as sufficient explanations. An explanation is sufficient if the selected part alone ensures the predicted label. Sufficient explanations have been widely adopted across data modalities, including graphs, strings, and images. However, a unified formal analysis of sufficient explanations remains missing. We bridge this gap with four main contributions: (i) We define a general explanation formalism consisting of an instance space, a classifier, and a select relation. The select relation specifies which potential sub-instances or feature subsets can be selected as explanations from each instance; (ii) We show that the existence of sufficient explainers is fully characterized by the interplay between the classifier and the select relation; (iii) We extend this analysis to practically relevant variants of sufficiency, including probabilistic sufficiency and deletion sufficiency; (iv) Based on the formal analysis, we devise statistics that bound the maximum achievable sufficiency for a given task. We evaluate these statistics on language, protein, and molecular classification datasets and find that full sufficiency is unattainable on most of them. Since these bounds hold for every explainer, they provide a task-level reference against which any explainer's sufficiency can be judged in absolute terms.

</details>


### [129] [MarathonTS: a benchmark of long synthetic time series from window assembly and long-series generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18190)
*Ilyas Varshavskiy,Sergey Shulgin,Shuhrat Khalilbekov,Bonu Boboeva,Azizjon Azimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MarathonTS 是首个在同一协议下同时比较"窗口拼接"与"长序列直接生成"两类合成长时序方法的开放基准，在9个公开小时级数据集、10个随机种子上，从保真度（83项指标）、实用性（TSTR）、记忆性与计算成本四方面评测，并对结果做独立的统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多合成时序应用需要远长于生成模型训练窗口的序列。现有路线一是用一组短生成窗口拼接（需决定选哪些窗口、顺序及接缝处理），二是直接用长序列生成器，但两类方法缺乏统一、可复现的比较平台。

Method: 提出开放基准 MarathonTS：所有方法在9个公开小时级数据集、10个种子下生成等长序列；以83项指标（12组）对照train/validation/out-of-time三个真实参考评估保真度；以14个分类与回归任务的TSTR vs TRTR评估实用性；同时评估对训练数据的记忆化与计算成本。统计分析作为独立阶段，读取已发布表格，给出各切片排名、研究问题的检验与不确定性、以及预先设定的验证性分析族结果。

Result: 主实验运行中，方法在两个验证性单元格上均存在差异；窗口的选择比接缝处理更重要，接缝处理并未提升序列质量；没有方法训练出的下游模型能与真实序列相当。排行榜顶层在其他硬件上可复现，但层内具体名次不可复现。

Conclusion: 窗口选择是拼接式方法的关键环节，接缝处理收益有限，合成数据在实用性上仍不及真实数据；评测结论具有顶层可复现性。代码、数据哈希与全部结果已公开以保证可复现。

Abstract: Many applications of synthetic time series require a series that is much longer than the windows on which a generative model is trained. Such a series can be assembled from a pool of short generated windows, which requires deciding which windows are taken, in what order, and how their seams are processed, or it can be produced directly by a long-series generator. This study presents MarathonTS, to our knowledge the first open benchmark that compares both families under one protocol. Every method produces a series of the same length on 9 open hourly datasets with 10 seeds. The series is evaluated by its fidelity to the real data (83 metrics in 12 groups, against three real references: train, validation and an out-of-time split), by its utility (models are trained on the synthetic series only and tested on real data, TSTR against TRTR, in 14 classification and regression tasks), by its memorisation of the training data and by its computational cost. The statistical analysis is carried out as a separate stage that reads the published tables and gives the rankings of every method in every slice, the answers to the research questions with tests and uncertainty, and the results of a pre-specified confirmatory family. In the main run, the methods differ in both confirmatory cells; the choice of windows matters more than the processing of their seams, which does not improve the series, and no method trains the downstream models as well as the real series. The top tier of the leaderboard is reproduced on other hardware, whereas the exact places within it are not. For the reproducibility of the study, the code, the data hashes and all results are publicly available (links below).

</details>


### [130] [T2ST: Data-Efficient Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models via Temporal Data and Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18199)
*Yue Xu,Kaiyun Wei,Wenying Duan,Zichen Xu,Shuai Ma,Xiaoxi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T2ST数据高效训练框架，利用丰富的时间序列数据并蒸馏冻结的时序基础模型知识，在仅用10%时空数据的情况下使时空基础模型的预测性能媲美甚至超越全量数据训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空基础模型（STFMs）依赖大规模训练语料，但同步观测与空间元数据采集和整理成本高昂，可获取的数据有限；而时间序列数据丰富且易采集，每个站点的观测本身即为一条时间序列，因此希望以充足的时序数据弥补时空观测的稀缺。

Method: 核心思想是：时间模式学习可借助外部丰富时序数据，而空间依赖学习仍以真实时空观测为基础。具体包括：(1) 检索匹配序列，强化稀疏观测下的时间模式；(2) 筛选可靠的补充序列，扩大时间覆盖范围；(3) 时序关系蒸馏，对齐历史patch之间的关系；(4) 多尺度预测蒸馏，在细粒度与粗粒度时间分辨率上迁移预测行为，实现跨异构架构的知识迁移。

Result: 仅使用原始时空数据的10%，T2ST使STFMs在6个长期与12个短期预测数据集上的整体预测性能与全量数据训练的对应模型相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了时空基础模型数据高效训练的新范式，表明借助外部时序数据与基础模型知识蒸馏可显著降低时空基础模型对大规模同步观测数据的依赖。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal foundation models (STFMs) require large-scale training corpora, yet synchronized observations and spatial metadata are costly to acquire and curate, limiting the availability of suitable data. By contrast, time-series data are abundant and easier to collect. Since observations at each location form a time series, this motivates a natural question: can abundant time-series data enhance STFMs when spatio-temporal observations are scarce? To bridge this gap, we propose T2ST, a data-efficient training framework that augments limited spatio-temporal observations with selected auxiliary time series and transfers knowledge from a frozen time-series foundation model. Our key insight is that temporal pattern learning can benefit from abundant external time series, while spatial dependency learning remains grounded in real spatio-temporal observations. Guided by this insight, T2ST retrieves matched sequences to reinforce sparsely observed temporal patterns and selects reliable complementary sequences to broaden temporal coverage. Temporal relation distillation aligns relationships among historical patches, while multi-scale prediction distillation transfers forecasting behavior at fine and coarse temporal resolutions, enabling knowledge transfer across heterogeneous architectures. Using only 10\% of the original spatio-temporal data, T2ST enables STFMs to achieve overall forecasting performance competitive with or superior to their full-data counterparts across six long-term and twelve short-term forecasting datasets. These results establish a new paradigm for data-efficient training of spatio-temporal foundation models. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/T2ST-65F3/.

</details>


### [131] [NeoResearch: Literature- and Experience-Guided Autonomous Time Series Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18235)
*Ze Guo,Mingyue Cheng,Xiaoyu Tao,Shuo Yu,Shangtong Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeoResearch：一个以文献与经验为引导、以大语言模型为研究者的自主研究框架，发现轻量级领域专用的时间序列预测模型。其双环工作流将假设驱动的架构探索与迭代训练/超参优化耦合，并结合网络文献挖掘与分层经验记忆；在10个数据集上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为不同时间序列领域开发有效预测模型需要大量专家知识、网络科学文献证据与反复实验；而把知识/研究经验转化为模型设计，需保留来源语境并在任务特定假设下验证适用性，这一转化环节缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 1) 专家式双环研究工作流：假设驱动的架构探索环 + 通过实验反馈进行的迭代训练与超参优化环，二者相互耦合；2) 知识落地发现：智能体自主从Web挖掘科学文献，抽取建模机制、经验证据与适用性条件，形成有任务依据、可检验的架构假设；3) 分层经验记忆：将文献发现与实验结果关联，提炼条件化经验（conditional lessons），指导架构发现与训练优化，并支持跨预测任务复用。

Result: 在10个数据集上实验，性能具竞争力；消融/分析支持双环结构、文献抽取与经验记忆复用的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM作为自主研究者、在文献证据与分层经验记忆引导下，能够自动发现轻量且领域适配的时间序列预测架构；文献知识与跨任务经验的可迁移复用是提升自动化模型设计效果的关键。

Abstract: Developing effective forecasting models for diverse time-series domains requires substantial expert knowledge, evidence from scientific literature on the web, and iterative experimentation. However, translating this knowledge and research experience into model designs requires preserving source context and testing applicability under task-specific assumptions. In this paper, we propose NeoResearch, a literature- and experience-guided autonomous research framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) as researchers to discover lightweight, domain-specific forecasting models. Specifically, NeoResearch establishes an expert-inspired dual-loop research workflow, coupling hypothesis-driven architecture exploration with iterative training and hyperparameter optimization through experimental feedback. To enable knowledge-grounded discovery, the agent autonomously mines scientific literature from the Web, extracting modeling mechanisms, empirical evidence, and applicability conditions to formulate task-grounded, testable architecture hypotheses. Furthermore, a hierarchical experience memory links literature findings to experimental outcomes and consolidates conditional lessons, guiding architecture discovery and training optimization while supporting reuse across forecasting tasks. Experiments on ten datasets show competitive performance and support dual loops, literature extraction, and memory reuse. Our code is available.

</details>


### [132] [Small Is Not Dead: Distance-Aware Cluster Revival for e-means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18238)
*Eyal Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 e-means（FAISS 中的 mini-batch k-means）的"死簇复活"机制增加了一个基于中心间距的第二判据：只有当小簇中心与另一中心足够接近（相对于运行估计的均方误差）时才删除该簇，从而避免误删覆盖稀有孤立点群的中心，几乎不增加运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: e-means 的消融实验显示，"死簇复活"（每五个 mini-batch 删除计数低于平均值十分之一的簇，并用其中心分裂大簇）是最关键的组件。但该规则只依据簇大小，无法区分"因冗余而小"的簇和"覆盖稀有孤立点群"的簇，在后一类数据上会反复删除代价高昂的中心。

Method: 在计数条件之外增加基于中心间距离的第二条件：仅当小簇中心相对运行估计的均方误差而言与另一中心足够接近时才删除该簇。理论依据是当中心等于其簇内点均值时，该平方距离是删除该中心所导致的簇内每点平均平方误差增量的上界。实现代价为每五个 mini-batch 对小簇做一次最近中心搜索。

Result: 在 e-means 原论文的 embedding 基准上从未阻止任何删除，结果与 FAISS 完全一致；在开发阶段未使用的、含小型孤立群的数据集上，两个 epoch 后的测试误差相比仅基于计数的复活降低约 10%–80%，并保持了 k-means++ 初始化的优势；在其余评测数据集上与"启用/禁用复活"中较优者的差距在约 1% 以内。运行时间无可测量增加。另报告了分析预测的两种失效模式。

Conclusion: 基于距离的第二判据在几乎零额外开销下，使死簇复活机制能区分冗余簇与稀有孤立群，在目标数据上显著降低误差且不损害原有性能；当前工作为 CPU 概念验证，GPU 实现留待未来工作。

Abstract: e-means (Bessa et al., 2026) is a recent mini-batch k-means algorithm, now part of the FAISS library, that reaches the error of Lloyd's algorithm about an order of magnitude faster on large embedding datasets. Every five mini-batches, it removes every cluster whose running point count falls below a tenth of the average and reuses its centre to split a large cluster. In the authors' ablation, removing this mechanism, called dead-cluster revival, raises the error more than removing any other component added to the moving-average update. The rule, however, considers only cluster size: it cannot distinguish a cluster that is small because it is redundant from one that covers a rare, isolated group of points, and on data with such groups it repeatedly removes centres whose removal is costly. We add a second condition, based on the distance between centres: a small cluster is removed only if its centre is close to another centre, relative to a running estimate of the mean squared error. When a centre equals the mean of its points, this squared distance is an upper bound on the average increase in squared error, per point of the cluster, caused by removing the centre. The condition costs one nearest-centre search for the small clusters every five mini-batches and adds no measurable running time. On the e-means paper's embedding benchmarks, it never prevents a removal, so the results are identical to those of FAISS. On datasets with small isolated groups not used during development, it reduces the test error after two epochs by roughly 10% to 80% relative to count-based revival, and it preserves the advantage of k-means++ initialization; on the other evaluation datasets, it stays within about 1% of the better of revival enabled and disabled. We also report two failure modes that the analysis predicts. This is a CPU proof of concept; GPU implementations are left to future work.

</details>


### [133] [Neural Classifiers as Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18239)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin,Renata Avros,Zeev Volkovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将监督深度分类建模为Shannon意义下纠错码的解码问题，建立统一框架并从该视角推导量化、知识蒸馏与结构正则的新准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督分类中交叉熵、softmax与标准散度目标（KL、Jeffreys）的广泛使用缺乏统一解释；决策边界稳定性、Neural Collapse几何与隐式码本学习现象彼此孤立，缺少可贯通分类与自监督表征几何的理论视角。

Method: 提出统一框架：把监督深度分类视为纠错码解码。证明交叉熵优化使softmax分类器与最大似然解码对齐，其中logit对应到类特定码字的Hamming距离；据此解释标准散度目标的出现并给出决策边界稳定性的几何基础；进一步将Neural Collapse与LeJEPA的几何识别为各向同性噪声下的最优星座；与经典ECOC对比，论证深度网络在特征空间中隐式学习有效码本。

Result: 框架统一解释了交叉熵–最大似然解码对应、标准散度目标的来源与决策边界稳定性；识别Neural Collapse与LeJEPA为各向同性噪声下最优解码星座；基于码冗余导出无损量化准则、改进的知识蒸馏方法及结构正则化准则；并推广至自监督编码器，对现代神经模型潜在表征几何给出自洽解释。

Conclusion: 深度分类与自监督表征几何可在纠错码解码的统一视角下理解，深度网络隐式习得特征空间码本，该视角兼具解释性与实用价值，可指导量化、蒸馏与正则化设计。

Abstract: A unified framework is proposed that characterizes supervised deep classification as the decoding of error-correcting codes in the Shannon sense. Cross-entropy optimization is shown to align softmax classifiers with maximum-likelihood decoding, where logits map to Hamming distances to class-specific codewords. This correspondence explains the emergence of standard divergence objectives (e.g., Kullback-Leibler, Jeffreys) and provides a geometric basis for the stability of decision boundaries. Furthermore, the geometries of Neural Collapse and LeJEPA are identified as optimal constellations for decoding under isotropic noise. Unlike classical Error-Correcting Output Codes, deep networks are shown to learn effective codebooks in the feature space implicitly. This insight is leveraged to derive novel criteria for lossless quantization, improved knowledge distillation, and architectural regularization based on code redundancy. Finally, this perspective is extended to self-supervised encoders, offering a cohesive explanation for the geometry of latent representations in modern neural models.

</details>


### [134] [ParetoM$^3$: Learning on the Pareto Set under Preference Guidance via Min-Max-Min Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18252)
*Pei Tang,Songtao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出罚式min-max-min（M³）统一建模偏好优化与弱Pareto最优性，并给出单循环一阶算法ParetoM³及非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中各目标本质冲突，实际只需从Pareto集中选出符合偏好的折中解；已有偏好引导方法难以在非凸情形下同时保证弱Pareto最优性与偏好最优性。

Method: 构造在弱Pareto集上取零的merit函数，将问题写成罚式min-max-min（M³）问题，把偏好优化与弱Pareto最优性的强制统一到单一目标；据此设计单循环一阶算法ParetoM³求M³问题的稳定点。

Result: 在局部Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz假设下证明：罚参数足够大时，ParetoM³可找到M³问题的ε-稳定点，且该点对原多目标问题近似弱Pareto最优，并给出显式非渐近收敛速率；同时证明该稳定性概念在既有假设下可恢复近似偏好稳定与近似KKT条件等最优性判据。合成基准、图像分类、回归与数学推理实验显示其能可靠获得偏好弱Pareto最优解且实际性能强。

Conclusion: M³为偏好引导多目标优化提供了统一且可分析的单目标刻画，ParetoM³以单循环一阶复杂度实现理论保证与实用效果兼具的偏好弱Pareto解计算。

Abstract: Learning across multiple inherently conflicting objectives requires selecting desirable trade-offs from the Pareto set. In this work, we study preference-guided multi-objective optimization, where a predefined preference function is minimized over the weak Pareto set of multiple nonconvex objectives. Leveraging a merit function that vanishes on the weak Pareto set, we propose a penalty-based min-max-min formulation, termed the M$^3$ problem, which unifies preference optimization and the enforcement of weak Pareto optimality in a single objective. We further develop ParetoM$^3$, a single-loop first-order algorithm for computing stationary solutions of the resulting M$^3$ problem. Under a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz assumption, we prove that ParetoM$^3$ finds an $ε$-stationary point of the M$^3$ problem that is also approximately weakly Pareto optimal for the original multi-objective problem when the penalty parameter is sufficiently large, with explicit non-asymptotic convergence rates. We also show that this stationarity notion recovers existing optimality criteria, including approximate preference stationarity and approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions, under the same assumptions used in prior works. Experiments on a synthetic benchmark, image classification, regression, and math reasoning demonstrate that ParetoM$^3$ reliably attains preferred weak Pareto optimal solutions while achieving strong practical performance. Code is available at https://github.com/tpgoodboy/ParetoM3.

</details>


### [135] [Right Token, Right Time: From Per-Token Gating to Trajectory-Aware Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18274)
*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Sagarika Suresh,Xiaotong Xu,Jithin Pradeep,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将SAE激活引导视为固定预算下的逐token分配问题，提出CFP（对比特征投影，无记忆）与CLABS（闭环自适应伯努利引导，轨迹感知）两种门控，在相同预算下实现更大行为偏移并更好保持流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE引导在每个生成token上都施加干预，使表示偏离模型训练分布，损害生成质量。论文提出"何时干预"（token选择）是决定性的控制维度，可在不牺牲质量的前提下提升行为控制效果。

Method: 把引导形式化为固定可引导token预算下的逐token分配问题，使各类方法仅在token选择上不同、分配成为唯一变量。提出两个门控：(1) CFP，无记忆，通过稀疏可解释特征投影选择与目标行为最不对齐的token；(2) CLABS，轨迹感知，将分配视为闭环反馈控制，跟踪生成已累积的对齐度，并把预算投向轨迹滞后的位置。在线性引导-响应模型上推导保证闭环稳定性的显式增益区间，并证明积分项降低稳态跟踪偏差。

Result: 在匹配预算下，CFP与CLABS在预算化门控中取得最大行为偏移；CLABS在预算最紧、分配最关键时表现最强，同时最能保持生成流畅性。

Conclusion: 干预时机是SAE引导的关键控制轴；将引导重构为预算约束下的token分配可统一比较不同方法，闭环反馈式分配（CLABS）在有限预算下兼顾控制强度与生成质量。

Abstract: Activation steering via sparse autoencoders (SAEs) enables behavioral control of large language models without task-specific fine-tuning. Yet, standard methods apply the steering signal at every generated token, displacing representations from the distribution the model was trained on and eroding generation quality. We show that \emph{when} along a generation the intervention is applied is a decisive axis of control. We cast steering as a per-token allocation problem under a fixed budget of steerable tokens, so that methods differ only in which tokens they select, making allocation the sole variable. Within this framework we introduce two gates. The first, Contrastive Feature Projection (CFP), is memoryless and selects the tokens least aligned with the target behavior through a sparse, interpretable feature projection. The second, Closed-Loop Adaptive Bernoulli Steering (CLABS), is trajectory aware. It treats allocation as closed-loop feedback control, tracking the alignment a generation has accumulated so far and spending budget where the trajectory falls behind. On a linear steering-response model we derive an explicit gain region that guarantees closed-loop stability of CLABS and show that its integral term reduces the steady-state tracking offset. Empirically, CFP and CLABS achieve the largest behavioral shift among budgeted gates at matched budgets, with CLABS strongest under the tight budgets where allocation matters most, while best preserving fluency.

</details>


### [136] [Clean Tickets, Wrong Orders: Catching Silent Errors with a Language Model in Your Browser Tab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18284)
*Umar Ahmed,Maxim Dunaev,Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov,Ivan Khodnenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示一个在浏览器内本地运行的0.8B参数微调模型Web演示：通过WebAssembly编译llama.cpp，实时将用户输入的订单文本解析为结构化格式，并用移植到TypeScript的PIZZA基准匹配器对每张订单打分，重点让访客寻找或触发"静默错误"（格式合法但并未被真实下单的错误输出）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型把订单文本转结构化时会失败于两类情形：一是输出破损文本，系统可直接拒绝；二是输出干净合法但并非用户真实下单内容的订单，作者称之为"silent error"，且认为后者更危险。为此需要一个可交互演示来暴露并研究这类静默错误，同时避免订单文本离开用户设备以保证隐私。

Method: 将llama.cpp 编译为WebAssembly，使微调的0.8B模型完全在浏览器标签页内推理；页面在访客输入时即时解析订单；把PIZZA基准自带的匹配器移植为TypeScript，用于对每张订单评分；对菜单外内容以"?"拒绝，使模型面对开放输入；并提供交互界面让访客寻找静默错误或尝试制造静默错误。

Result: 在浏览器中对全部1,357条PIZZA测试订单运行，部署模型有99.0%的输出与服务器端评测一致，达到相同的精确匹配率0.882；同时报告了在3台消费级笔记本上的延迟表现。

Conclusion: 浏览器端WASM推理可与服务器端评测结果高度一致（输出一致率99.0%，精确匹配0.882），证明设备端私有化部署的可行性，并为公众提供一个直观探查模型静默错误的交互平台。

Abstract: A small language model that turns a typed order into structure can fail in two ways. It can print something broken, which any system rejects. Or it can print a clean, well formed order that nobody placed. Our companion work calls the second kind a silent error, and it is the dangerous one. We present a web demo in which a fine-tuned 0.8B parameter model runs entirely inside the visitor's browser tab, through llama.cpp compiled to WebAssembly. No order text leaves the device. The page parses orders as the visitor types, scores every ticket with the PIZZA benchmark's own matcher ported to TypeScript, refuses anything off the menu with "?" so the model can face the open web, and lets visitors hunt for silent errors or try to cause one. Run in the browser on all 1,357 PIZZA test orders, the deployed model writes the same output as the server evaluation for 99.0% of them and reaches the same exact match (0.882); we also report its latency on 3 consumer laptops. Demo: https://wrong.cuyor.com

</details>


### [137] [Emulating physics-based XCO2 retrievals across OCO-2 viewing modes with compact neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18333)
*Xianlei San,Chengxing Zhai,Hui Su,Kuai Dai,Shuangchen Du*

Main category: cs.LG
