TL;DR: 该文用SVD压缩OCO-2光谱、以小型多层感知机学习"全物理反演值与先验值之差"，实现跨多年、多观测模式的CO2柱平均干空气摩尔分数（XCO2）高效业务化仿真反演，精度约0.9–1.4 ppm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于卫星光谱的全物理反演XCO2计算代价高昂，难以满足大规模、业务化处理需求；需要一种在保持精度的前提下显著降低计算成本的高效替代方案，并能跨观测模式与年份稳定泛化。

Method: 对OCO-2光谱分波段做奇异值分解（SVD）压缩，取光谱得分（spectral scores）作为特征；用紧凑多层感知机（MLP），结合先验信息与观测几何，预测"全物理反演值减业务先验值"的残差Δ；将预测Δ加回先验得到XCO2估计。模型用2015–2019年观测训练，2020年观测验证。

Result: 在2021–2025年测试中，相对ACOS业务反演产品的年平均RMSE为：海洋耀斑0.89 ppm、陆地耀斑1.35 ppm、天底1.43 ppm。对东亚天底观测采用已发表的光谱Transformer筛选准则时，本模型2020–2022年平均误差低于该Transformer模型的报道值。

Conclusion: 紧凑神经网络结合SVD压缩光谱即可在多观测模式、多年份上有效仿真全物理反演，兼顾精度与计算效率，具备业务化替代潜力；同时表明神经网络可在特定区域/筛选条件下优于Transformer类模型。

Abstract: Operational full-physics retrieval of the column-averaged dry-air mole fraction of carbon dioxide from satellite spectra is computationally demanding. We use singular value decomposition to compress spectra from the Orbiting Carbon Observatory-2 separately by band. Compact multilayer perceptrons use the resulting spectral scores, prior information, and viewing geometry to predict the difference between the full-physics retrieval and its operational prior. Adding the predicted difference to the prior yields an estimate of the retrieved mole fraction. The models were trained on 2015-2019 observations and validated on 2020 observations. In 2021-2025 tests, the mean annual root-mean-square errors relative to the Atmospheric Carbon Dioxide Observations from Space retrieval product were 0.89, 1.35, and 1.43 parts per million for ocean glint, land glint, and nadir observations, respectively. On East Asian nadir observations screened using the published criteria for the spectral Transformer, our model yielded a lower mean annual error than reported for that model in 2020-2022. These results show that compact neural networks using compressed spectra can efficiently emulate the full-physics retrieval across multiple viewing modes and years.

</details>


### [138] [DECODE: Decomposable Environments for COntrolled Sequential-Decision Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18367)
*Diego Silva Prado,Bruno Caldas de Souza,Tiago Amaral Penteado de Jesus,Ricardo Guimarães Herrmann,Cristiano Breuel,Cristiano Bevitori Maffia De Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECODE 是一个用于在规划与可用信息层面开展受控实验、检验决策系统改进假设的基准。其消费者催收序列决策生成器提供五个部分可观测环境，共享已知动态矩阵，仅变化记忆参数与过程噪声。实验在固定求解器其余部分的前提下改变特定输入或规划规则。确定性基础环境上，规划期从1个月延长到6个月使两个梯度提升求解器得分提升15–17个百分点（相对精确最优）；即在6个月规划下，最佳求解器仍低于最优15.6个百分点。按指定干预序列，三项状态输入改动使其得分下降6.3点，随后以真实响应模型替换拟合模型则提升15.6点，净恢复9点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列决策系统未达最优时，其得分本身无法揭示哪些修改能改善决策，缺少可归因、可控的诊断手段。

Method: 构建消费信贷催收生成器，产出五个部分可观测环境（已知动态矩阵，变化记忆参数与过程噪声）；开展受控实验，每次比较明确指定求解器的状态输入、响应输入与规划期，单独改变输入或规划规则；以含隐藏状态与借款人特征的精确最优为满分基准，并用指定干预序列（状态输入改动、以真实响应模型替换拟合模型）逐项检验。

Result: 确定性基础环境中六个月的规划使两个梯度提升求解器得分提高15–17个百分点；最佳求解器在6个月规划下仍落后最优15.6点。指定干预序列中三项状态输入及搜索期评估改动导致−6.3点，替换为真实响应模型后+15.6点，净恢复9点。

Conclusion: 在已知动态与显式信息条件下，DECODE 使关于求解器性能的假设可通过指定干预加以检验；但中间效应依赖于响应模型与改动的顺序，故不能唯一地把性能差距归因于各自独立的成因。

Abstract: When a sequential decision system falls short of an optimum, its score does not reveal which modifications would improve its decisions. DECODE is a benchmark for testing such modifications through controlled experiments on planning and available information. Its consumer-credit collections generator provides five partially observed environments that share known dynamics matrices and vary a memory parameter and process noise. The experiments change selected inputs or planning rules while keeping the rest of the solver fixed. Each comparison specifies the solver's state inputs, response inputs and planning horizon. On the deterministic base environment, which was selected for a large gap between six-month and one-month planning with the hidden state, extending planning from one to six months increases the scores of two gradient-boosted solvers by 15 to 17 percentage points of the exact optimum, computed with the hidden state and borrower traits. Even at this horizon, the best graded solver, whose repayment response is fitted on logs, remains 15.6 points below that optimum. In a specified intervention sequence, three changes to its state inputs and their evaluation during search lower its score by 6.3 points, and replacing its fitted repayment model with the true response, evaluated with hidden borrower traits, then raises it by 15.6, a net recovery of nine of those points. Because the intermediate effects are conditional on the repayment model and the order of the changes, they do not uniquely assign the gap to separate causes. Within these limits, DECODE makes hypotheses about solver performance testable through specified interventions under known dynamics and explicit information conditions.

</details>


### [139] [f-Divergence Self-Play for Tabular Anomaly Detection via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18370)
*Hoang Tran Vuong,Linh Ngo Van,Dang Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Phuoc Nguyen,Mehrtash Harandi,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自博弈微调框架 DiSPaT，通过 f-散度最小化视角与"策略生成伪异常—判别器区分—策略更新"的交替优化，强化 LLM 对表格数据正常性的刻画，在多种基准上优于现有 LLM、深度学习与经典无监督检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据特征类型异构（数值、类别、文本混合），难以学习有效的正常性表示；现有将行序列化为文本并依赖 LLM 有监督微调的方法仅为一次性训练，提供的信号有限，无法充分收紧模型对正常分布的描述。

Method: DiSPaT 采用自博弈式微调：以 f-散度最小化为理论基础，推导出训练目标与"缩小真实正常数据与模型生成样本间分布差距"之间的紧致近似；训练采用交替优化——每轮迭代中当前策略生成合成样本作为伪异常，评论家判别器学习区分伪异常与真实正常样本，该判别信号驱动策略更新，使模型分布逐步对齐真实正常数据分布。

Result: 在多种多样化基准上的大量实验表明，DiSPaT 在表格异常检测任务上稳定优于先前的基于 LLM 的方法、深度学习方法以及经典无监督检测器。

Conclusion: 用自博弈对抗式微调替代一次性监督微调，可为 LLM 提供持续且更紧致的正常性学习信号，是提升异构表格异常检测性能的有效范式。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data poses significant challenges due to heterogeneous feature types-mixing numerical, categorical, and textual attributes, which complicate learning meaningful representations of normality. Recent work has applied large language models (LLMs) to this problem by serializing table rows as text sequences, yet these approaches rely on one-shot supervised fine-tuning that offers limited signal to tighten the model's description of normality. We propose DiSPaT, a self-play fine-tuning framework that strengthens the model's understanding of normal data. Building on the theoretical foundation of f-divergence minimization, we derive a tight approximation connecting our training objective to reducing the distributional gap between real normal data and model-generated samples. DiSPaT operates through an alternating optimization: at each iteration, the current policy generates synthetic samples that serve as pseudo-anomalies, while a critic discriminator learns to distinguish these from real normal samples; this signal drives policy updates that progressively align the model distribution with the true normal-data distribution. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DiSPaT consistently outperforms prior LLM-based methods, deep learning approaches, and classical unsupervised detectors for tabular anomaly detection.

</details>


### [140] [Where Tree Ensembles Decide: Structural Local Explanation with Decision Predicate Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18382)
*Sylvio Barbon Junior,Luca Longo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部决策谓词图（local DPG），把树集成对单样本执行的规则汇成可验证的计算记录，并从中选出"关键谓词"（critical predicate）作为可解释的阈值级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征归因与反事实解释未说明预测响应哪些树阈值，结构解释又会压缩规则、丢失原始树；因此需要一个能定位具体阈值并保留对应树的局部解释。

Method: 构建 local DPG：聚合样本所执行规则形成可验证记录，附带复现预测的类支持；据此选取关键谓词——估计越过该谓词最能将模型推向目标类的已执行谓词，并给出共享该阈值的树，无需外部解释器。

Result: 在16个数据集、4类集成模型上，区分不同预测邻居优于随机与最近阈值谓词；单次跨越在46–69%的错误预测中恢复真实类，而TreeSHAP首位特征同规则仅29–50%；植入故障实验中以46–71%定位错误阈值（随机6–21%）。但对谓词的免训练编辑未降低未见数据误差。

Conclusion: 关键谓词不替代归因与反事实，而是补充指出预测可在哪个阈值处被改变；可作为无训练的解释工具。

Abstract: Tree ensembles are among the most accurate models for tabular data, yet the computation behind a single prediction is spread over hundreds of decision rules. Feature attributions and counterfactuals explain such predictions through the influence of features or a change of the input, leaving open which of the thresholds applied by the trees a prediction responds to, while structural explanations compress these rules and lose the trees behind them. To address this gap, we propose a local Decision Predicate Graph (local DPG), which gathers the rules executed for a sample into a verifiable record of the computation, with a class support that reproduces every explained prediction. From these rules we select the critical predicate, the executed predicate whose crossing is estimated to move the model most strongly toward a chosen class, which offers the user a concrete threshold and the trees that share it, without an external explainer. Across 16 datasets and four ensemble families, it separates differently predicted neighbours better than random and nearest-threshold predicates. A single crossing recovers the true class in 46-69% of wrong predictions, against 29-50% when the same rule is applied to the feature ranked first by TreeSHAP, a measure close to the selection criterion, and on planted faults it finds the faulty threshold in 46-71% of the resulting errors, against 6-21% at random. Although training-free edits of these predicates did not reduce the error on unseen data, the critical predicate complements attributions and counterfactuals by naming where a prediction can be changed.

</details>


### [141] [WeatherDreamer: Learning a World Model from Multimodal Earth Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18393)
*Kate Duffy,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Thomas J. Vandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherDreamer 是一个端到端学习"观测—隐状态—预报"递归同化循环的全球天气世界模型，直接从18类观测系统学习小时级潜状态更新，无需再分析数据。其在12小时内的站点系综预报优于GEFS且校准性更好，短临推断降水、云顶高度和辐照的技能可比或超过业务产品，单GPU约1.5分钟生成逐小时全球分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习天气模型虽已接近业务全球预报水平，但仍依赖独立且昂贵的数据同化（DA）系统生成的分析场（全球模型通常每6小时更新一次）。现有"观测到观测"模型虽摆脱这一依赖，却大多缺少DA的核心递归滤波循环——即利用背景状态在稀疏、不规则观测间传递信息的能力。

Method: 提出 WeatherDreamer 世界模型，端到端学习递归同化循环：从18个全球观测系统推断潜状态，用学习到的动力学逐小时前推，并每小时用新观测进行校正，同时可预测任一观测系统在任意位置应测得的量。训练不使用再分析数据。模型中学习到的更新步骤在未给定观测误差的情况下，隐含估计误差量级；并通过解耦观测几何与大气状态实现临边观测处理。

Result: 在留出站点，其系综在温度、露点、风场上12小时内优于NOAA GEFS，且校准性优于GEFS与ECMWF系综；降水、云顶高度、太阳辐照（推理时不作为输入）短时效技能匹配或超过业务产品。相对NOAA GSI同化系统初猜场，地面站短程偏差在10%以内，微波湿度敏感通道在25%以内，但温度探测通道偏差可达5倍。学习到的观测误差接近微波探测仪与散射计风的已知仪器与反演误差；恢复83–96%的临边暗淡效应并生成无缝星下点影像。单GPU上约1.5分钟产出逐小时全球分析。

Conclusion: 同化与预报可以完全由观测数据联合学习，绕开独立的DA系统与再分析依赖；递归潜状态滤波循环是实现小时级全球分析与观测约束预报的关键，但温度探测通道的偏差表明辐射传输/温度反演约束仍是待改进环节。

Abstract: Machine learning weather models now rival operational global forecasts but still start from analyses produced by separate, expensive data assimilation (DA) systems, which for global models are typically updated every six hours. Observation-to-observation models avoid this dependence, but most omit the recursive filtering cycle that lets DA systems carry a background state through sparse, irregular observations. We introduce WeatherDreamer, a world model that learns this cycle end-to-end from 18 global observing systems, without reanalysis. It infers a latent state from observations, carries it forward hour by hour with learned dynamics, corrects it each hour with new observations, and can predict what any of its observing systems would measure at any location. At held-out stations, its ensembles beat NOAA's Global Ensemble Forecast System (GEFS) for temperature, dew point and wind out to 12 h and are better calibrated than GEFS and the ECMWF ensemble. Precipitation, cloud-top height and solar irradiance, never inputs at inference, are inferred with skill matching or exceeding operational products at short leads. Against the first guess of NOAA's Gridpoint Statistical Interpolation (GSI) DA system, WeatherDreamer's short-range departures are within 10% of GSI's at surface stations and within 25% for moisture-sensitive microwave channels, but up to five times larger for temperature-sounding channels. Never given observation errors, its learned update assumes errors close to known instrument and retrieval errors of microwave sounders and scatterometer winds. WeatherDreamer also separates viewing geometry from atmospheric state, recovering 83-96% of observed limb darkening and producing seamless nadir-view imagery. Producing an hourly global analysis in about 1.5 minutes on one GPU, WeatherDreamer shows that assimilation and forecasting can be learned together directly from observations.

</details>


### [142] [Deep Neural Networks Training Efficiency: Does Grokking Modify its Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18396)
*Eduardo Cueto-Mendoza,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 grokking（过拟合后延迟出现的分布外泛化）是否改变深度网络的训练效率（以每单位能量的准确率衡量），结论是在所考察的设置中 grokking 并未实质改变训练效率的动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking 表现为训练-测试误差长期分化后测试准确率突然跃升，但尚不清楚这一泛化"突变"是否伴随能量/效率层面的变化，即是否存在效率的非平滑或局部提升。

Method: 采用模算术与 MNIST 两个典型任务，两种神经网络模型，在 16/32/64 位精度下训练；使用 Orthograd 施加不同强度的正交化以调控是否诱发 grokking；设置短程（≤500 epoch）与长程（≤20,000 epoch）两类实验，观测测试准确率与"准确率/单位能量"的效率曲线。以"持续训练-测试差距后测试准确率急剧上升"作为 grokking 判据。

Result: 短程实验中，逐步增强早期训练阶段的 Orthograd 强度并未诱发 grokking，效率平稳下降；长程实验中 Orthograd 成功诱发 grokking，但效率曲线仍近似单调，在 grokking 转变处未见可察觉的局部上升。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，grokking 不实质改变整体训练效率动态；泛化的延迟涌现不必然对应能量效率层面的可观测转变，提示泛化相变与效率相变可能解耦。

Abstract: We study whether grokking, the delayed emergence of out-of-distribution generalization after apparent overfitting, alters training efficiency in deep neural networks, where efficiency is measured as accuracy per unit energy. Using two canonical settings, modular arithmetic and MNIST, we analyze two neural network models under 16-, 32-, and 64-bit precision and use Orthograd to test whether grokking produces noticeable deviations from the typically decreasing dynamics of training efficiency. We consider both short training runs of up to 500 epochs and long training runs of up to 20,000 epochs, and in both cases identify grokking as a sharp improvement in test accuracy after a sustained train-test gap. In the short-horizon experiments, applying Orthograd at progressively stronger levels during early training does not induce grokking, and efficiency remains smoothly decreasing. In the long-horizon experiments, Orthograd induces grokking, but the efficiency curves remain approximately monotonic and show no appreciable local increase at the grokking transition. These results suggest that, in our setting, grokking does not materially alter overall training-efficiency dynamics.

</details>


### [143] [Fuse-PINN: Combining Finite Element Methods and Physics-Informed Neural Networks to enable massively parallel simulations of non-linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18409)
*Stanislas Pamela,Nicolas Pailliez,Emmanuel Franck,Victor Michel-Dansac,Laurent Navoret,Vignesh Gopakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作总结了 FEM-NN 形式的物理信息神经网络及其模块化框架 Fuse-PINN。该框架支持 GPU HPC 上的可扩展并行化，并采用能量自然梯度下降优化器实现高精度收敛；实验表明，在多 GPU 大规模非线性对流模型上实现了近完美的扩展性，反驳了“PINN 不可扩展”的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PINN 方法难以扩展到 HPC 架构，多数研究局限于变量少、方程简单的玩具模型，很少涉及大空间尺度、长时间演化的复杂非线性动力学，因此 AI 界部分观点认为“PINN 不可扩展”。作者旨在证明该观点不成立。

Method: 提出数学上严谨的有限元法神经网络（FEM-NN）公式，并据此构建模块化框架 Fuse-PINN，实现 PINN 在 GPU HPC 系统上的可扩展并行化；同时引入能量自然梯度下降（Energy Natural Gradient Descent）优化器以保证高精度收敛。

Result: 在磁约束聚变相关的物理模型（含细尺度动力学的非线性对流模型）上验证了 Fuse-PINN；随问题规模增大，多 GPU 运行实现了近完美的扩展效率。

Conclusion: FEM-NN 表述为 PINN 在 HPC 上的高效并行提供了可行基础，说明 PINN 能够扩展到大规模非线性物理问题，从而否定了“PINN 不可扩展”的普遍看法。

Abstract: Research in the domain of Physics-Informed Neural Networks applied to PDE modelling has advanced significantly in recent years, providing a wide ecosystem of novel methods. However, one frequent limitation is the inability of PINNs to scale out on High-Performance Computing architectures to address more ambitious challenges, particularly non-linear physics models with turbulent dynamics in large spatial domains. Typical studies restrain demonstrations to toy models with simple physics equations, few variables, and rarely any complex non-linear dynamics evolved over long timescales. Part of the AI community has even adopted the idea that "PINNs don't scale". This work proves otherwise. We present a mathematically robust formulation of Finite-Element-Method for Neural Networks (FEM-NN) implemented into a comprehensive, modular framework: Fuse-PINN. This formulation provides the basis for a sclable parallelisation of Physics-Informed Neural Networks on GPU-based HPC systems. Furthermore, an Energy Natural Gradient Descent optimiser is implemented to allow high-precision convergence. Fuse-PINN is demonstrated on physics models relevant to Magnetic Confinement Fusion, including non-linear convective models with fine-scale dynamics. Near-perfect scaling is achieved on multi-GPU runs with increasing problem size.

</details>


### [144] [PACT: A Decoder-Confidence-Guided Two-Round Receiver for Joint Channel and Phase-Noise Estimation in Sub-THz OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18450)
*Ruilin Wang,Xiaodai Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种由译码器置信度引导的两轮迭代接收机，结合复数Transformer信道/相位噪声联合估计、带状LMMSE均衡与神经归一化最小和译码，在子太赫兹编码OFDM中显著降低误块率并具备跨配置泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子太赫兹频段的编码OFDM接收同时受到频率选择性衰落与振荡器相位噪声（PN）的联合损伤，现有基于学习的接收方案难以同时处理二者的耦合影响，且缺乏跨信道/PN模型与多配置的泛化验证。

Method: 1）PACT（导频感知复数Transformer）估计器：通过相互作用的复数域分支，联合估计频域信道与等效时域相位噪声；2）带状线性最小均方误差（LMMSE）均衡；3）神经归一化最小和（neural normalized min-sum）译码；4）两轮接收结构：利用译码器生成的伪导频与连续星座感知置信度，通过PACT的输入与注意力机制引导第二轮精化，两轮共享参数。

Result: 在 Eb/N0=30 dB 时，两种基准设置下PACT接收机的码块错误率分别比最优的基于学习基线低约70%与57%；冻结权重测试进一步验证其在信道/PN模型、载波频率以及FFT/带宽配置间均具适用性。

Conclusion: 将译码器置信度反馈与复数域联合信道-PN估计深度融合的两轮接收架构能有效抑制子太赫兹OFDM的复合损伤，并在参数冻结条件下保持对多种信道、频点与系统配置的鲁棒泛化。

Abstract: Frequency-selective fading and oscillator phase noise (PN) jointly impair coded sub-THz OFDM reception. We propose a decoder-confidence-guided two-round receiver combining a {pilot-aware complex transformer} (PACT) estimator, banded linear minimum mean-square error equalization, and neural normalized min-sum decoding. PACT jointly estimates the frequency-domain channel and effective time-domain PN through interacting complex-valued branches. Decoder-derived pseudo-pilots and continuous constellation-aware confidence guide second-round refinement through PACT inputs and attention, with parameters shared across rounds. At $E_b/N_0=30$~dB, the PACT receiver achieves approximately $70\%$ and $57\%$ lower code-block error rates than the best-performing learning-based baseline in the two benchmark settings, respectively. Frozen-weight tests further demonstrate applicability across channel/PN models, carrier frequencies, and FFT/bandwidth configurations.

</details>


### [145] [Black-Box Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models via Ensemble-of-Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18453)
*Wang Ma,Debarun Bhattacharjya,Junkyu Lee,Nhan H Pham,Harsha Kokel,Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两级黑盒集成方法，仅需采样访问即可将LLM不确定性分解为偶然不确定性与认知不确定性，并给出三项理论支撑，在多基准上媲美白盒基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠使用LLM需要区分提示本身固有的歧义与真正的知识缺口。贝叶斯/深度集成可做偶然/认知（AU/EU）分解，但在LLM规模上计算代价过高且需白盒访问；现有黑盒方法只给出单一标量，无法区分二者。

Method: 两级集成：内层为固定提示的随机解码，外层为保持语义的提示扰动。在连续嵌入空间中度量变异性，利用全协方差定律给出精确的组内/组间扰动协方差分解以估计AU/EU，仅需黑盒采样。理论层面：(i) 嵌套蒙特卡洛的有限样本偏差恒等式及闭式偏差校正EU估计量；(ii) 连接扰动总体AU/EU与贝叶斯目标的界；(iii) 分离性结果，指出仅依赖一致性的黑盒方法必然漏检、而扰动基EU可检出的失效模式类。

Result: 在5个短问答基准、4个指令微调LLM（2个家族、2个规模）上，与强白盒基线具竞争力；在同等生成预算下，错误排序上与领先黑盒方法相当，且可分解AU/EU。受控配对干预显示AU与EU分别选择性响应提示歧义与模型知识缺口。NQ长答案划分的长文本试点表明框架可扩展至声明级事实性且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 两级扰动集成提供了可计算、黑盒可用的AU/EU分解，兼顾理论保证与实证效果，并可推广到长文本事实性评估。

Abstract: Reliable use of large language models (LLMs) requires uncertainty estimates that separate ambiguity intrinsic to a prompt from genuine knowledge gaps. Bayesian and deep-ensemble methods deliver this aleatoric/epistemic decomposition in principle, but are computationally prohibitive at LLM scale and require white-box access. Existing black-box methods, in contrast, summarize to a single scalar that cannot make the distinction. We introduce a two-level ensemble that bridges this gap. Stochastic decoding of a fixed prompt forms the inner ensemble; meaning-preserving semantic perturbations of the prompt form the outer ensemble. Variability is measured in a continuous embedding space, and the law of total covariance gives an exact within-/between-perturbation decomposition to estimate aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty (AU/EU); it requires only black-box sampling access. On top of this estimator, we contribute three theoretical results that ground the framework: (i) a finite-sample bias identity for nested Monte Carlo with a closed-form bias-corrected epistemic estimator; (ii) a Bayesian bridge bounding the gap between perturbation-population AU/EU and Bayesian targets; and (iii) a separation result identifying a class of failure modes that any consistency-only black-box method provably misses while perturbation-based EU detects. Across five short QA benchmarks and four instruction-tuned LLMs spanning two families and two scales, our estimator is competitive with strong white-box baselines, and at a matched generation budget it matches leading black-box methods on error ranking while decomposing AU and EU; controlled paired interventions show that AU and EU respond selectively to distinct, actionable sources of failure: prompt ambiguity and model knowledge gaps; and a long-form pilot on the NQ long-answer split shows the framework extends to claim-level factuality without retraining.

</details>


### [146] [Sample-efficient prediction of high-entropy alloy strength through outcome-aware robust sequential learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18462)
*Ahmed Shoyeb Raihan,Md Habibor Rahman,Ashish Nimbarte,Imtiaz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ROSE-HEA主动学习框架，以"结果感知+最坏情形预测风险"的采集策略，在有限拉伸试验预算下高效预测高熵合金抗拉强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金拉伸试验数据获取成本高、实验难度大，需在有限实验预算下尽可能准确地由成分与工艺条件预测抗拉强度（UTS）。

Method: 提出ROSE-HEA（Robust Outcome-aware Sequential Experimentation for HEA）：采集策略先预估潜在UTS结果如何更新预测及互补高斯过程（GP）描述的支持度，再在参考域上重新评估剩余的最坏情形期望预测风险，据此选择信息量最大的候选。基于501条HEA记录开展回溯评估，与EI、UCB贝叶斯优化、随机GP采样及8种常规回归器对比，30次匹配运行共享初始观测与测试条件。

Result: 从25个初始观测出发、追加100个响应后，ROSE-HEA取得最低平均RMSE（152.84 MPa）与最高平均R²（0.7443）；相对EI、UCB平均RMSE分别降低6.16%和6.37%。所选观测在6类回归器家族上均提升预测并带来更广的设计空间覆盖；仅用125个观测即达到最强常规回归器用185个观测的平均预测性能，训练观测减少32.4%。

Conclusion: 结果感知的鲁棒序贯学习能在受限训练预算下有效改进高熵合金UTS预测，具备指导成分与工艺决策的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of high-entropy alloy (HEA) strength can guide composition and processing decisions; however, obtaining tensile-test data for HEAs can be costly and experimentally demanding. This study proposes Robust Outcome-aware Sequential Experimentation for High-Entropy Alloys (ROSE-HEA), a sample-efficient framework for predicting ultimate tensile strength (UTS) from alloy composition and processing conditions. Its acquisition policy anticipates how possible UTS outcomes update predictions and the support for complementary Gaussian process (GP) descriptions. It then reassesses the worst-case expected predictive risk remaining across a reference domain to select informative candidates. A retrospective evaluation on 501 HEA records compares ROSE-HEA with expected-improvement (EI) and upper-confidence-bound (UCB) Bayesian optimization (BO), random GP sampling, and eight conventional regressors across 30 matched runs with shared initial observations and test conditions. Starting from 25 initial observations and acquiring 100 additional responses, ROSE-HEA achieves the lowest mean root-mean-square error (RMSE), at 152.84 MPa, and the highest mean R^2, at 0.7443. Mean RMSE is reduced by 6.16% and 6.37% relative to EI and UCB, respectively. The selected observations improve prediction across six regressor families and provide broader design-space coverage. ROSE-HEA also surpasses the strongest conventional regressor's mean predictive performance using 125 rather than 185 observations, corresponding to 32.4% fewer training observations. These findings demonstrate the potential of outcome-aware robust sequential learning to improve UTS prediction in HEAs under constrained training budgets.

</details>


### [147] [Asymptotic Margins and Feature Confinement in Conic Classification Heads under Softmax Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18507)
*Omer Nezih Gerek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对"锥形分类头在有界类区域下训练时特征是否有界"的问题，作者证明类区域由旋转二次曲面界定，并引入可由二阶锥规划计算的渐近间隔：间隔为正时损失下确界为零且仅在特征发散处趋于零，间隔为负时损失强制有界、极小点落在显式球内。结论是softmax下正间隔与有界区域可共存，故有界区域并不约束特征；实验在CIFAR-10/100上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥函数分类头为每类赋予特征空间中的有界区域，常用于拒识未见类样本。但作者关心一个此前未澄清的问题：当该头用softmax交叉熵训练时，这些有界区域是否也能把特征本身约束在有界范围内。由于softmax只依赖类分数之差，这一直觉值得严格检验。

Method: 考虑类分数为线性项与欧氏范数组合的头：(1) 证明每类区域被一个旋转二次曲面界定，其离心率为某个学习比值的倒数；(2) 为每类定义渐近间隔，通过比较该类分数与其他所有分数的增长阶来刻画，可用二阶锥规划求解；(3) 建立间隔符号与损失性质（下确界是否为零、是否强制、极小点位置）之间的等价关系；(4) 将结论推广到其他范数，包括多面体锥函数。

Result: 损失对某类下确界为零当且仅当该类间隔为正，且此时损失仅沿发散特征趋于零；间隔为负时损失是强制的，极小点位于一个显式球内。理论上指出：由于softmax只依赖分数差，区域有界的类仍可能有正间隔，因此有界区域并不能约束特征。实验上，CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100上67个已训练锥形头的全部1210个单元均具有正间隔；而原型头按理论预测仅10个单元中9个有界。

Conclusion: 锥形头提供的有界类区域不等同于对特征的有界约束；在softmax交叉熵下，渐近间隔才是决定损失行为与特征是否有界的关键量。经验结果与理论完全一致，提醒不应把类区域有界性误当作特征范数的正则化机制。

Abstract: Classification heads built on conic functions give each class a bounded region of the feature space, and such regions are used to reject samples of unseen classes. We ask whether these regions also keep the features bounded when the head is trained with softmax cross-entropy. For a head whose class scores combine a linear term with a Euclidean norm, we first show that the region of each class is bounded by a quadric of revolution, whose eccentricity is the reciprocal of one learned ratio. We then attach to each class an asymptotic margin, which compares the growth of its score with the growth of all the other scores and can be computed by a second-order cone program. The loss of a class has infimum zero exactly when its margin is positive, and it then approaches zero only along diverging features. When the margin is negative, the loss is coercive and its minimizers lie in an explicit ball. Since softmax depends only on the differences between scores, a class with a bounded region can still have a positive margin, so bounded regions do not confine the features. The result extends to other norms, including the polyhedral conic functions. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, all 1210 cells of 67 trained conic heads have positive margins, whereas a prototype head keeps nine of its ten cells bounded, as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [148] [On numerical issues of divergences between nearly equal distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18511)
*Brandon Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出分类分布间常用散度运算（前向/反向KL、广义JSD、蒙特卡洛KL估计与熵）存在与softmax/soft交叉熵类似的数值问题，即分布接近相等时舍入与抵消误差影响前向计算和梯度，并给出数值稳定重写与示例实现，经性质测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barron et al. (2026) 已在 softmax 与 soft cross-entropy 中发现数值问题，作者推测同类问题也存在于散度运算中；而散度广泛用于知识蒸馏、强化学习、近端方法等优化目标，数值误差会直接影响训练稳定性与结果可靠性，因此需要系统识别并修复。

Method: 分析分类分布之间前向与反向 KL 散度、广义 Jensen-Shannon 散度、蒙特卡洛 KL 估计及熵的计算形式，定位分布近似相等时的舍入误差与灾难性抵消来源；针对各使用场景提出数值稳定的等价重写公式，并给出对应的示例实现，采用基于性质的测试（property-based tests）进行验证。

Result: 证实在上述散度运算中确实存在与前人报道相似的数值问题，且当两个分布接近相等时，前向数值与梯度计算均受舍入与抵消影响；所提出的稳定重写与实现通过了性质测试验证，能够缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 散度运算的数值稳定性问题具有普遍性，不应仅关注 softmax 与交叉熵；采用数学等价但数值稳定的重写是低成本且有效的解决方案，建议在知识蒸馏、强化学习与近端方法等依赖散度的优化流程中替换为标准实现。

Abstract: Barron et al. (2026) recently identified numerical issues in the softmax and soft cross-entropy loss. We show that similar issues are present in standard divergence operations between categorical distributions. This includes the forward and reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and generalized Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), as well as Monte Carlo KL estimates and entropy. When the distributions are nearly equal, rounding and cancellation issues affect the forward and gradient computations. These issues can affect settings using divergences in optimization objectives, such as knowledge distillation, reinforcement learning, and proximal methods. We propose numerically stable reformulations and provide example implementations for each setting, validated by property-based tests.

</details>


### [149] [WeightBench: Evaluating Language Models as Weight-Space Programmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18553)
*Aayush Marasini,Barsat Khadka,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 WEIGHTBENCH，一个评估大语言模型直接读写、修改与生成神经网络参数的基准，涵盖 42 个任务、五个赛道，网络规模从 2K 参数控制策略到 7B 语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明 LLM 可在无需梯度下降的情况下直接操作其他网络的参数（即 Weight-Space Programming, WSP），但缺乏系统性评估手段，因此需要一个稳健的基准来量化不同能力层级模型在该范式下的表现。

Method: 构建 WEIGHTBENCH 基准：42 个 WSP 任务，划分为五个赛道——读取权重、修复权重、写入权重、识别不可行的编辑、无数据压缩；被测网络从 2K 参数的控制策略到 7B 参数的语言模型；配套任务注册表、OS 级沙箱执行环境和在线排行榜。

Result: 前沿智能体已能读取小型网络并在不运行网络的情况下写出可用的控制策略，但尚不能修补语言模型、无数据压缩模型，或区分"不可行编辑"与"困难编辑"。42 个任务上 Claude Opus 5.5 宏平均得分 0.535（Sonnet 5.5 0.466，Haiku 5.5 0.402，DeepSeek v4-flash 0.215）；在 GPT-6.1-sol 参与的 38 个任务上其得分为 0.568，Opus 为 0.546。

Conclusion: WSP 是一个可行但能力边界清晰的新范式：小规模网络的读取与生成已初步实现，而大模型修补、无数据压缩和编辑可行性判断仍是开放难题；WEIGHTBENCH 提供了可复现的标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Recent work shows that large language models (LLMs) can operate directly on the parameters of other neural networks. They can read what a network was trained to do, fix a network that fails, and write working networks from scratch, all without gradient descent. This capability has been termed Weight-Space Programming (WSP). Exploring this new paradigm systematically requires a robust benchmark. We introduce WEIGHTBENCH, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs of increasing capability on 42 WSP tasks across five tracks: reading, fixing and writing weights, recognizing edits that are impossible, and data-free compression. The networks whose weights the LLMs read and edit range from 2K-parameter control policies to 7B-parameter language models. Experimental benchmark results show that frontier agents can already read small networks and write working control policies without ever running them, but cannot yet patch language models, compress them without data, or tell an impossible edit from a hard one. On all 42 tasks, Claude Opus 5.5 reaches a macro score of 0.535 (Sonnet 5.5 0.466, Haiku 5.5 0.402, DeepSeek v4-flash 0.215); on the 38 tasks it was run on, GPT-6.1-sol reaches 0.568 against Opus's 0.546. Code, the task registry, our OS-level sandbox harness, and the live leaderboard are publicly available at https://github.com/Aayush-Marasini/WeightBench.

</details>


### [150] [Pairing AIS with RF: Analysis of a Markovian Approach for Maritime Data Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18563)
*Daniel Grimm,Kyra Jongman,Renato Cordeiro Ferreira,Giuseppe Cascavilla,Alfredo Cuzzocrea,Willem-Jan Van Den Heuvel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究沿用CRISP-DM流程，用Pair Hidden Markov Model（PHMM）将单次星载RF探测与AIS轨迹对齐，在NOAA AIS数据与合成RF探测上评估，并与最近邻基线比较。长度校正的Forward分数在多候选探测验证集上top-1精确率为0.7776、暗船检测AUC为0.637；对数几率（log-odds）分数将精确率与AUC分别提升至0.8580与0.852；但时间加权最近邻基线（精确率0.9013、AUC 0.904）仍优于该单探测手工设计PHMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上活动监测对安全与安保（尤其非法活动识别）很重要，但海域规模大、复杂度高。AIS存在已知脆弱性；星载RF观测是独立信息源，却缺乏船舶身份标识，无法单独识别船只，因此需要将RF探测与AIS轨迹进行关联匹配。

Method: 按CRISP-DM流程，构建Pair Hidden Markov Model对单条RF探测与AIS轨迹做对齐，利用时间与空间信息并显式建模噪声与时间偏差；在NOAA AIS数据加合成RF探测上评估，与最近邻基线对比，并通过偏差检验分析误差与轨迹长度、采样密度、静止性及AIS–RF距离的关系；进一步提出去除匹配转移的零模型构成对数几率分数及其长度校正变体。

Result: 长度校正Forward分数在多候选探测验证集上top-1精确率0.7776，通过预筛探测中暗船检测AUC为0.637；单次RF探测时该分数主要反映AIS轨迹本身，误差偏向更长、更静止的轨迹。对数几率分数精确率0.8580、AUC 0.852；其长度校正变体不再显著偏向静止轨迹与短消息间距离，但仍偏向离RF探测更远的候选。未拟合的时间加权最近邻基线更优（精确率0.9013、AUC 0.904）。

Conclusion: 在单次RF探测、手工设计的PHMM设定下，方法接近但未能超越时间加权最近邻基线；对数几率评分显著改善精度与偏差特性，提示模型形式与评分函数仍需改进。

Abstract: Monitoring maritime activities is important for safety and security, particularly for detecting illegal activities, but remains challenging due to the size and complexity of the maritime domain. The Automatic Identification System (AIS) is commonly used for this purpose, despite its known vulnerabilities. In contrast, space-based radio frequency (RF) observations provide an independent source of vessel activity but lack ship identification and therefore cannot identify vessels on their own. Following the CRISP-DM process, this study applies a Pair Hidden Markov Model (PHMM) to align individual RF detections with AIS trajectories, using temporal and spatial information while modeling noise and timing discrepancies. We evaluate it on NOAA AIS data with synthetic RF detections against nearest-neighbor baselines and analyze its errors with bias tests on trajectory length, sampling density, stationarity and AIS--RF distance. The length-corrected Forward score reaches a top-1 precision of $0.7776$ on the validation split of multi-candidate detections, where errors are possible, and an AUC of $0.637$ for detecting dark vessels among the detections that pass the prefilter. With a single RF detection, the score mostly reflects the AIS trajectory itself, and its errors favor longer and more stationary trajectories. A log-odds score, which compares the full model with a null model without match transitions, raises precision to $0.8580$ and AUC to $0.852$. Its length-corrected variant no longer shows a significant bias towards stationary trajectories and short inter-message distances, but still favors candidates farther from the RF detection. A time-weighted nearest-neighbor baseline without fitting remains ahead (precision $0.9013$, AUC $0.904$), so this single-detection, hand-designed PHMM formulation approaches but does not beat it.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Agentic-Teacher: An Agentic Data Engine for Failure-Driven VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17394)
*Gongyue Teng,Yuqing Xie,Yixian Zhang,Feng Gao,Shu'ang Yu,Jiaji Rao,Sukai Wang,Guanghui Ren,Ya Zhang,Maoqing Yao,Yu Wang,Chao Yu,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic-Teacher：一个面向 VLA 模型失败驱动后训练的自主数据引擎，用编码智能体生成并筛选高质量示范数据。在 LIBERO-10、MetaWorld-10 与三项 RoboCasa 任务上显著提升 π0 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型后训练需要能针对其薄弱环节并提供有效监督信号的示范数据；编码智能体虽能通过环境交互自主生成成功轨迹，但"执行成功"并不等于"对 VLA 学习有效"，即数据生成与数据筛选之间存在断层。作者希望把目标 VLA 的失败弱点与训练数据的生成和筛选两端打通。

Method: 输入为一个 VLA 检查点、任务说明与环境工具配置；引擎分析失败 rollout、选择干预状态，并借助执行反馈迭代开发闭环代码策略；验证通过的策略成为可复用的示范生成器，从 VLA 失败状态出发采集成功续段。数据筛选方面引入三项处理：(1) 时间相关动作噪声以扩大状态-动作覆盖；(2) 去除空闲帧但保留夹爪切换帧；(3) 用冻结基模型的 flow-matching 损失过滤轨迹。所得示范与 VLA 成功 rollout 混合用于后训练，最终策略推理时无需编码智能体参与。

Result: 仿真中提升 π0 成功率：LIBERO-10 由 79.4% 升至 87.8%；MetaWorld-10 由 80.4% 升至 90.6%；三项 RoboCasa 任务由 0% 升至 30.0%。

Conclusion: 以失败为驱动的自主数据引擎可同时闭合"数据生成"与"数据筛选"两个环节，把目标 VLA 的弱点显式映射为训练数据来源，从而在不引入推理期编码智能体依赖的前提下实现有效的 VLA 后训练；在低成功率（0%）任务上的提升表明该范式对极端薄弱任务尤为关键。

Abstract: Improving a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model requires demonstrations that address its weaknesses and provide effective supervision for learning. Coding agents can autonomously generate demonstrations through environment interaction, yet successful execution alone does not ensure effective supervision for VLA learning. We introduce Agentic-Teacher, an autonomous data engine for failure-driven VLA post-training. Given a VLA checkpoint, a task specification, and configured environment tools, the engine analyzes failed rollouts, selects intervention states, and iteratively develops closed-loop code policies through execution feedback. Validated policies become reusable demonstration generators that collect successful continuations from states where the VLA struggles. To make these demonstrations suitable for learning, Agentic-Teacher introduces temporally correlated action noise to broaden state-action coverage, removes idle frames while preserving gripper transitions, and filters trajectories using the frozen base model's flow-matching loss. The resulting demonstrations are combined with successful VLA rollouts for post-training, yielding an updated policy that operates without coding-agent assistance. Agentic-Teacher thus connects the weaknesses of a target VLA to both the generation and curation of its training data. In simulation, it improves $π_0$ success rates from 79.4% to 87.8% on LIBERO-10, from 80.4% to 90.6% on MetaWorld-10, and from 0% to 30.0% across three RoboCasa tasks.

</details>


### [152] [Racing the Clock: Teaching VLNs Budget-Conditioned Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17442)
*Maitree Meher,Siming He,S. Shankar Sastry,Jitendra Malik,Claire Tomlin,Jonas Frey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究"预算条件目标搜索"任务：给定目标类别与显式动作预算，智能体须在预算耗尽前尽可能找到更多不同目标实例。作者比较两种监督信号——基于全图与真实目标位置的近最优TSP专家，与基于跨场景语义先验、在部分可观测下最大化期望回报的房间级Bellman专家。结果显示后者监督的VLN在HM3D留出场景上更优，且预算越长优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN模型通常在固定且策略不可观测的episode限制下评测，但时间关键的搜救等任务要求行为随可用预算自适应（30秒应奔向最有希望的房间，10分钟应做更广的巡游）。作者因此提出预算条件目标搜索，并追问：应如何为这类任务监督VLN。

Method: 任务设定为在显式动作预算下最大化发现的不同目标实例数。对比两类专家监督：(1) 特权TSP专家，利用完整地图与真实目标位置规划近最优路径；(2) 房间级Bellman专家，利用高层跨场景语义先验，在部分可观测条件下最大化期望回报，并以此生成示范来监督VLN。

Result: 尽管TSP专家自身性能显著更高，其示范对学习探索却更不有效。在留出HM3D场景上，Bellman监督的VLN持续优于TSP监督的VLN，且优势随预算增长而扩大。2000步（500m）时，Bellman监督VLN找到71.4%的可达目标实例，TSP监督为65.5%，预训练VLN为44.8%。

Conclusion: 结果区分了"最优路径规划"与"最优探索"：有效的探索监督应在部分可观测下优化决策，而非模仿只有在环境完全已知后才最优的路线；专家自身性能高并不等于其示范更适合作为学习信号。

Abstract: A rescue robot entering a building with thirty seconds on the clock should sprint toward the single most promising room; given ten minutes, it should instead explore a broader tour. Yet vision-language-navigation (VLN) models are typically evaluated under a fixed episode limit that the policy never observes, even though time-critical missions require behavior to adapt to the available budget. We study budget-conditioned object search: given a target category and an explicit action budget, an agent must find as many distinct target instances as possible before the budget expires. We investigate how to supervise VLNs for this task by comparing a privileged TSP expert, which plans near-optimal routes using the full map and true target locations, with a room-level Bellman expert that maximizes expected return under partial observability using high-level cross-scene semantic priors. Despite the TSP expert achieving substantially higher expert performance, its demonstrations are less effective for learning exploration: on held-out HM3D scenes, the Bellman-supervised VLN consistently outperforms the TSP-supervised VLN, with the advantage becoming more pronounced at longer budgets. At 2000 steps (500m), the Bellman-supervised VLN finds 71.4% of reachable target instances, compared with 65.5% for the TSP-supervised VLN and 44.8% for the pretrained VLN. These results highlight a distinction between optimal routing and optimal exploration: effective exploration supervision should optimize decisions under partial observability rather than imitate routes that are optimal only once the environment is fully known.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Latent Goal Costs for Visual World-Model Planning with an Expanding Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17529)
*Konstantinos Mylonas,Thrasyvoulos Spyropoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在冻结的视觉世界模型中做图像目标规划，关键在于如何用预测观测对目标进行打分。作者在冻结的 I-JEPA 嵌入上拟合一个小型残差适配器来改进潜在目标代价，无需任务特定进度标签，使规划成功率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于冻结视觉世界模型的图像目标规划依赖“预测观测—目标”打分函数。预训练视觉编码器嵌入间的 MSE 在目标附近有效，但会偏好障碍物对面视觉相似却难以到达的状态，导致规划失败。

Method: 在冻结的 I-JEPA 嵌入之上拟合一个小型残差适配器以改进潜在目标代价。规划器从目标附近出发，逐步扩大初始状态区域收集轨迹；适配器从成功轨迹上观测的先后顺序学习，并利用失败 rollout 推断出的额外比较进行训练。拟合不使用任务特定的进度标签，因此可迁移到其他目标到达任务。

Result: 在起点、目标、世界模型与规划器设置完全相同的配对 50 案例评测中，DotWall 成功率由 54% 提升到 98%，PointMaze 由 82% 提升到 96%。在 16 个未见过的 DotWall 布局-目标案例上，于单一固定布局拟合的适配器将潜在代价排序与最短合法路径排序的一致性从 71.9% 提升到 85.8%。

Conclusion: 规划经验可在不重训编码器或世界模型的前提下改进冻结视觉表征的目标代价，说明成本函数是可学习的关键组件，且所提方法具有一定的跨布局泛化能力。

Abstract: Image-goal planning with a frozen visual world model depends on how predicted observations are scored against the goal. Mean-squared error (MSE) between embeddings from a pretrained visual encoder can provide a useful signal near a goal, yet favor states across an obstacle that are visually similar to the goal and difficult to reach. We fit a small residual adapter on top of frozen I-JEPA embeddings to improve this latent goal cost. Starting near each goal, a planner collects trajectories from progressively wider regions of initial states. The adapter learns from the order of observations along successful trajectories, with additional comparisons inferred from failed rollouts. No task-specific progress labels are used for fitting, so the procedure can be applied to other goal-reaching tasks. DotWall and PointMaze are used in this work. In paired 50-case evaluations with identical starts, goals, world models, and planner settings, success increases from 54% to 98% in DotWall and from 82% to 96% in PointMaze. On 16 unseen DotWall layout-goal cases, the adapter fitted on one fixed layout improves agreement between latent-cost ordering and shortest legal-path ordering from 71.9% to 85.8%. The results indicate that planning experience can improve a frozen visual representation's goal cost without retraining the encoder or the world model.

</details>


### [154] [Talk to Act: A Synthetic Multi-Turn Dialogue Benchmark for Human Intent Extraction and Action Planning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17549)
*Deepu Krishnareddy,Mohammad Haziq Khan,Michael Danner,Matthias Rätsch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向人机交互（HRI）的合成多轮对话数据集，并将意图抽取建模为工具调用（tool-calling）问题，系统评测了270M–8B参数的小型开源语言模型在零任务微调条件下进行意图抽取与动作规划的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话式HRI仍具挑战：现有系统多为刚性的命令式接口，对用户不直观；LLM虽具潜力，但缺乏能反映真实交流细微差别（多轮、歧义、上下文依赖）的数据集与基准，且实际部署中本地化与效率需求使小型模型更受关注。

Method: 合成生成带标注的多轮HRI对话数据集，标注动作意图、目标指代与任务计划，覆盖多场景与多种用户画像；将意图抽取转化为工具调用问题，每个预测动作对应一个结构化函数调用、其参数需由对话接地；在270M至8B参数的开源模型上进行零样本（无任务特定微调）评测。

Result: 即使在较小规模和无需任务特定微调的情况下，模型仍能在该任务上取得合理性能，说明紧凑、可本地部署的模型已可作为对话式机器人系统的有效基线。

Conclusion: 小型开源模型为对话式HRI提供了可用的零样本基线；预期通过任务特定微调与更真实的训练数据可进一步提升性能，使其更适用于真实机器人应用。

Abstract: Multi-turn conversational human-robot interaction (HRI) continues to be a challenging problem in robotics, as many existing systems rely on rigid, command-driven interfaces that can be unintuitive for users. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) provide a promising direction, given their ability to process natural language, generate structured outputs, and support tool-based reasoning, all of which are relevant for interpreting user intent in robotic settings. However, progress in this area is constrained by the limited availability of datasets and benchmarks that capture the nuances of real-world human communication, such as multi-turn dialogue, ambiguity, and context-dependent requests.
  In this work, we present a synthetically generated multi-turn dialogue dataset for HRI, annotated with action intents, object references, and task plans across a range of scenarios and user personas. Our primary focus is on evaluating the capability of small, open-weight language models for robotic tasks, particularly in settings where local deployment and efficiency are important. To this end, we frame intent extraction as a tool-calling problem where each predicted action corresponds to a structured function call with arguments grounded in the dialogue and benchmark models ranging from 270M to 8B parameters on their ability to perform intent extraction and action planning without task-specific fine-tuning.
  Our results show that these models can achieve reasonable performance on the task even at relatively small scales and without any task-specific fine-tuning. This suggests that compact, locally deployable models already provide a useful baseline for conversational robotic systems. We expect that task-specific fine-tuning and more realistic training data will enhance performance further and make such models increasingly suitable for real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [155] [Grounding Object-to-Object Hazard Reasoning into 3D Risk Fields for Task-Conditioned Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17566)
*Mariana Sosa Guzmán,Yide Song,Amin Abyaneh,Gregory Dudek,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的框架，从RGB-D观测与自然语言任务指令推断任务与场景相关的安全约束；先判断任务是否应被拒绝，再识别可预防的物体对危险并指定需避免的空间关系，结合开放词汇分割与深度在3D中构建连续风险场，与避障模块耦合修正机器人运动，在仿真与真实世界中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人安全不能仅靠几何约束，还依赖语义：任务含义、环境属性与物体间交互。现有方法缺乏对任务级、场景级安全约束的语义推理能力。

Method: 1) 给VLM一个结构化"安全工具"，输入RGB-D观测与自然语言指令；2) 判断任务是否因不可恢复的危险后果而被拒绝；3) 否则识别可预防的物体对危险，输出需避免的空间关系；4) 用开放词汇分割与深度将物体在3D中定位，构建表征危险区域的连续风险场；5) 将风险场集成到避障模块，在执行中修正名义轨迹。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界中对可预防危险、需拒绝的任务以及安全变体进行了评估，验证框架有效性。

Conclusion: 将VLM的语义安全推理与几何风险场避障结合，可统一处理"任务拒绝"与"可预防危险规避"两类安全问题，形成从语义判定到运动修正的闭环框架。

Abstract: Safety in robotics does not solely depend on geometric constraints, but also on semantics: the given task, environment properties, and interactions between objects in the scene. We present a framework based on vision-language models (VLMs) to infer task- and scene-dependent safety constraints for robot manipulation. We provide the VLM a structured safety tool based on an RGB-D observation and a natural-language task instruction that first decides whether the task should be rejected since its execution may result in a non-recoverable hazardous outcome. Otherwise, it identifies preventable object-pair hazards and specifies the spatial relations that must be avoided. Open-vocabulary segmentation and depth information are then used to ground the detected objects in 3D and construct continuous risk fields that represent the corresponding hazardous regions. These fields are integrated with an obstacle avoidance module that corrects nominal robot motions during execution while accounting for the risk field. We evaluate the framework on preventable hazards, tasks requiring rejection, and safe variants in simulation and the real-world. Project website: https://vlm-hazard-riskfield.github.io

</details>


### [156] [Natural Swing-Up of an Underpowered Double Pendulum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17575)
*Arne Sachtler,Manuel Keppler,Florian Loeffl,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 弱驱动两连杆直接驱动机器人沿非线性正规模态（二维不变流形上的周期轨道）实现摆起；将力矩分解为功率中性的转向分量与唯一的标量泵能分量，用两变量线性规划分配受饱和约束的力矩，硬件实验验证可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电机很弱的机器人（力矩仅为肩关节重力矩的约6%）不能"对抗"自身动力学，必须顺应自然动力学。而在非严格（non-strict）模态上，注入能量必然使状态偏离流形，因此泵能与镇定争夺同一有限力矩，需要明确解决这一耦合与饱和问题。

Method: 以直接驱动平面两连杆机器人为对象，沿非线性正规模态（由周期轨道构成的二维不变流形）设计摆起。将输入力矩拆分为功率中性的转向（steering）分量与标量泵能（pumping）输入——后者是唯一改变能量的分量。分配问题化为二变量线性规划：在力矩上限与一个控制-Lyapunov约束下最大化泵能；该约束采用基于模型的误差变化率，因此无需设计镇定反馈增益。

Result: 硬件实验实现了两种模态上的摆起，并将该分配方法与"求和后限幅"（summing-and-clipping）以及"优先镇定"（prioritized stabilization）两种基线进行对比。

Conclusion: 将力矩按功率性质解耦并以线性规划做饱和下的最优分配，可在弱驱动条件下同时兼顾沿流形的泵能与偏离抑制，且不依赖手工整定的镇定增益；实验表明其优于常规限幅与优先镇定策略。

Abstract: Robots with weak motors must ride their natural dynamics rather than fight them. We swing up a direct-drive planar two-link robot, whose motors deliver only about 6\,\% of its shoulder gravity torque, along its nonlinear normal modes, two-dimensional invariant manifolds consisting of periodic orbits. On a non-strict mode, injecting energy necessarily moves the state off the manifold, so pumping and stabilization compete for the same bounded torque. We split the input torque into a power-neutral steering component and a scalar pumping input, the only component that changes the energy. The allocation then becomes a linear program in two variables: maximize pumping subject to the torque limits and one control-Lyapunov constraint. The constraint uses the model-based rate of the error, so no stabilizing feedback gain is needed. Experiments in hardware show swing-ups along both modes and compare the allocation with summing-and-clipping and with prioritized stabilization.

</details>


### [157] [X-Nav Technical Report: Real-World Long-Horizon Indoor-to-Outdoor Navigation on a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17598)
*Jiaxin Huang,Chengxing Lin,Xiaoyu Luo,Sirui Chen,Jingyu Guo,Aolin Li,Kairui Chen,Rui Zhen,Cheng Wen,Kerui Gu,Shunyao Wang,Zhihui Peng,Shaoli Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Nav 是首个让双足人形机器人完成从室内到室外、语言指令驱动的长程导航任务的系统，覆盖数据采集、导航模型（MELIOR）、智能体工作流（X-Navarch）到真机部署的全流程，并在灵犀 X2 上完成约百米的室内外跨楼任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程室内外导航对人形机器人仍是开放问题：单条指令需覆盖房间、门与道路，且相机随步态晃动。AGIBOT 的 Worken 指标衡量生产性机器人作业，依赖机器人自主抵达工作地点，因此需要具备语言指令下的室内外长程导航能力。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：(1) 数据引擎，在开源场景、部署站点数字孪生及真机上采集导航数据；(2) 导航模型 MELIOR，仅输入机器人自带相机的 RGB 图像，分为预测动作的 MELIOR-A 与预测导航目标的 MELIOR-N；(3) 智能体工作流 X-Navarch，将简短人类指令拆解为子任务，以显式目标导航与避障方式部署 MELIOR；目标接近与底层控制使用 LiDAR 与里程计。部署阶段还利用站点数字孪生数据对 MELIOR 进行自适应微调。

Result: 在灵犀 X2 上完成约百米任务：从办公室大厅到邻楼商店，两个 MELIOR 版本均成功（可跟随目的地变更、穿门出门上路），配合 X-Navarch 还能绕过静态与动态障碍。数字孪生中的自适应提升了闭环成功率，适配模型随后部署到真实站点。MELIOR 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 基准上取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: X-Nav 验证了纯 RGB 输入的人形机器人语言长程室内外导航的可行性，并提出"数字孪生采集—闭环适配—真机部署"的实用范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon navigation from indoors to outdoors remains an open problem for humanoid robots: one instruction covers rooms, doors and roads, and the camera moves with the gait. Worken, AGIBOT's measure of productive robot work, depends on robots that reach their workplaces autonomously. We present X-Nav, to our knowledge the first system to take a bipedal humanoid through a language-instructed long-horizon mission from indoors to outdoors. X-Nav covers a pipeline from data to deployment. Its data engine collects navigation data in open-source scenes, in digital twins of deployment sites and on real robots. Its navigation model, MELIOR, takes only RGB images from the robot's built-in camera and comes in two versions: MELIOR-A predicts actions, and MELIOR-N predicts a navigation goal. Its agentic workflow, X-Navarch, turns a brief human instruction into sub-tasks and deploys MELIOR on the robot with explicit target navigation and obstacle avoidance. On the Lingxi X2, X-Nav completes a mission of about a hundred metres from an office lobby to a store in a neighbouring building; LiDAR and odometry are used for target approach and low-level control. The mission is completed with both versions of MELIOR: the robot follows a change of destination and walks through a door from the lobby to the road, and with X-Navarch it also passes static and dynamic obstacles on the route. During deployment, MELIOR is adapted with data collected in a digital twin of the site; the adaptation raises its closed-loop success rate in the twin, and the adapted model is then deployed on the robot at the real site. In addition, MELIOR achieves competitive results on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [158] [TouchTwin: Scaling Human Touch Data via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17619)
*Huiwen Han,Nuo Chen,Ziyao Zeng,Dayou Li,Hao Wang,Amir Hossein Rahmati,Xiaoning Qian,Bangya Liu,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchTwin 通过引入实测触觉数据，重建带接触与握力的仿真回放，弥合视觉重建模仿学习中的“力差距”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示正成为灵巧操作的主要数据来源，real-to-sim-to-real 管线可放大演示数量；但其交互仅由视觉重建，只能恢复手如何运动，无法观测哪些手指接触、何时接触、接触力度。手部姿态只反映手指停在表面何处，而非被驱动闭合的程度。实测显示：模仿观测姿态的手策略握力仅为记录值的 0.31 倍，基于视觉跟踪姿态物理回放训练的策略为 1.55 倍，即存在显著的“力差距”。

Method: 采集 TouchScale 记录（头部与腕部 RGB-D 相机 + 880 元触觉手套），在 MuJoCo 中重建每条交互；由编码智能体将物体编写为经物理验证的程序；使用逐受试者的触觉手模型回放交互，以记录到的触觉信号设定各手指的接触与闭合力。

Result: 基于触觉落地回放训练的策略在仿真中握力达记录值的 0.99 倍；在留出录制上成功率 20/20，在 35/36 个未见扰动中成功；其训练的接触预测器在真实接触帧上的漏检率为 13%，而视觉回放为 32%。每条回放还可生成带仿真接触与力标签的扰动演示，并在新视角、新场景下渲染为逼真视频。

Conclusion: 以实测触觉作为监督可有效闭合力差距，使仿真回放的接触与力分布对齐真实交互，提升策略与接触预测的可靠性，并支持带标签的合成演示扩增。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are becoming the main source of data for dexterous manipulation, and real-to-sim-to-real pipelines multiply each demonstration in simulation. These pipelines rebuild the interaction from vision, which recovers how the hand moves. Which fingers touch, when, and how hard remain unobserved, because a hand pose shows where each finger stops on the surface rather than how far it is driven to close. This gap is a force gap: on held-out recordings, a hand policy that imitates the observed pose grips with 0.31x the recorded force, and a policy trained on physics replays of vision-tracked poses grips with 1.55x. TouchTwin closes the gap with measured human touch. Each selected TouchScale recording, captured with head and wrist RGB-D cameras and an 880-taxel glove, is rebuilt in MuJoCo. Coding agents write the objects as physics-tested programs, and a per-subject tactile hand replays the interaction with the recorded touch setting the contact and the closing force of each finger. In simulation, a policy trained on touch-grounded replays grips at 0.99x the recorded force and succeeds in 20/20 trials on held-out recordings and 35/36 unseen perturbations; a contact predictor trained on them misses 13% of real contact frames, against 32% for vision-based replays. Each replay also seeds perturbed demonstrations with simulated contact and force labels, rendered as realistic video in new views and scenes.

</details>


### [159] [On the Utility of Pre-Trained Visual Representations for Policy Learning of Contact-Rich Insertion Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17631)
*Leon Kim,Dinesh Jayaraman,Michael Posa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文聚焦接触密集型操作中的插入任务，系统对比视觉基础模型表征与显式接触中心表征，发现表征优劣高度依赖具体任务与设定，但空间接地、尤其接触中心信息对接触密集型策略学习具有独特价值，同时指出在真实视觉复杂性与传感器噪声下可靠估计此类线索仍很困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动模仿学习数据效率低、对任务无关视觉变化脆弱；预训练视觉表征虽有望缓解，但"适合机器人学的表征"尚无定论——现有研究任务分布广而稀疏，结论不一致甚至相互冲突，显示强任务与设定依赖性。作者希望在单一接触密集型任务上做"放大镜式"精细研究，厘清表征选择的关键因素。

Method: 聚焦"插入"这一接触密集型操作任务，在两个高度相似的插入任务上，将广泛使用的视觉基础模型表征与显式接触中心表征进行对比；沿遮挡程度与物理容差两个受控轴对任务施加扰动，并在仿真与真实世界实验中检验结论的可迁移性。

Result: 任务依赖性比以往宽口径评估所揭示的更为细微：即便在两个近乎相同的任务之间，最优表征也会不同，微小改动即会以难以预测的方式改变趋势。尽管如此，仿真与真机实验一致表明，空间接地信息（尤其接触中心信息）对接触密集型策略学习具有独特价值；同时也凸显了在真实视觉复杂度和传感器噪声下可靠估计此类线索的难度。

Conclusion: 呼吁进一步开展接触密集型操作中的表征研究，并推动接触感知方法及其与视觉运动策略学习集成的进展；表征评估需在任务设定受控且有明确针对性的条件下进行。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation learning has become a predominant paradigm for real-world robotic manipulation, yet it remains data-inefficient and brittle to task-irrelevant visual shifts. Pre-trained visual representations promise to ease this burden by distilling priors from internet-scale data, but the ``right'' representation for robotics remains unresolved: existing studies span broad but sparse task distributions and reach conclusions that vary and even conflict, pointing to strong task and setting dependence. We examine this question by bringing a magnifying glass to a common contact-rich manipulation task: insertion. We compare representations from widely used vision foundation models against an explicit contact-centric representation on two closely related insertion tasks, perturb each along controlled axes of occlusion and physical tolerance, and test whether conclusions transfer to the real world. Task dependence proves to be more subtle than previous, broadly scoped evaluations revealed: the best representation differs even between our near-identical tasks, and small changes shift trends in unpredictable ways. Despite this variability, results across simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the distinct value of spatially-grounded, and in particular, contact-centric information for contact-rich policy learning, while underscoring how difficult it is to estimate such cues reliably under realistic visual complexity and sensor noise. We hope these findings motivate further study of representations in contact-rich manipulation, along with advances in contact perception and its integration into visuomotor policy learning.

</details>


### [160] [AI-guided robotic in situ fabrication of curvature- and tissue-adaptive wireless bioelectronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17638)
*Hiroyuki Tetsuka,Jiaju Ma,Naomi Kumano,Minoru Hirano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI引导的机器人原位打印平台，可在透明伤口敷料上直接制造无线伤口电疗器件，并通过将伤口三维几何与组织介电负载纳入射频设计优化，实现几何自适应、接近25 MHz目标频率谐振的个性化无线生物电子器件制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化伤口治疗生物电子器件既需在复杂体表共形制造，又需使电学特性适配个体伤口几何。传统方法仅将表面几何用于打印轨迹生成，未考虑解剖曲率与组织介电负载导致的谐振偏移，难以保证器件在工作频率下的性能。

Method: 构建集成3D视觉相机、六自由度机械臂、直写打印（DIW）与几何感知射频设计优化的AI引导机器人原位打印平台；利用AI伤口分割获取伤口尺寸与局部表面曲率，将重建的三维几何纳入接收线圈与叉指电极的电磁设计，同时考虑解剖曲率与组织介电负载以补偿谐振偏移；系统表征可打印银墨水的流变学、尺寸可重复性、电阻及机电稳定性，并在曲面聚氨酯敷料上制造多层无线器件；通过实验与数值方法评估无线传能、阻抗特性与电场施加；最后在拟人组织等效前臂体模上演示原位制造，并将实测介电特性纳入射频优化。

Result: 银墨水具备可控流变与良好机电稳定性，可在曲面敷料上实现多层无线器件打印；实验与仿真一致地验证了无线功率传输、阻抗与电场分布；在前臂体模上实现接近25 MHz目标频率的谐振工作。

Conclusion: 工作建立了"感知—设计—制造"的几何自适应无线生物电子框架，为未来物理AI驱动的个性化在体电疗器件制造奠定技术基础。

Abstract: Personalized bioelectronic devices for wound therapy require not only conformal fabrication on complex body surfaces but also functional adaptation of their electrical characteristics to individual wound geometries. Here, we present an AI-guided robotic in situ printing platform for fabricating wireless wound electrotherapy devices directly on transparent wound dressings. The system integrates a 3D vision camera, a six-degree-of-freedom robotic arm, direct-ink-writing, and geometry-aware RF design optimization. AI-based wound segmentation is used to determine wound dimensions and local surface curvature, while the reconstructed three-dimensional geometry is incorporated into the electromagnetic design of the receiving coil and interdigitated electrodes. In contrast to conventional approaches that use surface geometry only for trajectory generation, the proposed method accounts for both anatomical curvature and tissue dielectric loading to compensate for geometry-induced shifts in resonant behavior. A printable silver ink was characterized in terms of rheology, dimensional reproducibility, electrical resistance, and electromechanical stability, and was used to fabricate multilayer wireless devices directly on curved polyurethane dressings. Wireless power transfer, impedance characteristics, and electric-field delivery were evaluated experimentally and numerically. Finally, robotic in situ fabrication was demonstrated on an anthropomorphic tissue-equivalent forearm phantom, where the measured dielectric properties of the phantom were incorporated into the RF optimization, enabling resonant operation near the target frequency of 25 MHz. This work establishes a perception-to-design-to-fabrication framework for geometry-adaptive wireless bioelectronics and provides a technological foundation for future physical-AI-enabled manufacturing of personalized on-body electrotherapy devices.

</details>


### [161] [Imitation Policies Learn a Proprioceptive Anchor: A Predictive Law for Perception Noise and Its Closed Loop Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17658)
*Christopher Malili*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 模仿策略会以 α=V/(V+s²) 的权重折减带噪物体位置读数，其中 V 是本体感受锚点（机器人自身状态所携带的物体位置信息）的误差方差；仅测量一次 V 即可跨任务、跨学习器、跨噪声水平预测策略对读数的敏感度，且能预测扩散策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿策略常在带噪物体位置上训练，并学会折减噪声读数，但折减多少、为何折减尚不清楚；经典变量含误差（EIV）回归对折扣系数的估计偏高，缺乏正确解释。作者希望用一个可测量、可预测的机制量替代演示数据拟合出的线性模型。

Method: 理论建模：演示者朝向物体运动，使机器人自身状态（本体感受）成为物体位置的"锚点"，策略将锚点与读数按贝叶斯式权重 α=V/(V+s²) 融合，s² 为读数噪声，V 为锚点误差方差，且 V 属于学习器而非演示数据。实验：两个 robomimic 任务 × 两种学习器 × 四个新噪声水平，用留出集敏感度检验预测；与经典 EIV 对比；设置无参数偏差检验以排除"策略只是高估噪声"的替代解释；另做闭环持续读数偏置实验与注册式前瞻成功预测（Can 任务、两个新噪声水平），所有检验预先注册。

Result: 仅一次测得的 V 预测了十个留出敏感度，平均绝对误差 0.025，经典 EIV 为 0.37；无参数偏差检验排除简单高估噪声假说。闭环部署中，对持续读数偏置的敏感度在全部 15 个通过注册门槛的策略上均超过 α，但"完全跟随偏置"的更严格注册预测仅在 8 个上成立。事后分析显示更低 α 对应更低成功率，扩散策略尤为陡峭，并经注册前瞻检验在 Can 上两个新噪声水平确认：测得 α 在无 rollout 情况下于 0.03 内（95% 区间 ±0.08）预测成功，不过训练噪声预测效果相当。

Conclusion: 模仿策略对噪声读数的折减可由本体感受锚点误差方差 V 定量解释，V 属于学习器、远小于演示线性模型隐含值，故经典 EIV 高估 α；该折扣在闭环部署中不成立（敏感度超过 α），低 α 与低成功率相关。所有实验均为仿真与合成高斯噪声，局限性明确。

Abstract: Imitation policies for robot manipulation are often trained on noisy object positions, and they learn to discount the noisy reading. We show how much they discount it, and why. Because demonstrators steer toward the object, the robot's own state carries information about where the object is. A trained policy combines this proprioceptive anchor with the reading and weights the reading by $α= V/(V + s^2)$, where $s^2$ is the reading noise and $V$ the anchor's error variance. $V$ belongs to the learner and is far smaller than a linear model of the demonstrations implies, which is why classical errors-in-variables (EIV) regression overestimates $α$. Measured once, $V$ predicted ten held-out sensitivities across two robomimic tasks, two learners and four new noise levels, with mean absolute error 0.025 against 0.37 for classical EIV, and a parameter-free bias test rules out the simpler explanation that the policies just overestimate the noise. The discount does not carry over to deployment, where the policy produces its own state: closed-loop sensitivity to a persistent reading offset exceeded $α$ in all 15 policies that passed the registered gates, although the stricter registered prediction, that all would follow the offset fully, held for 8 of them. Post hoc, lower $α$ went with lower success, steeply for diffusion, and a registered prospective test confirmed this for diffusion on Can at two new noise levels: the measured $α$ predicted success before any rollout, within 0.03 (95% intervals $\pm 0.08$), although training noise predicts it equally well. Every test was registered before its data, including the classical benchmark that failed, and all other analyses are labelled post hoc; all results are in simulation with synthetic Gaussian noise.

</details>


### [162] [EViDS: An Environment--Vehicle Dynamics Integrated Simulation Platform for Intelligent Chassis Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17660)
*Lu Xiong,Yiming Zhou,Xuge Hu,Yi Zhang,Haoran Lv,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EViDS——一个面向智能底盘开发的环境-车辆动力学一体化仿真平台，将CARLA环境仿真与CarSim高保真车辆动力学耦合，实现时空一致的多速率交互与轮胎-路面射线投射交互，支持环境感知的闭环智能底盘控制验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能底盘开发需要将环境与感知信息同高保真车辆动力学融合，以支撑环境感知的底盘控制；但现有自动驾驶仿真器与专业车辆动力学工具开发目的不同，难以统一集成环境信息、车辆动力学与底盘算法。

Method: 构建EViDS平台：以CARLA提供环境与传感器仿真，CarSim提供高保真车辆动力学；采用基于参考位姿的状态对齐与多速率交互保证空间与时间一致性；通过基于射线投射的轮胎-路面交互将环境信息传递至车辆动力学模型；并提供场景配置、同步数据采集与算法接口。

Result: 通过环境-动力学交互测试与车辆动力学一致性对比，并结合智能底盘应用进行评估；结果表明EViDS能保持车辆动力学特性、可靠传递环境信息，并支持在线闭环智能底盘控制。

Conclusion: EViDS为环境感知的智能底盘开发与验证提供了统一的仿真基础，弥补了环境仿真与车辆动力学工具割裂的问题。

Abstract: The development of intelligent chassis increasingly requires the integration of environmental and perception information with high-fidelity vehicle dynamics to support environment-aware intelligent chassis control. However, existing autonomous-driving simulators and professional vehicle-dynamics tools are generally developed for different purposes, making it difficult to integrate environmental information, vehicle dynamics, and chassis algorithms. This paper proposes EViDS, an Environment-Vehicle Dynamics Integrated Simulation Platform for Intelligent Chassis Development. EViDS integrates CARLA-based environment and sensor simulation with CarSim-based high-fidelity vehicle dynamics. Reference-pose-based state alignment and multi-rate interaction maintain spatial and temporal consistency, while ray-casting-based tire-road interaction transfers environmental information to the vehicle-dynamics model. Scenario configuration, synchronized data acquisition, and algorithm interfaces further support intelligent chassis development. The platform is evaluated through environment-dynamics interaction tests with vehicle-dynamics consistency comparisons, together with intelligent chassis applications. Results show that EViDS preserves vehicle-dynamics characteristics, reliably transfers environmental information, and supports online closed-loop intelligent chassis control, providing a unified simulation basis for environment-aware intelligent chassis development and validation.

</details>


### [163] [Ego2Flow: Benchmarking the Traffic-System Impact of Autonomous Driving on Maps Distilled from Real-World Roads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17720)
*Yu Dong,Weiying Shen,Hangyu Li,Shu Han,Hong Li,Xiangbo Gao,Keshu Wu,Zhengzhong Tu,Soyoung Ahn,Zhiyuan Liu,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环基准 Ego2Flow，在由真实城市地图蒸馏得到的 CARLA 地图上评估自动驾驶策略对整体交通系统的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶评估主要关注自车安全与任务完成，忽略了其在城市交通中与多车持续交互所产生的集体效应，系统层面影响缺乏充分评估。

Method: 设计智能体引导的地图蒸馏框架，融合真实地图、观测与补充地理信息；通过迭代反馈机制与交通信息图共同引导场景生成，在保留车道拓扑、关键道路几何与决策相关视觉条件的同时简化非必要细节并补全未观测区域；基于该框架从 Argoverse 2 生成并公开六张 CARLA 地图，构建支持配对真实/仿真观测对比的闭环仿真。

Result: 相较现有构建方法，配对真实与仿真观测下规划输出的平均位移误差最低；在多种道路结构下评估自动驾驶策略对交通效率、安全性、稳定性与能耗的影响，并对代表性驾驶策略进行系统级基准测试。

Conclusion: Ego2Flow 为自动驾驶的城市交通系统级影响提供了可复现的闭环评估平台，并开源地图与基准，推动从单车子安全评估向系统效应评估扩展。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving is predominantly evaluated through ego-vehicle safety and task completion. Its deployment in urban traffic, however, creates sustained interactions among vehicles whose collective effects remain insufficiently evaluated. We introduce Ego2Flow, a closed-loop benchmark for assessing the traffic-system impact of autonomous driving on maps distilled from real-world cities. To construct realistic 3D urban environments for sustained closed-loop traffic simulation, we develop an agent-guided map distillation framework that integrates real-world maps and observations with complementary geographic information. Through an iterative feedback mechanism and a traffic information graph that jointly guide scene generation, the framework preserves lane topology, critical road geometry and decision-relevant visual conditions while simplifying nonessential details and completing unobserved regions. Using our proposed framework, we generate and publicly release six CARLA maps from the Argoverse 2 dataset, covering the corresponding real-world regions and enabling paired comparisons of driving behavior under real and simulated observations. On these maps, Ego2Flow evaluates how autonomous driving policies affect traffic efficiency, safety, stability and energy consumption across diverse road structures. Compared with existing construction methods, our framework achieves the lowest average displacement error between planning outputs from paired real and simulated observations. We further benchmark representative driving policies to characterize their system-level impact on urban traffic. Project Page: https://github.com/Fisher-007/Ego2Flow.

</details>


### [164] [ERA: Error-Resistant Autonomous Object Navigation with Predicted Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17730)
*Shunyang Huang,Shuo Wang,Wenyuan Xie,Mingyu Lin,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ERA：一种无需训练的零样本目标导航系统，用RGB图像预测深度替代深度传感器，通过"带误差移动"范式（可修正占据地图+可达视点探索+跨视角目标验证）在三个基准上取得无深度传感器、免训练方法中的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度与3D基础模型可从RGB预测几何，使无深度传感器的零样本目标导航成为可能。但若把预测深度当作传感器深度直接建图，视角相关误差会在建图中累积、抹除探索目标，导致智能体在能够观察并纠正误差之前就终止搜索。

Method: 提出ERA（免训练框架），遵循"Move with Error"范式：移动带来新视角以修正地图误差，修正后的地图又支撑进一步移动。具体由三部分组成：可修正的占据地图（revisable occupancy）、从可达视点出发的探索（无前沿时仍能继续搜索）、停止前跨视角的目标验证。

Result: 在三个基准上，ERA在无深度传感器、免训练方法中达到SOTA；其地图与探索模块在相同预测深度下将VLFM成功率从10.8%提升至61.7%；并在真实机器人上完成部署。

Conclusion: 预测深度可在系统层面（而非仅模型层面）替代深度传感器，关键在于将地图视为可随移动而修正的假设，并以可达性驱动的探索与跨视角验证保障搜索鲁棒性，具备真实机器人可部署性。

Abstract: Depth and 3D foundation models can predict geometry from RGB images, allowing zero-shot object navigation without a depth sensor. However, when predicted depth is mapped as if it came from a sensor, view-dependent errors accumulate in the map, remove exploration goals, and end the search before the agent can observe and correct them. We propose ERA, a training-free object navigation system that replaces the depth sensor with depth predicted from RGB images and poses. ERA follows a paradigm we call Move with Error: movement provides new views that correct map errors, and the corrected map enables further movement. It realizes this paradigm with revisable occupancy and with exploration from reachable viewpoints, which continues the search when no frontier remains, and verifies the target across views before stopping. On three benchmarks, ERA achieves state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods without depth sensors. Its map and exploration components also raise VLFM's success from 10.8% to 61.7% under the same predicted depth. We further deploy ERA on a real robot.

</details>


### [165] [When Does Cross-View Consistency Matter in Multi-View Appearance Augmentation? A Controlled Study for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17751)
*Zixuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文用仿真状态回放解耦“跨视角一致性”的具体形式，系统量化外观一致性与几何一致性对下游扩散策略控制性能的影响，发现外观耦合可放松、跨视角几何对应需保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角视觉增强常默认追求跨相机一致性，但具体哪类一致性对下游控制真正重要尚不清楚；若一概强制一致，可能施加无必要甚至有害的约束。

Method: 采用仿真状态回放（simulation-state replay）在保持各相机扰动分布、物理轨迹与动作标签不变的前提下操纵跨视角耦合关系。在 LIBERO 的 7 个操作任务上，对晚融合 Diffusion Policy 分别施加边缘分布匹配的全局外观扰动、ghost 注入、目标颜色扰动以及几何反耦合扰动（匹配位移幅度、方向边缘分布与受影响视角数），并报告贝叶斯后验区间；辅以相机干预与特征分析，并与 ACT 对比。

Result: 外观类扰动（全局外观、ghost、目标颜色）平均代价接近零甚至为负，90% 后验可信区间最大上端点为 2.55 个百分点；几何反耦合相对耦合观测使成功率下降 3.30 个百分点（90% CrI [0.64, 6.28]）。相机干预显示对腕部视角几何依赖显著；特征分析显示外观敏感性与训练期随机化因素相关。ACT 对目标颜色的代价估计高于 DP，但区间含零且对任务构成敏感。

Conclusion: 在测试任务与策略配置下，结论支持“选择性一致性约束”：外观耦合有时可放松，而任务相关几何与跨视角空间对应关系值得保护。

Abstract: Multi-view visual augmentation often seeks consistency across cameras, but it is unclear which forms of consistency matter for downstream control. We use simulation-state replay to manipulate cross-view coupling while preserving the per-camera perturbation distributions, physical trajectories, and action labels. Across seven LIBERO manipulation tasks, marginal-matched changes in global appearance, ghost injection, and target color have small average costs for a late-fusion Diffusion Policy: point estimates are near zero or negative, and the largest upper endpoint of the 90% posterior credible intervals is 2.55 percentage points. In contrast, geometrically anti-coupled observations reduce success by 3.30 percentage points relative to coupled observations (90% CrI [0.64, 6.28]), with displacement magnitude, direction marginals, and the number of affected views matched. Camera interventions reveal substantial dependence on wrist-view geometry, while feature analysis shows appearance sensitivity patterns associated with the factors randomized during training. ACT shows a higher target-color cost estimate than DP, with uncertainty that includes zero and sensitivity to task composition. Within the tested tasks and policy configurations, these findings motivate selective consistency constraints: appearance coupling can sometimes be relaxed, whereas task-relevant geometry and cross-view spatial correspondence warrant protection.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Prediction Gain: LASR as a Metric for Action-Grounded Latents in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17775)
*Yu Zhou,Changsong Lei,Yixiang Wei,Kairui Wen,Huankun Sheng,Ming Li,Yeying Fan,Yu-Hui Wen,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无因次指标LASR（Latent Action Support Ratio），在离线留出数据上量化隐动作变量对未来预测的贡献中，超出当前状态与非动作替代量的部分，并用GeoLASR与WorstLASR两类汇总刻画典型与最弱情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要真正编码动作的潜表示，但大规模视频缺乏动作标注，只能依赖从观测序列推断隐动作的模型；然而提升预测性能的潜变量可能只是编码当前状态、episode进度、任务身份等预测捷径。现有评估（重构损失、rollout保真度、动作恢复、下游性能）无法隔离这一区分。

Method: 构建LASR基准：以记录动作为正目标、物理状态变化为动作相关目标，采用episode级留出划分，所有比较共用同一预测器类型，并设置正负对照（非动作捷径）。定义无因次比值指标LASR衡量潜变量对动作相关目标的预测支持相对于状态与非动作替代量的增益，输出GeoLASR（典型支持）与WorstLASR（最弱情形）两个汇总；并据此训练与审计轻量残差适配器。

Result: 在机器人操作记录数据及匹配的多任务面板上，LASR能够检测已知动作信息、拒绝代表性非动作捷径，并支持对不完美潜表示的梯度化比较；用LASR训练与审计的残差适配器在点估计上描述性更优，其不确定性通过配对比较评估。

Conclusion: LASR为衡量世界模型潜表示中动作相关预测支持提供了有原则的离线评估协议，可区分真正的动作信息与预测捷径，并用于训练与审计改进。

Abstract: World models that support planning, control, and interpretable reasoning require latent representations that genuinely encode action. Yet the large-scale video data from which such models are learned overwhelmingly lack action annotations. Latent-action models infer action-like variables from observation sequences, enabling action-aware learning from unlabeled videos or trajectories. A fundamental concern, however, remains: a latent that improves future prediction may simply encode the current state, episode progress, task identity, or other predictive shortcuts. Existing evaluation practices---reconstruction loss, rollout fidelity, action recovery, or downstream performance---cannot isolate this distinction. We introduce the Latent Action Support Ratio (LASR), a dimensionless metric that measures, under offline evaluation on held-out data, whether a learned latent provides predictive support for action-related targets beyond the current state and non-action alternatives. LASR provides two complementary summaries: GeoLASR captures typical support, while WorstLASR identifies the weakest case. We instantiate the LASR benchmark with recorded actions and corresponding physical state changes as targets, episode-held-out splits, one predictor type for all comparisons, and positive and negative controls. Experiments on recorded robot manipulation data and a matched multi-task panel demonstrate that LASR detects known action information, rejects representative non-action shortcuts, and enables graded comparisons among imperfect latent representations. We further use LASR to train and audit a lightweight residual adapter, obtaining descriptively better point estimates whose uncertainty is assessed with paired comparisons. LASR thus provides a principled offline protocol for measuring action-related predictive support in world-model latents.

</details>


### [167] [Multimodal Person Concept Model for Person Identification under Varying Observation Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17802)
*Mizuho Okitani,Yoshinobu Hagiwara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态人物概念模型，基于中餐馆过程非参数贝叶斯生成模型，融合CLIP/CLAP特征并经PCA降维，实现无需人物标签、从少量观测中学习人物概念并在变化条件下实现鲁棒识别，平均识别准确率达0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭与办公场景的服务机器人需识别用户以提供个性化服务，但可用观测样本少，且外观变化、环境噪声和话语内容差异会使观测条件发生改变，导致识别困难；同时缺乏人物标签。

Method: 构建多模态人物概念模型：采用基于中餐馆过程的非参数贝叶斯生成模型，可自动推断人物概念数量；融合视觉、声学、语言三类特征，特征由CLIP与CLAP提取后经PCA降维；支持无标签、小样本学习。

Result: 在6名被试、8种条件（外观变化、加性噪声、转写错误单独及组合出现）下评估，8条件平均识别准确率0.89，且模型自行推断概念数；与提供真实人数信息的k-means（0.88）相当。此外分析了PCA维度、模态可用性与变化强度的影响，并在丰田HSR机器人上完成演示。

Conclusion: 多模态人物概念学习能在变化条件下支持鲁棒人物识别；即使概念数量未被正确恢复，识别仍可成功。

Abstract: Service robots in domestic and office environments must identify users to provide personalized services based on their preferences and previous interactions. This is challenging because only a few observations may be available for learning, while observation conditions change owing to appearance, environmental noise, and utterance content. To address this challenge, this study proposes a multimodal person concept model that learns person concepts from a small number of observations, without person labels, and enables robust identification under changing conditions. The model employs a nonparametric Bayesian generative model based on the Chinese restaurant process and integrates visual, acoustic, and linguistic features extracted using Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) and Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP), followed by principal component analysis (PCA). We evaluated six participants under eight conditions in which appearance changes, additive noise, and transcription errors were introduced individually and in combination. Averaged over the eight conditions, the proposed model achieved an identification accuracy of 0.89 while inferring the number of person concepts from the observations, which is comparable to k-means clustering that was supplied with the true number of participants (0.88). We also examined PCA dimensionality, modality availability, and variation strength, and demonstrated the model on a Toyota Human Support Robot. These results show that multimodal person concept learning supports robust person identification under changing conditions, and that identification can succeed even when the number of concepts is not correctly recovered. The project page and demonstration video are available at https://okitamisan01.github.io/person_concept/.

</details>


### [168] [Per-Agent-Safe Navigation of Globally Driven Robot Collectives via Affine Field Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17859)
*Seunghwan Jang,Steven Ceron,Wei Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种“广播式”仿射场控制框架，用单一空间仿射场同时驱动群体机器人的运动与形变，并借助控制屏障函数在单个二次规划中统一施加逐机器人的墙壁、驱动与内聚约束，实现无需预规划变形的狭窄通道安全穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群体（swarmalator）控制器依赖局部控制，每个机器人接收独立指令，导致控制与通信开销随智能体数量线性增长；同时需要在受限环境中协调运动与形变，兼顾每个机器人的安全。

Method: 引入广播控制形式：单个空间仿射场同时支配集体的运动与形变。场的标量分量调节集体尺寸，其空间梯度通过非互易相互作用产生集体平移；两分量直接进入集体动力学，从而可在整个集体的单一二次规划（QP）内用控制屏障函数（CBF）施加逐机器人墙壁约束、驱动约束与内聚约束。进一步推导驱动预算与可达速度、最小可通行走廊宽度之间的关系。

Result: 控制器能随局部环境连续自适应调整集体尺寸，在无预规划缩放的情况下安全通过比无约束集体更窄的走廊；给出驱动预算决定可达速度与最小可通行宽度的解析关系；并证明两个独立驱动的集体可凭不同固定相位标签同时通过共享交叉口，而无需集体间协调。

Conclusion: 广播式仿射场控制以与智能体数量无关的指令维度实现了群体运动—形变的统一安全控制，兼具可扩展性、安全保证与解析可设计性，为受限环境中群体机器人导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Safe navigation of robot collectives requires coordinating motion and deformation so that the collective can traverse constrained environments while ensuring each robot's safety. Existing controllers for swarmalator collectives largely rely on local control, where each robot receives an individual command, which causes the control and communication requirements to grow with the number of agents. We introduce a broadcast control formulation in which a single spatially affine field governs both the motion and deformation of the collective. The scalar component of the field regulates collective size, and its spatial gradient generates collective translation through non-reciprocal interactions. Both components enter the collective dynamics directly, which allows per-robot wall, actuation, and cohesion constraints to be enforced using control barrier functions within a single quadratic program for the whole collective. The resulting controller continuously adapts the collective size to the local environment, and enables safe passage through corridors narrower than the unconstrained collective without pre-planned resizing. We further derive how the available actuation budget determines the reachable speed and minimum passable corridor width, and we demonstrate that two independently driven collectives can simultaneously traverse a shared junction using distinct fixed phase labels without inter-collective coordination.

</details>


### [169] [Reusing Locomotion Training Recipes Across Humanoids via Body-Aligned Virtual Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18003)
*Sol Choi,Mingon Kim,Seokin Seo,Jongeun Choi,Donghyeon Kim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"体对齐虚拟关节空间"作为策略与机器人原生关节坐标之间的标准接口，使同一套RL训练配方可跨腿轴布局不同的人形机器人复用；在三种人形机器人与G1实体上稳定训练并显著降低航向漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已能实现稳健的人形机器人行走，但训练配方（奖励、模型架构、超参数）难以跨机器人复用：策略输出的是原生关节角，而动作缩放、动作边界、增益与惩罚项都定义在机器人特定的坐标上，导致不同原生关节坐标系的机器人之间接口不统一。现有传动（transmission）模型虽能把原生目标转成执行器指令，却未标准化暴露给策略的坐标接口。

Method: 在该映射的上游引入体对齐虚拟关节空间（body-aligned virtual joint space）：策略的动作与观测遵循统一的解剖学约定，再由一个确定性运动学层将虚拟目标映射为原生关节目标；现有传动层与低层控制栈保持不变，即无需改动奖励与超参数。

Result: 在腿部轴布局不同的三种人形机器人上使用同一未修改的配方，该接口是唯一能同时在三者上精确跟踪、保持足部与行进方向对齐并稳定训练的接口；在Unitree G1硬件上，航向漂移降低近六倍。

Conclusion: 标准化"暴露给策略的坐标接口"而非改动传动或低层控制，是提升人形机器人RL训练配方可移植性的关键，且该虚拟关节空间在仿真多平台与实体硬件上均验证有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has enabled robust humanoid locomotion, yet training recipes (rewards, architectures, hyperparameters) remain hard to reuse across robots with different native joint coordinates, since policies output native joint angles while action scales, bounds, gains, and penalties are defined in those robot-specific coordinates. Existing transmission models convert native targets into actuator commands but do not standardize the coordinate interface exposed to the policy. We introduce a body-aligned virtual joint space upstream of this mapping: policy actions and observations follow a common anatomical convention, and a deterministic kinematic layer maps virtual targets to native ones, leaving the existing transmission and low-level control stack unchanged. Under a single unmodified recipe on three humanoids with different leg-axis layouts, it is the only interface that tracks accurately, keeps the feet aligned with the travel direction, and trains stably on all three, and on Unitree G1 hardware it reduces heading drift by nearly a factor of six.

</details>


### [170] [A Generalizable Approach to Safety-Aware Robotic Behavior Planning with an Explicit Constraint Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18026)
