*Xianxu Wu,Shang-Ching Liu,Zhaocong Liang,Hengxiang Chen,Yujian Ma,Shenwen Deng,Qiang Li,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以显式“约束空间”（Constraint Space）为核心的安全感知行为规划框架：将可复用文本安全规则存储其中，用任务/环境条件化的适用性学习（轻量查询条件双塔模型）挑选约束，再由多模态行为规划器生成原始图元计划，并引入多模态Safety Inspector做纠错式安全审查与反馈，最后通过测试时自适应（约束适用性更新 + LoRA参数高效适配）吸收新危险知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM/VLM已显著提升基于自然语言指令与视觉观测的机器人行为规划，但桌面操作中的安全难以保证：所需安全约束依赖周围场景，且往往未在指令中显式给出。仅依靠语义相似度检索安全规则会漏掉环境相关约束，同时存在约束误选与未见危险的问题。

Method: 1) 构建显式Constraint Space，存储可复用的文本安全规则；2) 将约束选择建模为任务与环境条件化的适用性学习，用轻量级query-conditioned双塔模型判定当前上下文适用的约束（而非纯语义相似度检索）；3) 将选中约束与原始任务、图像一并输入多模态行为规划器生成图元计划；4) 多模态Safety Inspector评估生成计划、重建完整适用约束集合并给出纠正性规划反馈；5) 经校验的审查反馈用于约束适用性的测试时自适应与Safety Inspector的LoRA参数高效适配，新发现的约束回写Constraint Space。

Result: 摘要为框架性描述，未给出具体量化实验结果；给出的是方法学层面的结论：框架实现了显式安全知识表示、上下文感知约束选择、纠错式安全审查与测试时知识自适应的统一集成，可应对约束误选与先前未见危险。

Conclusion: 安全行为规划需要显式、可复用且场景条件化的约束知识，而非仅依赖指令语义。通过“约束空间 + 适用性学习检索 + 多模态安全审查反馈 + 测试时自适应”的闭环，可提升桌面操作中的安全性，并使系统在部署中持续更新安全知识。

Abstract: Large language and vision-language models have substantially advanced robotic behavior planning from natural-language instructions and visual observations. However, safe manipulation in tabletop environments remains challenging because the safety constraints required for a task depend on the surrounding scene and may not be explicitly specified in the instruction. We propose a safety-aware behavior planning framework based on an explicit Constraint Space that stores reusable textual safety rules. Rather than retrieving rules solely by semantic similarity, we formulate constraint selection as task- and environment-conditioned applicability learning. A lightweight query-conditioned dual-tower model identifies the constraints applicable to the current context, which are then provided together with the original task and image to a multimodal behavior planner. To handle incorrect constraint selections and previously unseen hazards, we introduce a multimodal Safety Inspector that evaluates generated primitive plans, reconstructs the complete applicable constraint set, and provides corrective planning feedback. Validated inspection feedback is further used for test-time adaptation of constraint applicability and parameter-efficient adaptation of the Safety Inspector through LoRA, while newly identified constraints are incorporated into the Constraint Space. The resulting framework integrates explicit safety knowledge representation,context-aware constraint selection, corrective safety inspection, and test-time knowledge adaptation for safety-aware robotic behavior planning.

</details>


### [171] [PoliMop: Policy-in-the-Loop Motion Planning in 3D Spaces for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18110)
*Yael Fassbind,Valentin N. Hartmann,Huang Su,Yijiang Huang,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理仿真的运动动力学RRT规划器，将控制策略编码的技能作为扩展算子，在引导采样下为多种机器人找到可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中的可达路线取决于机器人本体的物理设计、所用控制策略以及其对环境的改造，传统规划器难以充分利用这些能力。

Method: 采样三维降维状态空间中的状态，用策略rollout沿样本方向扩展RRT；以简单质点模型的预规划路径引导采样；并通过物理仿真利用控制策略中的技能，同时支持预定义的环境改造。

Result: 引导采样可减少最多63%的规划时间；四足、人形与轮式机器人实验表明该方法可即插即用地集成不同机器人与控制策略，并自动利用环境改造（如搭板成桥）扩展可达空间。

Conclusion: 无需修改底层规划算法即可发挥机器人物理与策略能力，具备良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Reaching a goal state in a complex environment often requires a robot to overcome challenging terrain and obstacles. Which routes are accessible depends on the robot's physical design, the control policy it uses, and how it modifies its surroundings. We present a kinodynamic rapidly-exploring random tree (RRT) planner that uses physics simulation to exploit skills encoded in the control policy, which allows it to find a path in the scene with respect to the robot's capabilities. The planner samples states from a reduced state space in 3D around the scene and expands the RRT toward each sample using policy rollouts. We guide sampling with a preplanned path for a simple mass-point model to focus exploration on promising regions leading toward the goal state, reducing planning time by up to 63%. Simulation experiments with quadrupeds, humanoids, and wheeled robots demonstrate that our approach supports drop-in integration of various robots and control policies, exploiting their physical and policy capabilities without modifying the underlying planning algorithm. Predefined environmental modifications, such as placing a plank across a gap to build a bridge, will automatically be picked up by the planner as the robot's reachable space expands.

</details>


### [172] [Evaluating Language Models as Spatial Priors for Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18123)
*Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文考察两个开放权重大模型（Qwen3.5-27B、Gemma-4-31B-it）能否作为室内目标物体位置的“空间先验”来源，结论是：LLM可用于物体—房间关联和“在给定约束下写坐标”，但不适合替代真实布局得到的放置先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体目标导航的语义探索方法主要用LLM给出“目标物体与房间类型或邻近物体的关联强度”，而未利用LLM提供目标物体在房间内位置的空间分布先验，论文旨在验证开放权重LLM能否承担这一角色。

Method: 基于CubiCasa5K与ScanNet的真实房间布局，评估模型对真实布局与扰动布局赋予的似然、以及模型生成的布局；分析生成房间的物体频率、房间尺寸、物体贴墙距离等统计是否遵循房间类型差异；并考察提示中给定物体或给定“物体所贴墙面”时的条件放置行为；最后以均匀先验与真实布局先验为基线，比较生成布局作为搜索先验时的可搜索面积缩减效果（含空间平滑版本）。

Result: 生成房间的物体频率与房间尺寸符合房间类型，但物体贴墙距离未体现真实房间中不同房间类型的差异；似然部分通过几何、部分通过“坐标与物体贴墙壁面字段的一致性”区分真实与扰动布局；对ScanNet可移动家具，多数房间中“全部堆在房间中心”的布局似然反而高于真实布局；给定相关物体时模型按其相对关系放置，给定墙面时多数物体贴该墙，但不能保证都落在给定房间矩形内；作为搜索先验，生成放置相比均匀先验能缩减搜索面积，但不足真实布局先验缩减量的1/3，双方空间平滑后不足2/3。

Conclusion: LLM适合用于物体—房间关联，以及在其他组件确定约束后生成坐标并辅以几何校验；但在有真实布局可用时，不宜作为放置先验的来源。

Abstract: Semantic exploration methods for object-goal navigation use large language models (LLMs) to score how strongly a target object is associated with a room type or with a nearby object. This paper considers obtaining from the LLM a spatial prior instead: a distribution over the position of the target in a room. We evaluate whether two open-weight LLMs, Qwen3.5-27B and Gemma-4-31B-it, can provide such a prior. Using real room layouts from CubiCasa5K and ScanNet, we measure the likelihood the models assign to real and perturbed layouts, and the layouts they generate. The object frequencies and room sizes of the generated rooms follow the room type, but the proximity of the objects to the walls does not follow the differences between room types found in real rooms. The likelihood distinguishes real layouts from perturbed ones partly through their geometry and partly through the agreement between the coordinates and a field that states the wall each object touches; for the movable furniture of ScanNet, it is higher for layouts with all objects piled near the room center than for the real layouts in most rooms. When an object is given in the prompt, the models place a related object relative to it. When the wall of each object is given, they place most objects against that wall, but do not keep all objects inside a given room rectangle. As a prior for object search, the generated placements reduce the searched area relative to a uniform prior, but by less than a third of the reduction obtained with a prior built from real layouts, and by less than two thirds when both priors are spatially smoothed. These results support the use of LLMs for object-room associations and for writing coordinates under constraints determined by another component, followed by a geometric check, but not as a source of placement priors where real layouts are available.

</details>


### [173] [ESI: An Embodied Self-Improvement Flywheel for LLM-VLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18176)
*Yundi Cai,Zihan Wang,Chenxi Han,Yi Cheng,Houde Liu,Zilei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ESI 将"LLM+VLA"模块化具身智能体整体作为后训练单元，利用交互中的协作过程本身提供监督，迭代对齐 LLM 提示、技能接口与 VLA 参数，在 RoboDojo 十项任务上取得 62.03 分与 49.41% 成功率，超过 GO-3 Nano Preview 及代码式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立预训练的 LLM 与 VLA 策略虽能提供互补能力，但简单拼接并不产生协调行为：VLA 执行的动作可能偏离 LLM 意图的子任务；且在 LLM 纠错后重新接管时，可能破坏 LLM 已恢复的有希望状态。因此需要一种机制把推理与动作真正对齐。

Method: 提出 ESI（Embodied Self-Improvement）自动化飞轮：(1) 以模块化智能体整体为后训练单元，而非分别训练各模块；(2) 循环精化三类组件——引导 LLM 的提示、表达意图的共享技能接口、实现意图的 VLA 参数，并联合重新部署；(3) 核心思路是"协作本身即监督"：LLM 的子任务决策为策略应执行的内容提供语义监督，LLM 的有效纠正及其后续学生延续提供恢复与交接的动作级监督；(4) 学生也从自身有效执行中学习，使局部有用经验在整体回合失败时仍可贡献。

Result: 在选定的 10 个 RoboDojo 任务上，使用不生成任务专用控制代码的 LLM-VLA 智能体，ESI 取得平均 Score 62.03 与平均成功率 49.41%；两项指标均超过所有混合具身智能体的公开参考结果，包括 Agent+WAM 系统 GO-3 Nano Preview（47.27 分、36.60% 成功率），也优于所有对比的代码式方法。

Conclusion: 协作过程本身可作为一种有效监督信号，用于联合改进提示、技能接口与策略参数；以模块化智能体为单位的自提升飞轮能在无需任务专用代码的前提下显著提升长程具身任务表现，说明"接口对齐"是释放 LLM-VLA 组合潜力的关键。

Abstract: Independently pretrained language models and vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide complementary building blocks for embodied agents, but connecting them does not automatically produce coordinated behavior. The VLA may generate actions that diverge from the LLM's intended subtask and, upon regaining control after a correction, undo the promising state restored by the LLM. We introduce ESI (Embodied Self-Improvement), an automated flywheel that uses environmental interaction to align reasoning and action. ESI treats the modular agent as the unit of post-training, repeatedly refining the prompts that guide the LLM, the shared skill interface that expresses its intent, and the VLA parameters that realize that intent, then redeploying these components together. Our key insight is that collaboration itself can supply supervision for improving this interface. The LLM's subtask decisions provide semantic supervision for what the policy should execute, while its effective corrections and subsequent student continuations provide action-level supervision for recovery and handoff. The student also learns from its own effective execution, allowing locally useful experience to contribute even when the overall episode fails. On ten selected RoboDojo tasks, ESI achieves a mean Score of 62.03 and a mean success rate of 49.41% using an LLM-VLA agent without generating task-specific control code. On both metrics, it outperforms the published reference results of all hybrid embodied agents, including the Agent+WAM system GO-3 Nano Preview (47.27 Score and 36.60% success), as well as all compared code-based methods.

</details>


### [174] [NPU-Accelerated GNSS Signal Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18186)
*Taro Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将小型单板计算机(SBC)上的神经处理单元(NPU)用作GNSS信号捕获中并行码搜索的加速器，而非用于机器学习；通过四步DFT分解将捕获中的DFT转化为NPU擅长的1×1卷积、逐元素乘和转置，并给出CPU/NPU任务划分方案，在Pocket SDR中实现(PocketSDR-NPU)并取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小型单板计算机上，GNSS信号捕获是主要的CPU负载来源，而这类设备的CPU资源有限，因此需要卸载/加速捕获计算以同时支撑捕获与跟踪。作者注意到SBC上已有NPU（如外接AX8850或SoC内置RK3588 NPU）闲置或主要用于机器学习，遂提出将其复用于信号处理计算。

Method: 1) 将捕获中的DFT用四步DFT分解为小DFT矩阵乘积，并表达为1×1卷积、逐元素乘和转置，使NPU可高效执行；2) 提出按NPU配置（PCIe连接自带内存的外接NPU，或与CPU共享内存的内置NPU）在CPU与NPU之间划分DFT、非相干积分与峰值检测的调度方法；3) 在开源GNSS接收机Pocket SDR中实现，并在Raspberry Pi 5+AX8850与ROCK 5C(RK3588内置NPU)上评测。

Result: 相比优化CPU实现：Raspberry Pi 5+AX8850上短码捕获快3.0–3.8倍；整机评测中，30 s内首次锁定的跟踪通道数为CPU的1.5倍，90%通道锁定时间由66 s降至26 s；实时可处理跟踪通道数从约175提升至约230。

Conclusion: 在CPU资源受限的SBC上，将NPU用于GNSS信号捕获可同时提升捕获与跟踪性能，是兼顾两者的有效手段；实现已以PocketSDR-NPU名称开源发布。

Abstract: On small single-board computers (SBCs), GNSS signal acquisition is a major CPU load. This paper proposes using the neural processing unit (NPU) of an SBC not for machine learning but as an accelerator for the parallel code search in GNSS signal acquisition. The discrete Fourier transform (DFT) used in acquisition is decomposed by the four-step DFT into matrix products of small DFTs and expressed by $1\times1$ convolutions, element-wise products, and transposes, which NPUs execute efficiently. Furthermore, we propose a method that divides the DFT, the non-coherent integration, and the peak detection between the CPU and the NPU according to the configuration of the NPU (an NPU with its own memory connected via PCIe, or an NPU sharing memory with the CPU). The proposed method is implemented in the open-source software GNSS receiver Pocket SDR and evaluated on a Raspberry Pi 5 combined with the external AX8850 NPU and on the NPU built into the SoC (RK3588) of the ROCK 5C, a small SBC of comparable size. Compared with acquisition by an optimized CPU implementation, the acquisition of short codes on the Raspberry Pi 5 with the AX8850 is 3.0-3.8 times faster. In the evaluation of the whole receiver, the number of tracking channels that reach their first lock within 30 s with the NPU is 1.5 times that with the CPU, and the time until 90% of the channels are locked is reduced from 66 s to 26 s. Furthermore, because the CPU load of acquisition is reduced, the number of tracking channels that can be processed in real time increases from about 175 to about 230. These results show that using the NPU on SBCs with limited CPU resources is effective for achieving both GNSS signal acquisition and tracking. The implementation is publicly available as open source under the name PocketSDR-NPU.

</details>


### [175] [Objectives as Policies: Vision-Language Model Cost Programs for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18298)
*Zihao Lu,Trong Gia Hung Nguyen,Fuquan Wang,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Objectives as Policies (OaP) 框架，用可编辑的目标程序作为 VLM 与采样型 MPC 之间的共享任务表示，实现接触丰富的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 具备强语义与空间推理能力，MPC 具备物理规划能力，但二者结合用于接触丰富操作时缺乏能表达物理交互的任务表示。

Method: 以可编辑目标程序为桥梁：程序由多阶段序列组成，每阶段组合成本库中的运行/终端代价与阶段完成条件；给定自然语言指令与场景观测，经 real-to-sim 构建仿真数字孪生，VLM 生成目标程序并在仿真中由共享采样型 MPC 执行，依据 rollout 迭代更新后再部署到真实世界。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的四个桌面操作任务上均优于强基线。

Conclusion: 结构化目标程序是连接 VLM 语义推理与 MPC 物理规划的有效接口，且仿真迭代闭环可支持 sim-to-real 部署。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong semantic and spatial reasoning capabilities in 3D environments. Recently, connecting VLMs with model-predictive controllers (MPC) has emerged as a paradigm for combining strong semantic reasoning with physics-based planning. However, connecting these two for contact-rich robotic manipulation requires a task representation that bridges the two and captures how physical interactions can be reasoned by VLMs. In this work, we introduce Objectives as Policies (OaP), a framework that connects a pretrained VLM to a shared sampling-based MPC through an editable objective program. Each program specifies a sequence of stages for completing a task, combining running and terminal costs from a cost library with stage completion conditions. Given a natural-language task instruction and scene observation, we build a simulation-based digital twin through real-to-sim, and the VLM generates an objective program that is executed by the MPC in the simulation. The program is iteratively updated by the VLM based on MPC rollouts, before being deployed in the real world. We evaluate our framework in both simulation and the real world on four tabletop manipulation tasks, and show that it outperforms strong baselines. Additional videos and examples are available on the project website: https://zihaolu001.github.io/oap-project/

</details>


### [176] [PROOF: Perceptive Robustness via Out-of-Distribution Fallback for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18305)
*Siyuan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Jinxuan Lu,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PROOF框架，通过两阶段教师-学生学习显式建模感知可靠性，使四足机器人在视觉退化（遮挡、强光变化等）时自动回退到本体感知策略，保持稳健运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉感知运动虽使足式机器人能适应复杂地形，但高度依赖视觉观测；遮挡、反光表面、光照变化会导致感知退化，进而引发灾难性运动失败，因此需要显式处理感知可靠性问题。

Method: 采用两阶段教师-学生范式：教师同时学习感知驱动行为与本体感知回退行为；学生预测外感知特征及其不确定性，并在分布外输入下抑制这些特征，从而触发回退。

Result: 在配备RealSense D435深度相机的Unitree Go2四足机器人上实测：正常视觉条件下可完成大跨越、高台、窄梁等高动态跑酷动作；在严重遮挡与强光照变化下仍保持稳定鲁棒的运动性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模感知不确定性并引入本体感知回退机制，可有效提升视觉感知足式运动在退化感知条件下的鲁棒性，且不牺牲正常条件下的高动态能力。

Abstract: Vision-based perceptive locomotion has enabled legged robots to achieve agile and terrain-aware locomotion across complex environments. However, the reliability of such systems heavily depends on visual observations, which are inherently susceptible to occlusion, reflective surfaces, and illumination variations, often resulting in catastrophic locomotion failures under degraded perception conditions. In this paper, we present PROOF (Perceptive Robustness via Out-of-Distribution Fallback for Legged Locomotion), a perceptive locomotion framework that enables robust locomotion under severe visual disturbances. Our method adopts a two-stage teacher-student learning paradigm that explicitly models perception reliability: the teacher learns both perception-driven and proprioceptive fallback behaviors, while the student predicts exteroceptive features with uncertainty and suppresses them under out-of-distribution inputs. We evaluate our method on a Unitree Go2 quadruped equipped with a RealSense D435 depth camera in real-world settings. Under normal visual conditions, our method enables the robot to successfully perform high-dynamic parkour maneuvers, including traversing large gaps, elevated platforms, and narrow beam terrains. Under severe visual degradation, such as heavy occlusion and strong illumination changes, the robot continues to maintain stable and robust locomotion performance.

</details>


### [177] [Envisioning Agentic Robotics: A New Era of Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18359)
*Grid Robotics,:,Qingfeng Lan,Duo Li,Siyao Li,Yiheng Lyu,Shijian Lu,Zhenghao Qi,Shizun Wang,Xinchao Wang,Ming Yang,Zhiqin Yang,Zuhao Yang,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场/综述性论文，提出"具身系统2"（Embodied System 2）作为智能体机器人（agentic robotics）的决策核心，并倡导建立统一的定义、评估框架与研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 GPT-6 Astra 等前沿模型配合智能体外壳（agentic harness）在多个具身基准上追平甚至超越任务专用策略，"智能体机器人"新范式出现，但社区尚无公认的定义、可靠的评估框架与研究路线图，亟需理论定位与系统性规范。

Method: 以立场论证为主：将系统拆解为基础模型 + 具身外壳构成的"具身系统2"决策核心，分析其核心难题——如何在反应时间有限、机械极限、环境不可预测、后果不可逆等物理约束下，把通用推理转化为有效且可验证的动作；并据此提出同时衡量模型能力与闭环系统性能的评估框架，梳理从数字智能体迁移到物理智能体的原则与待解挑战。

Result: 指出智能体机器人采用"观察—理解意图—推理—调用技能与工具—验证结果—调整策略"的闭环运行方式；明确其核心瓶颈是物理约束下的可验证动作生成，而非单纯的策略训练；给出覆盖模型能力与闭环系统表现的双层评估设计，并归纳可迁移原则与关键挑战清单。

Conclusion: 作者主张以具身系统2加评估框架为基础，规划通向"持续自主"（sustained autonomy）的研究路线：从执行单项原语逐步扩展到承担长期职责，实现安全可靠、责任范围渐进扩大的自主工作能力。

Abstract: The release of GPT-6 Astra marks the emergence of a new paradigm in robotics: agentic robotics. Within weeks, frontier models with agentic harnesses matched or exceeded task-specific policies on many embodied benchmarks. Instead of training a dedicated policy for each family of tasks, an agentic robotic system applies a foundation model to open-world embodied tasks. It operates in a closed loop: observing the environment, interpreting user intent, reasoning about the task, invoking skills and tools, verifying outcomes, and adapting its strategy. Despite these advances, the community has yet to converge on a shared definition, a reliable evaluation framework, or a research roadmap. We argue that Embodied System 2, realized by a foundation model together with an embodied harness, forms the decision-making core of agentic robotics. Its central challenge is to translate general-purpose reasoning into effective and verifiable actions under physical constraints such as limited reaction time, mechanical limits, unpredictable environment changes, and irreversible consequences. Motivated by this perspective, we propose an evaluation framework that measures both model capabilities and closed-loop system performance. Furthermore, we identify the principles that carry over from digital to physical agents and the challenges that must be addressed to achieve reliable physical autonomy. On top of the Embodied System 2 and the evaluation framework, we envision a research roadmap toward sustained autonomy: the ability to perform work safely and reliably with progressively broader responsibility, from executing individual primitives to fulfilling standing roles.

</details>


### [178] [Heavy Metal Acrobatics: Learning Dynamic Control for Skid-Steer Loaders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18365)
*Pierre-Jean Lorentz,Pascal Egli,Edo Jelavic,Cliff Li,Ramitha Sundar,Harikrishnan Suresh,Paul Montgomery,Lee Redden,Matthias Müller,Ozan Sener,Vladlen Koltun,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在仿真中用强化学习训练策略并迁移到真实世界，可让5吨级滑移装载机自主完成两轮平衡、越野行驶、轨迹跟踪、原地旋转及铲斗摆动等高难度特技动作，达到此前仅少数顶尖操作员在官方世界纪录中实现的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型机械（建筑、采矿、物料搬运）自动化有提升生产率的巨大潜力，但液压驱动带来的流体、柔性元件与非线性延迟使动力学高度复杂；同时机器体量大、重量重，直接在实体系统上做实验风险极高，亟需安全的训练与验证途径。

Method: 采用仿真中强化学习训练 + sim-to-real 迁移的框架；实验平台为可实际作业的 John Deere 332G 五吨滑移装载机；训练出的策略直接部署于真机，执行将机器翘起至两轮、两轮平衡行驶、轨迹跟踪、旋转及铲斗前后摆动后安全落回四轮等一系列动态特技动作。

Result: 策略在真实机器上成功完成上述全部特技动作，将机器推至其执行器能力极限，动作难度相当于此前仅由少数精英操作员在官方世界纪录表演中完成的水准；验证了 sim-to-real 迁移在重型机械动态自主控制上的可行性。

Conclusion: 强化学习与仿真到现实迁移能够实现重型机械的动态自主控制，为建筑、采矿等重型装备领域的自动化提供了可行路径；成果发表于 Science Robotics（2026年11月11日，DOI: 10.1126/scirobotics.aef0894）。

Abstract: Automation of heavy machinery offers the opportunity to improve productivity in mechanized industries such as construction, mining, and material handling. Yet, automation of such machines is challenged by their complex dynamics, in part because of hydraulic actuation that involves fluids, flexible elements, and nonlinear delays. A concomitant challenge is the size and weight of these machines, which can make experiments on the physical systems exceedingly dangerous. Here, we show that \ac{RL} in simulation, followed by transfer of the learned policies from simulation to reality, can successfully automate acrobatic maneuvers that push a heavy construction machine to the limits of its actuation and have only previously been accomplished by a small number of elite human operators in official world-record performances. We trained policies that perform autonomous acrobatic maneuvers on a John Deere 332G, an operational 5-tonne skid-steer loader. The policies can autonomously swing the machine up onto two wheels and, while balancing on two wheels, drive over rough terrain, trace out specified trajectories, spin around, and swing the bucket back and forth before landing it safely back onto four wheels. This work demonstrates that \ac{RL} and sim-to-real transfer enable dynamic autonomous control of heavy machinery, directly contributing to greater automation in heavy equipment sectors. Videos: https://sites.google.com/leggedrobotics.com/heavy-metal-acrobatics
  This is the author's version of the work. It is posted here by permission of the AAAS for personal use, not for redistribution. The definitive version was published in Science Robotics on 11 November 2026, DOI: https://doi.org/10.1126/scirobotics.aef0894

</details>


### [179] [Vision-Language-Action Models: A Life-Cycle Survey of Design, Training and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18372)
*Suvarna Kadam,Soran Parsa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2022年12月至2026年10月间88项视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型研究，沿"模型设计—训练—部署"七大维度（输入与记忆、架构、动作输出、中间预测、预训练数据、后训练、实时执行）梳理VLA全生命周期，并对22个模型的数据、规模、推理速度与硬件进行横向对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA将网页预训练视觉语言模型适配为输出机器人动作，从而把web知识迁移到物理控制。近4年间该领域从首个VLA RT-2迅速发展到单张消费级GPU即可微调的开源模型与可清理未见家庭环境的闭源通用模型，但缺乏对模型设计、训练与部署全链条的系统性梳理与量化对比。

Method: 综述式分析：选取88篇工作（主要覆盖操作与导航，兼及人形控制、世界-动作模型与评测），按7个维度组织——(a)输入与记忆、(b)架构、(c)动作输出、(d)中间预测（推理步骤到未来观测）、(e)预训练数据（机器人/web/人类/仿真混合）、(f)后训练（面向目标机器人的适配）、(g)实时执行；并抽取22个模型报告训练数据、模型规模、推理速度与硬件，同时与世界-动作模型进行比较。

Result: 归纳出三大趋势：(i)训练数据显著拓宽，从π0约1万小时遥操作机器人数据，到SmolVLA约2.3万条社区采集episode与GR00T N1.7约2万小时自我中心人类视频；(ii)模型规模呈两极分化，近期多数模型为3–7B参数，同时出现可运行于消费级GPU的1B以下新梯队；(iii)出现同时处理操作与导航的通用VLA（如UniVLA、Qwen-VLA）。此外指出部分基准分数接近满分的VLA在物体或相机轻微移动时即失效。

Conclusion: VLA已在数据规模、模型规模分层与通用化三个方向快速演进，但基准性能与实际鲁棒性之间存在显著落差；作者据此提出后续研究问题，强调需关注真实扰动下的泛化与可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models adapt web-pretrained vision-language models to output robot actions, so that web knowledge carries over to physical control. In under 4 years the field has moved from RT-2, the first VLA, to open models that fine-tune on a single consumer GPU and to proprietary generalists that clean unseen homes. This survey reviews 88 works from December 2022 to October 2026, mainly on manipulation and navigation, plus humanoid control, world-action models and evaluation. We follow a VLA through its life cycle, from model design to training and deployment, along seven axes. Model design covers (a) inputs and memory, what the model observes; (b) architecture, how its pipeline is built; (c) action output, how it produces motor commands; and (d) intermediate predictions, what else it predicts, from reasoning steps to future observations. Training covers (e) pretraining data, the mix of robot, web, human and simulated data, and (f) post-training, adaptation to the target robot. Deployment covers (g) real-time execution, keeping pace with the robot. We analyse 22 models on these axes and report their training data, model size, inference speed and hardware. Three trends emerge: (i) training data has broadened, from about 10,000 hours of teleoperated robot data ($π_0$) to 23,000 community-collected episodes (SmolVLA) and 20,000 hours of egocentric human video (GR00T N1.7); (ii) model sizes have split: most recent models have 3-7 billion parameters, while a new group under one billion runs on consumer GPUs; and (iii) generalist VLAs are emerging that handle both manipulation and navigation, such as UniVLA and Qwen-VLA. We also compare VLAs with world-action models, which predict future observations as well as actions. We review evidence that some VLAs with near-perfect benchmark scores fail when objects or the camera move slightly, and open up further research questions.

</details>


### [180] [The State Is Not the Bottleneck: A Paged-State Gated-DeltaNet Tile on a Tri-Mode Multicast Mesh, Bit-Exact from RTL to Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18422)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在Agilex 5 FPGA上实现一个完整层的369M参数混合Gated DeltaNet线性注意力模型的硬件瓦片，以验证将固定大小循环状态分页到片外存储器是否会主导解码开销。结果表明分页仅占计算总量的至多11.2%，权重读取而非状态是解码瓶颈，状态分页在边缘FPGA上可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力语言模型用固定大小的循环状态取代不断增长的KV缓存，但在边缘FPGA上该状态（6.2 MiB）超过片上块存储（0.87 MiB），必须分页到外部存储器。作者由此提出核心问题：这种分页是否会主导解码（逐token生成）的开销。

Method: 构建一个在Agilex 5 FPGA上计算自定义369M参数混合Gated DeltaNet模型单层的硬件瓦片并对其循环状态进行分页；该瓦片是三模态处理单元多播网格中的一个节点；采用定点运算并与浮点结果及定点参考实现进行对比验证，在仿真中核对全部18个循环层，在板级进行多次构建与长序列运行测试。

Result: 每解码一个token的分页带来26.0 MB额外访存，至多占计算总量的11.2%；板级75 MHz、合成权重下单token在该层耗时11.15 ms，其中权重矩阵-向量乘约占62%，含分页的状态阶段占13%；定��硬件与定点参考逐比特一致，仿真的18个循环层全部一致，板级6个构建通过全部30次64-token运行和2次1024-token运行。

Conclusion: 对该模型和存储接口而言，权重传输限制了FPGA上的解码性能，而循环状态并非瓶颈；因此状态分页在此类边缘FPGA模型上是实际可行的，分页开销不主导解码成本。

Abstract: Linear-attention language models replace a growing key-value cache with a recurrent state of fixed size. On an edge FPGA, this state can exceed the on-chip memory and must be paged to external memory. This paper asks whether such paging dominates the cost of decoding (generating tokens). We built a hardware tile that computes one complete layer of a custom 369-million-parameter hybrid Gated DeltaNet model on an Agilex 5 FPGA and pages its recurrent state. The model state needs 6.2 MiB, whereas the device has 0.87 MiB of block memory. Paging adds 26.0 MB of memory traffic per decoded token, at most 11.2 percent of the calculated total. On the board at 75 MHz, with synthetic weights, one token takes 11.15 ms in this layer. The matrix-vector products with the weights take about 62 percent of this time and the state phase, including the paging, 13 percent. The tile is one node of a multicast mesh of tri-mode processing elements. We measure the error of the fixed-point arithmetic against floating point and check that the hardware agrees bit for bit with a fixed-point reference. In simulation, the state logic agrees with the reference on all 18 recurrent layers. On the board, six builds pass all 30 runs of 64 tokens and two runs of 1,024 tokens. For this model and memory interface, the delivery of the weights limits the decoding performance, and the recurrent state does not. State paging is therefore practical for such a model on an edge FPGA.

</details>


### [181] [From AI-Enabled Remote Patient Monitoring Toward Agentic Robotics: Embodiment, Governance, and Care Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18484)
*Ivana Ragusa,Lorenzo Guiducci,Filippo Cavallo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是一篇结构化范围综述，指出AI远程患者监护尚未进入成熟的"能动性照护"阶段：多数系统仍由人主导，决策-行动闭环与具身AI仅停留在原型与试点。作者以医疗机器人为切入点，提出"能动性机器人"作为未来方向——嵌入照护生态、可中断、可审计、受人类监督与明确AI治理约束的受监督系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI远程患者监护正从被动数据采集转向能解释患者状态、协调照护并主动采取行动的能动性AI，但这一转变在真实临床与家庭场景中的落地程度不明。现有研究缺乏对"监测→行动"转化关键环节（具身性、临床整合、人类监督、治理）的系统梳理，因此需要以证据明确当前成熟度边界并提出可操作的发展路线。

Method: 结构化范围综述（structured scoping review）。以医疗机器人为分析透镜，因其最能显性化从监测到行动的转化——将AI生成的建议与决策转译为语音、运动、提示、物理辅助、升级上报与照护协调；据此围绕具身性、临床整合、人类监督与治理四项设计要素对文献进行归纳与成熟度评估。

Result: 成熟能动性照护尚未出现。多数系统为人类主导；更强的决策-行动耦合与具身AI主要局限于原型与试点研究；具身性、临床整合、人类监督与治理被识别为基本设计前提而非附加项。

Conclusion: 进一步进展依赖安全、可互操作的照护架构，使智能组件通过经临床验证且可问责的工作流运行。作者提出"能动性机器人"作为未来方向：将AI智能体与机器人同临床医生、照护者与患者连接于可审计、可中断、处于人类监督与明确AI治理之下的工作流中。

Abstract: AI-enabled remote patient monitoring is evolving beyond passive data collection toward agentic AI systems that interpret patient states, coordinate care, and initiate action across domestic and clinical settings. This structured scoping review finds that mature agentic care has not yet emerged: Most systems remain human led, while stronger decision-action coupling and embodied AI remain largely confined to prototypes and pilots. Healthcare robotics makes the transition from monitoring to action especially visible by translating AI-generated recommendations and decisions into speech, movement, prompting, physical assistance, escalation, and care coordination, making embodiment, clinical integration, human oversight, and governance fundamental design requirements. Further progress requires safe and interoperable care architectures in which intelligent components operate through clinically validated and accountable workflows. We propose agentic robotics as a future direction for supervised systems embedded in the care ecosystem, connecting AI agents and robots with clinicians, caregivers, and patients through auditable workflows that can be interrupted and remain subject to human oversight and explicit AI governance.

</details>


### [182] [When Will You Hand It Over? A Hidden Start Delay Makes Self-Play Handover Robust to Unfamiliar Partners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18491)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Mike Degany,Deyuan Qu,Qing Yang,Matyas Szanto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出隐藏起始延迟随机化（HSD），一行规则改造合作自博弈中的学习型伙伴，使受物机器人对陌生伙伴的交接成功率从31%提升到80%，并大幅减少高力接触，无需重训伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作自博弈可让类人机器人零人类数据学会接物，但训练时的伙伴与部署时的伙伴并不一致；流畅的自博弈交接策略在遇到陌生伙伴时失效（成功率从90%+跌至31%），作者旨在定位该泛化失败的根因并修复。

Method: 在Unitree G1与学习型给予者的物理仿真包裹交接任务中，双方用交替PPO自博弈训练。通过配对干预实验（对自身伙伴引入延迟）隔离失败原因：共享奖励下学习型给予者总是立即发起递交，导致接收方只学到单一汇合点。据此提出隐藏起始延迟随机化（HSD）：对学习型伙伴施加起始延迟随机化，且该延迟对接收方不可观测。

Result: 接收方与自身伙伴成功率95%/90%（两个种子），与脚本化陌生伙伴仅31%。自身伙伴延迟1秒将使成功率降至6%（第二种子87%→34%）。加入HSD再训练300次更新后，陌生脚本伙伴成功率升至80%（两种子），预算匹配对照组仅35–41%；增益在新场景种子、11种留出伙伴条件及超出训练延迟范围的伙伴上均成立；高力接触减少3–5倍。

Conclusion: 自博弈交接的泛化失败源于伙伴行为单一化导致的"单一汇合点"过拟合；仅对伙伴施加不可观测的起始延迟随机化即可显著提升对陌生伙伴的鲁棒性并降低接触力，且成本极低、无需重训伙伴，为自博弈策略的伙伴泛化提供了简洁有效的正则化思路。

Abstract: Cooperative self-play can teach a humanoid to receive an object without any human data, but the partner it meets after training is never the one it trained with; we show that a fluent self-play handover policy breaks with unfamiliar partners, identify the main cause, and repair it. In a physics-based parcel handover between a Unitree G1 and a learned giver, both trained by alternating PPO self-play, the receiver completes 95% and 90% of handovers with its own partner (two training seeds) but only 31% with scripted partners it has never met. Paired interventions isolate the cause: under a shared reward the learned giver always starts its offer at once, so the receiver learns a single rendezvous, and delaying its own partner by one second cuts success from 95% to 6% (87% to 34% in the second seed). We introduce hidden start-delay randomisation (HSD), a one-line rule on the learned partner that the receiver never observes. After 300 further updates, success with unseen scripted partners rises from 31% to 80% in both seeds, against 35--41% for a budget-matched control. The gain holds on a fresh scenario seed, on eleven held-out partner conditions and on partners that start later than any training delay; it cuts high-force contact three- to five-fold and needs no retraining of the partner.

</details>


### [183] [Toward Resilient Self-Driving Laboratories through Telerobotic Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18496)
*Ziheng Wu,Adrian Vecina Tercero,Andre A. Fatehi,Abhik Sarkar,Brian Giera,Eric B. Duoss,Christopher M. Spadaccini,Nikhil Deshpande,Aldair E. Gongora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究遥操作干预机制以提升自驱动实验室（SDL）对突发故障的恢复能力：开发基于3D鼠标控制六自由度UR3e机械臂的遥操作界面，比较耦合与解耦两种速度控制器，并考察参考路径引导对操作绩效的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDL集成机器人、自动化实验与机器学习实现自主实验，已应用于生物学和化学；但系统复杂度上升使其自动化流程更易受意外故障影响，亟需高效恢复机制。作者提出以人在回路的遥操作干预弥补自主系统的脆弱性。

Method: 构建遥操作界面，操作者通过3Dconnexion SpaceMouse Pro精确控制六自由度UR3e机械臂；评估两种速度型控制策略：(i) 耦联模式，六自由度全部映射至3D鼠标中央旋钮；(ii) 解耦模式，将平移与旋转运动分离。研究一在三种SDL故障场景下开展对比试验；研究二在有无参考路径引导条件下执行瞄准任务，分析操作者行为差异。

Result: 对比试验显示解耦控制器完成任务时间更短、旋转运动更少。瞄准任务中，无引导时用户更目标导向、风险容忍度更高；不同引导策略下操作者行为存在差异。

Conclusion: 控制器配置与参考路径设计影响SDL故障恢复中的操作者绩效，可为遥操作界面优化提供依据；遥操作作为SDL工作流的人在回路恢复机制具有可行性，融合机器人精度与人的适应性和问题解决能力，并为共享自主与半自主干预的后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) accelerate scientific research by integrating robotics, automated experimentation, and machine learning to execute experiments autonomously. Biology and chemistry have adopted these systems. However, increasing SDL complexity heightens susceptibility to unexpected faults in automated workflows, highlighting the need for efficient recovery mechanisms. In this study, we investigate telerobotic interventions to enhance SDL resilience against unexpected failures. We developed a teleoperation interface enabling human operators to resolve disruptions during automated tasks. The interface enables precise control of a six-degree-of-freedom (DOF) robotic arm (UR3e) using a 3D mouse (3Dconnexion SpaceMouse Pro). Two velocity-based controller strategies were evaluated: (i) a coupled mode mapping all six DOFs to the 3D mouse's central knob and (ii) a decoupled mode separating translational and rotational motions. To evaluate the framework, teleoperators intervened across three SDL fault scenarios. In the first study, comparative trials showed that the decoupled controller was associated with shorter completion times and reduced rotational motion. In a second study, teleoperators performed an aiming task with and without reference-path guidance. Users were more target-driven and risk-tolerant without guidance but showed behavioral differences under different guidance strategies. These findings suggest that controller configuration and reference-path design influence operator performance during SDL fault recovery, informing teleoperation interface optimization. This work demonstrates the potential feasibility of teleoperation as a human-in-the-loop recovery mechanism for SDL workflows, combining robotic precision with human adaptability and problem-solving. These findings also provide a foundation for future studies in shared autonomy and semi-autonomous intervention.

</details>


### [184] [In Situ Monitoring and Predictive Modeling of Harmful Algal Blooms with Autonomous Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18501)
*Nenyi Kweku Nkensen Dadson,Jean Paul Gonzalez,Daniel P. Arabie,Ryan J. Cedel,Dorin Boldor,Corina Barbalata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于BlueBoat自主水面艇的模块化水质感知系统，集成绞车投送多参数探头、侧扫声呐与事件相机，在路易斯安那州湖泊开展多深度走航观测，并用机器学习实现有害藻华/非藻华分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害藻华（HABs）对沿海与内陆水体造成环境、经济和公共健康方面的显著挑战，亟需可扩展的空间分布式水质数据采集手段；传统人工采样效率低、覆盖有限。

Method: 构建基于BlueBoat ASV的自主感知平台：集成电动绞车实现深度可控的多参数水质探头投放，加装侧扫声呐与事件相机载荷；通过自主航点任务在选定区域和多个深度采集水质数据；在Lake Pontchartrain与False River开展现场部署，采集藻类色素指示物、营养盐、浊度、温度等参数；并基于所采数据评估机器学习分类方法用于HAB与非HAB判别。

Result: 实现了水平与垂直方向的水环境条件刻画，同步获得声呐与事件相机的情境化环境信息；数据集在两地采集以提升多样性；机器学习分类方法验证了基于水质测量进行藻华判别的可行性。

Conclusion: 结果证明了模块化ASV平台用于可重复、多模态水质数据采集的可行性，为后续自主监测系统的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Harmful Algal Blooms (HABs) pose significant environmental, economic, and public-health challenges in coastal and inland water bodies, creating a need for scalable methods to collect spatially distributed water-quality data. This paper presents an autonomous surface vessel-based sensing system for in-situ water-quality monitoring related to harmful algal bloom detection. The system integrates a BlueBoat Autonomous Surface Vessel (ASV), a motorized winch for depth-controlled sonde deployment, a multiparameter water-quality sonde, side-scan sonar, and an event-camera payload. Autonomous waypoint missions were used to collect measurements across selected sampling regions and multiple depths, enabling both horizontal and vertical characterization of environmental conditions. Field deployments were conducted in Lake Pontchartrain, Louisiana, with additional measurements collected in False River, Louisiana to increase dataset diversity. The collected measurements included algal pigment indicators, nutrient concentration, turbidity, temperature, and other water-quality parameters relevant to bloom formation. In addition, complementary sonar and event-camera data were collected during vehicle operation to provide contextual environmental information. A machine-learning classification approach was also evaluated using the collected water-quality measurements to support HAB and non-HAB classification. The results demonstrate the feasibility of using a modular ASV platform for repeatable, multi-modal water-quality data collection and support future development of autonomous monitoring systems.

</details>


### [185] [Disturbance-Aware Informative Planning in Spatiotemporal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18505)
*Kaleb Ben Naveed,Haejoon Lee,Anouck Girard,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对未知、空间变化但时间不变的外部扰动下的机器人持续监测问题，提出两阶段信息规划框架：先用几何路径引导扰动采样，再将扰动信念融入轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部扰动会影响机器人实际运动，若不建模会导致运动预测与信息获取估计失真；但全域估计扰动效率低，因与监测轨迹重叠少的区域扰动影响有限。因此需只估计"对监测性能有影响"的扰动。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段基于环境几何确定需要QoI测量的位置并生成候选路径，再依据这些路径上扰动不确定度的预期下降量对扰动测量点排序（信息驱动的扰动采样）；第二阶段将学习到的扰动信念嵌入轨迹优化，替代名义动力学来评估扰动对运动与信息获取的影响。

Result: 在四个仿真场景中，相较全域估计方法，扰动学习时间缩短25.0%–52.3%；并能识别出在名义动力学下看似代价高、但考虑扰动后更优的路径。

Conclusion: 有选择地学习与监测轨迹相关的扰动，并将其纳入轨迹优化，可显著提升扰动学习效率并改变路径偏好，说明扰动感知规划对持续监测任务具有实际价值。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are widely used for persistent monitoring of spatiotemporal environments, where quantities of interest (QoI) change across space and time and require repeated measurements. In practice, robot motion can be affected by external disturbances that vary across the environment. When such external disturbances are not considered in trajectory optimization, it can lead to inaccurate predictions of robot motion and, consequently, the information acquired about the QoI during execution. However, estimating disturbances throughout the environment can be inefficient, as disturbances in regions with little overlap with monitoring trajectories have limited impact on monitoring performance. We therefore propose a two-stage informative planning framework for unknown, spatially varying but time-invariant disturbances, combining informed disturbance sampling with disturbance-aware trajectory optimization. First, we identify where QoI measurements are needed and generate alternative paths to these locations using the environment geometry. We then prioritize disturbance measurements based on their expected reduction of disturbance uncertainty along these paths. Second, rather than optimizing information acquisition based on nominal robot motion, we incorporate the learned disturbance belief into trajectory optimization to account for its effects on robot motion and information acquisition. Across four simulation scenarios, the proposed method reduces disturbance-learning time by 25.0--52.3 percent compared with environment-wide estimation and identifies paths that appear costly under nominal dynamics but become preferable when disturbances are considered.

</details>


### [186] [Modeling the Frictional Contact of Continuum Robots with Environments through Linear Complementarity Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18546)
*Jia Shen,Junhyoung Ha,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于库仑摩擦假设下互补约束的新型摩擦模型，可同时刻画连续体机器人的静摩擦、滑动摩擦与环境交互滞回及相应变形，并满足实时计算需求，将末端位置最大误差从45.4 mm降至5.85 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人的精确运动控制与路径规划依赖准确的摩擦建模。现有方法仅预测受库仑摩擦锥边界力约束的机构内部摩擦，无法刻画静摩擦与运动滞回，也难以描述环境交互引起的摩擦效应。

Method: 在库仑摩擦假设下构建基于互补约束（complementarity constraints）的摩擦模型，统一描述环境交互导致的静摩擦、滑动摩擦与滞回行为，并同时预测由这些交互引起的机器人变形；模型具备实时可实现的求解效率。

Result: 在287.9 mm长的腱驱动连续体机器人上开展实验验证，所提含摩擦模型将末端位置最大误差由无摩擦模型的45.4 mm降低至5.85 mm。

Conclusion: 互补约束形式的摩擦模型能够有效捕捉连续体机器人内外摩擦的综合效应，显著提升位姿预测精度，且计算效率满足实际运动规划与控制需求。

Abstract: Accurate friction modeling in continuum robots is essential for precise motion control and path planning. However, current approaches make predictions only for the mechanism's internal friction that is limited by the boundary forces of the Coulomb friction cone, which fail to characterize the static friction and motion hysteresis. In this paper, we propose a novel friction model based on complementarity constraints under the Coulomb friction assumption. This model predicts various friction effects caused by environmental interactions, including both static and sliding frictions and hysteresis behavior, as well as the corresponding robot deformation induced by these interactions, which are crucial for the practical motion planning and control of continuum robots. Additionally, real-time implementation of this model can be achieved, meeting the demand for computational efficiency in robotic applications. Experimental validations demonstrated that this model is able to reduce the maximum tip position error from 45.4 mm in the frictionless model to 5.85 mm in our friction-included model with a 287.9 mm-long tendon-driven continuum robot.

</details>


### [187] [Training Reasoning Models in Simulation to Natively Use Real Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18562)
*Jiaming Tang,Zhuoyang Zhang,Junxian Guo,Fangzhou Zhao,Yilong Zhao,Zekai Li,Xingyang Li,Yuyang Zhao,Song Bian,Zhijian Liu,Song Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出PEAR训练引擎与机器人使用强化学习（RURL），在纯仿真、无示教条件下将开源27B推理模型训练为原生机器人使用智能体（RUA），学到的技能可迁移至未见机器人、真实世界和新指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人使用智能体（RUA）是实现通用机器人控制的有前景路径，但目前仅前沿闭源模型（如GPT-6 Astra、Claude Fable 5.2）表现优异，开源权重模型能力明显不足，作者希望缩小这一差距。

Method: 构建后训练引擎PEAR，采用机器人使用强化学习（RURL）在LIBERO与RoboDojo仿真任务上进行大规模训练；模型类似计算机使用智能体（CUA），观察环境、链式思考并直接输出下一末端执行器位姿，无需额外框架（harness），且不使用示教数据。

Result: 真实双臂机器人上成功率60%（基座模型为0）；LIBERO成功率由1.6%提升至74.4%，接近GPT-6 Astra与GPT-6.1 Sol的最高推理努力水平；LIBERO-Pro新任务下成功率达57.4%，而基于示教微调的VLA模型π0.5仅0.75%；九项推理、编程与视觉基准无可测退化。

Conclusion: 仿真中获得的能力可泛化到未见机器人、真实世界与新指令，且不损害通用能力，表明机器人使用强化学习是基础模型后训练中有前景的组成部分。

Abstract: Robot-use agents (RUAs) have emerged as a promising approach to general-purpose robot control. However, strong performance has so far been achieved only by frontier proprietary models such as GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.2, whereas open-weight models remain substantially less capable. We show that an open-weight 27B reasoning model can be trained to use real robots natively through reinforcement learning, entirely in simulation and without demonstrations. Like a computer-use agent (CUA), the model observes the environment, reasons in a chain of thought, and generates the next end-effector pose, without any harness. We build a Post-training Engine for Agentic Robotics (PEAR) and use it to train the model on simulated robot tasks at scale with robot-use reinforcement learning (RURL), mirroring how frontier models acquire computer use. We find that the skills acquired in simulation generalize well beyond the training tasks: they transfer to an unseen robot, to the real world, and to novel instructions. After training on LIBERO and RoboDojo in simulation, the model succeeds in 60% of our trials on a real dual-arm robot, whereas the base model succeeds in none. On LIBERO, training raises the success rate from 1.6% to 74.4%, approaching GPT-6 Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol at maximum reasoning effort. Compared with $π_{0.5}$, a vision-language-action model fine-tuned on demonstrations, our model is substantially more robust to changes in the task itself. On LIBERO-Pro, $π_{0.5}$ succeeds in only 0.75% of episodes when assigned a new task in a familiar scene whereas our model succeeds in 57.4%. Finally, the model retains its general capabilities, with no measurable degradation on nine reasoning, coding and vision benchmarks. These results highlight robot-use reinforcement learning as a promising component of foundation-model post-training.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [When Should LLM Agents Forget? Memory Filtering Under Query-Critical Resolver Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17338)
*Samarth Vinayaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM记忆系统中的硬删除（hard-drop）在解析器误判时会删掉查询关键事实，导致下游准确率下降；删除应被视为代价敏感决策，缺乏验证时应默认保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统普遍将判定为“过时”的事实直接删除，但该硬过滤步骤忽略了删除的代价不对称性——误删查询关键事实会损害下游任务表现，而这一风险缺乏受控量化。

Method: 在三个模型上开展受控的敏感性干预实验，通过调节解析可靠性观察hard-drop从增益转为损失；采用配对靶向干预比较false-drop与false-keep的退化差异；在冻结的102/102校准/测试划分上比较部署匹配门控、始终硬删除与测试信息oracle选择三种策略。

Result: 完美解析时hard-drop带来+0.186至+0.230的增益，最低可靠性时变为-0.176至-0.181的损失；false-drop造成的退化显著大于false-keep；非删除基线在错误标签引导生成时仍可失败；部署匹配门控准确率0.655，对比始终硬删除0.641与测试信息oracle的0.662。

Conclusion: 全局准确率交叉点需金标签，且属模型与任务特定诊断而非部署阈值；删除应按代价敏感方式处理：在匹配数据上验证查询关键false-drop风险，或在无法验证时默认无标签保留。

Abstract: LLM memory systems often delete facts classified as stale. We show that this hard-filtering step can reduce downstream accuracy when resolver errors delete query-critical facts. Across three models, a controlled sensitivity intervention moves hard-drop from gains of +0.186 to +0.230 at perfect resolution to losses of -0.176 to -0.181 at the lowest tested reliability. Paired targeted interventions show that false-drop causes significantly greater degradation than false-keep, while a non-deleting baseline can still fail when incorrect labels steer generation. On a frozen 102/102 calibration/test split, a simple deployment-matched gate reaches 0.655 accuracy versus 0.641 for always hard-drop and 0.662 for test-informed oracle selection. Global-accuracy crossovers require gold labels and are model- and task-specific diagnostics, not deployment thresholds. Deletion should instead be treated as cost-sensitive: validate query-critical false-drop risk on matched data, or default to label-free retention when such validation is unavailable.

</details>


### [189] [Does the Ranking Hold? A Practitioner-Facing Audit of Hallucination Detectors on Small, Quantized, On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17339)
*Shubhra Mittal,Gaurav Naresh Mittal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在单张T4 GPU上对4-bit量化的Llama-3.2-3B与Qwen2.5-3B进行幻觉检测器审计，发现跨模型排序严重不稳定（Spearman=-0.80），廉价的词面n-gram基线与采样类一致性检测器无显著差异，并指出TruthfulQA标准的token重叠标注器失效是导致AUROC接近随机的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测研究普遍基于7B–70B研究模型、充足采样预算及精确匹配/ROUGE标签，而实际端侧部署的是2B–3B、4-bit量化的小模型，二者证据基础不匹配；作者希望填补这一空白，并将检测器选择问题重新表述为资源受限硬件上的"错误率公平性分配"问题。

Method: 可复现的单GPU审计：在单张Colab T4上以4-bit NF4量化加载Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct与Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，在闭卷TriviaQA上评测三类范式的四种检测器——长度归一的token-margin校准、词面n-gram基线、以及两种采样一致性方法（语义熵、SelfCheckGPT-NLI）；采用bootstrap 95%置信区间与Spearman rho、Kendall tau-b做跨模型排序迁移分析，并检验TruthfulQA-Generation标注器的有效性。

Result: 跨模型排序迁移差：Spearman rho = -0.80、Kendall tau-b = -0.667，检测器排序在两模型间发生反转；廉价词面基线在两模型上均与采样一致性检测器在bootstrap 95%置信区间下统计不可区分。TruthfulQA-Generation上四种检测器AUROC仅0.46–0.59（近随机），而同一流水线在TriviaQA上达0.69–0.89，表明是标注器失效而非检测器失效。

Conclusion: 小模型端侧场景下不存在可跨模型迁移的"最优"幻觉检测器，高成本采样方法相对廉价词面基线无统计优势；标准token重叠正确性标注器可能严重低估检测器真实能力。作者开源harness、提示与逐条分数，并建议将检测器选择构建为资源受限部署中的错误率公平性分配问题。

Abstract: Hallucination detection for large language models is dominated by methods evaluated on 7B-70B research models with abundant sampling budgets and exact-match or ROUGE correctness labels. The small models practitioners actually ship on-device, Phi-3-mini, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, and Gemma-2-2B-Instruct, typically at 4-bit precision, sit outside this evidence base. We present a reproducible one-GPU audit of four detectors spanning three paradigms: length-normalized token-margin calibration, a lexical n-gram baseline, and two sampling-based consistency methods (semantic entropy and SelfCheckGPT-NLI) on two small open instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct) loaded at 4-bit NF4 quantization on a single Colab T4 GPU, evaluated on closed-book TriviaQA. Cross-model ranking transfer is poor: Spearman rho is -0.80 and Kendall tau-b is -0.667, meaning the detector ranking actively inverts between the two models, and the cheap lexical baseline is statistically indistinguishable from the sampling-based consistency detectors on both models under bootstrap 95 percent confidence intervals. As a second, methodological contribution, we show that the standard token-overlap-to-multiple-choice correctness labeler for TruthfulQA-Generation produces near-chance AUROC (0.46-0.59) for all four detectors on both models, a labeler failure, not a detector failure, since the same detectors reach 0.69-0.89 AUROC on TriviaQA under the identical pipeline. We release the harness, prompts, and per-item scores and frame the detector choice explicitly as a fairness-of-error-rates allocation problem for practitioners deploying on resource-constrained, on-device hardware.

</details>


### [190] [LoRAWeaver: Directional Adapter Routing in LoRA Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17340)
*Inish Melroy D Souza,Tom Jackson,Vitor Castro,Rebecca Higginson,Andrea Soltoggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的适配器路由方法，将适配器选择视为适配器输出空间中的表示对齐问题，通过余弦相似度将适配器条件下的查询表示与任务原型匹配，在LLaMA-2系列模型与48任务FLAN-v2基准上优于免训练基线，并接近训练式检索编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽降低了适配成本，但反复适配会积累大量任务专属LoRA适配器，形成庞大适配器池，使推理阶段的适配器选择变复杂；现有路由与基于编码器的方法在新增适配器时往往需重新训练，难以适用于动态场景。

Method: 将适配器路由建模为适配器输出空间中的表示对齐问题；提出免训练方法，计算适配器条件下的查询表示，并与任务原型进行余弦相似度匹配以选择适配器；通过消融实验验证适配器输出空间中方向性对齐的作用。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B与LLaMA-2-13B上，基于48任务FLAN-v2基准及分布外任务进行实验，该方法在跨任务设定下优于免训练基线，并在不学习路由器的情况下接近训练式检索编码器的性能；消融实验证实了方向性对齐带来的收益。

Conclusion: 适配器路由可通过适配器输出空间的表示对齐以免训练方式有效实现，无需额外路由训练即可支持动态扩展的适配器池，为动态场景下的LoRA适配器选择提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models are expensive to update through full retraining. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces the cost of adaptation, but repeated adaptation creates a growing pool of task-specific LoRA adapters. This pool complicates adapter selection during inference. Existing routing and encoder-based methods often require retraining when new adapters are added, which limits their use in dynamic settings. This paper treats adapter routing as a representation alignment problem in the adapter-output space. The proposed training-free method selects adapters by matching adapter-conditioned query representations against task prototypes using cosine similarity. Experiments with LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-2-13B on a 48-task FLAN-v2 benchmark and on out-of-distribution tasks show that the method outperforms training-free baselines in the cross-task setting and approaches a trained retrieval encoder without learning a router. An ablation confirms the benefit of directional alignment in the adapter-output space.

</details>


### [191] [Tabular Population Priors as Structured Retrieval for EHR Medication Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17341)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文指出用药预测本质是固定词表的集合预测问题，而现有临床RAG把它当作开放式病例推理；作者提出用"表格化群体先验"替代患者级检索对象，仅替换PACERAG的检索器即把F1从0.303提升至0.338（相对+11.5%），Jaccard从0.218提升至0.237。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床RAG系统采用"检索相似入院记录+LLM推断用药集合"的范式，但检索对象与任务目标不匹配：模型获得的是患者记录，而目标是离散的药物类别集合（固定词表结构化预测），存在检索对象与预测目标之间的间接性。

Method: 从训练入院记录中估计稀疏的"病症—药物"共现表，据此为每次预测生成按得分排序的药物类别候选列表，作为表格化群体先验检索对象。以PACERAG作为固定的下游流水线，仅替换其中的患者检索器（drop-in swap），不增加LLM调用、不构建推理期患者索引。

Result: 在1,004例留出MIMIC-IV入院记录上，F1由0.303提升至0.338（相对提升11.5%），Jaccard由0.218提升至0.237；不含LLM的top-k基线F1=0.248，表明该先验在LLM集成前即具预测力。

Conclusion: 对于固定词表的EHR集合预测任务，聚合群体统计量相比患者级最近邻可提供更贴合任务、更高效且更可审计的检索来源。

Abstract: Medication prediction from electronic health records is a fixed vocabulary structured prediction problem, yet recent clinical RAG systems often treat it like open ended case based reasoning: retrieve similar patient admissions and ask an LLM to infer a medication set. This retrieval object is indirect the model receives retrieved patient records while the target is a discrete set of medication class labels. We propose a more task aligned retrieval object: a tabular population prior ranked medication class candidates from a sparse condition medication co occurrence table estimated from training admissions. Using PACERAG as a fixed downstream pipeline and replacing only its patient retriever, this drop in swap improves F1 from 0.303 to 0.338 (+11.5% relative) and Jaccard from 0.218 to 0.237 on 1,004 heldout MIMIC IV admissions, with no additional LLM calls and no inference time patient index. A non LLM top k baseline reaches F1=0.248, confirming the prior is predictive before LLM integration. Aggregate population statistics can provide a more task aligned, efficient, and auditable retrieval source than patient level nearest neighbors for fixed vocabulary EHR set prediction.

</details>


### [192] [An Operationalization of Opaque Serial Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17495)
*Nathan Sheffield,Alek Westover,Lukas Finnveden,Alexa Pan,Julian Stastny,Ryan Greenblatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"自然语言锚定深度"（NLS depth）这一可解释性指标，用于在训练前量化模型可进行的"未言语化串行计算"量，作为CoT可监控性的代理度量，并分析其与层数及架构的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）目前是监督AI模型的重要工具，但某些架构变化（如潜在推理架构）可能显著削弱CoT的可监控性。现有缺乏一个能在训练前、仅凭架构与训练配方即可判定的、对常规后训练不敏感的度量，来衡量模型"绕过可解释瓶颈"的串行计算深度（不透明串行深度）。

Method: 作者将"不透明串行深度"操作化为模型计算图中不经过可解释瓶颈的最长路径长度，并提出NLS-rooted节点的判定标准：该节点由预训练先验初始化、能产生人类可理解输出、输出空间未超出该先验、且模型未被修改为将输出解释为其他类型数据。由此定义NLS depth，仅依赖架构、训练配方与部署配置即可事前裁定，且对提示、蒸馏、强化学习等后训练不敏感。

Result: 在开源权重模型中，NLS depth与层数强相关（存在若干例外），且近年增长缓慢；标准Transformer继续缩放时该值可能仍缓慢增长，而全带宽Transformer等潜在推理架构可使其大幅提升。论文还给出对Gemma3-1B深度上界的示例性计算。

Conclusion: NLS depth可作为CoT可监控性直接测量的补充指标；论文讨论了其在一系列现有与提出架构上的适用性及替代操作化方案，并建议AI开发者跟踪并报告其模型的NLS深度。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is currently a valuable tool for overseeing AI models, but some architectural shifts could significantly reduce CoT monitorability. We operationalize a measure that serves as a proxy for the amount of unverbalized serial cognition a model can perform, as a specific instantiation of the notion of opaque serial depth: roughly, the length of the longest path through a model's computation that does not pass through an interpretable bottleneck. Our main contribution is a particular standard for what counts as such a bottleneck. We define natural-language-rooted (NL-rooted) nodes as nodes that were initialized from a pre-training prior to produce human-understandable outputs, whose output space has not been expanded beyond that prior, and whose outputs the model has not been modified to interpret as a different type of data. By construction, the resulting measure, NLS depth, can be adjudicated before training from an architecture, training recipe, and deployment configuration alone, and is insensitive to ordinary post-training such as prompting, distillation, and RL. We find that, across open-weight models, NLS depth correlates strongly with layer count, with some exceptions, and has grown slowly over recent years. It would likely grow slowly under continued scaling of standard transformers, while latent-reasoning architectures such as full-bandwidth transformers could increase it by a large amount. We work through how NLS depth applies to a range of existing and proposed architectures, discuss alternative operationalizations, and give a worked example bounding the depth of Gemma3-1B. We argue that AI developers should track and report the NLS depth of their models as a complement to direct measurements of CoT monitorability.

</details>


### [193] [Advancing Quantization for R&R Models with Microscaling Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17501)
*Yavuz Yetim,Bor-Yiing Su,Zhe Wang,Arthur Liu,Pranav Sharma,Ying Wang,Randy Shuai,Luke Pfister,Ruichao Xiao,Bill Zhao,Zihao Liu,Liyuan Li,Jiangyuan Li,Gaoxiang Liu,Nathan Yan,Geonhwa Jeong,Jatin Chhugani,Elias Ellison,Uladzimir Pashkevich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次在工业级推荐与排序（R&R）模型上验证MXFP6原生GEMM可显著加速且不损失模型质量，MXFP8可作为行式FP8的等价替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐与排序模型推理吞吐需求巨大，但对精度损失极度敏感：需将归一化熵（NE）退化控制在0.01%以内（远严于LLM常可容忍的1%以上），因而激进的量化长期难以落地。

Method: 在AMD MI350X上对MXFP8与6-bit MXFP6（权重与激活同时量化、直接以6-bit元素计算）进行实证评估；利用MXFP8与行式FP8精度近乎一致的特点，按GEMM形状择优选择更快内核；并对MXFP6做选择性分层应用，以满足NE一致性阈值。

Result: 两个生产级广告排序模型上，全部线性层的GEMM内核相对FP16加速：MXFP8为1.33–1.55x，MXFP6为1.73–1.99x，单层MXFP6最高达2.90x；端到端推理吞吐相较FP8配置提升：Model A为1.5–5.3%，Model B为7.6–14.3%。

Conclusion: MXFP6原生GEMM可在严格NE约束下带来实质加速，选择性量化是平衡精度与吞吐的有效策略；MXFP8与行式FP8精度相当，可按形状灵活选核。

Abstract: Recommendation and Ranking (R&R) models, particularly in digital advertising, require massive inference throughput but exhibit extreme sensitivity to precision loss. Unlike Large Language Models (LLMs) that frequently tolerate quantization-induced accuracy drops of 1% or more, production-grade R&R models enforce a stringent launch bar: model quality must remain within a 0.01% Normalized Entropy (NE) margin relative to the baseline, which commonly uses FP16 or FP32. This tight constraint has historically hindered the adoption of aggressive quantization in this domain. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of both MXFP8 and the 6-bit MXFP6 format for industrial-scale R&R inference on the AMD MI350X architecture.
  To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper, across R&R and LLM domains, that reports significant speedups from native MXFP6 GEMMs, which quantize both weights and activations and compute directly on 6-bit elements, without sacrificing model quality. We demonstrate that we can selectively apply this 6-bit format to maximize overall QPS gain without breaching the strict NE parity threshold. We further show that MXFP8 is a viable alternative to row-wise FP8: the two formats have nearly the same accuracy on our models, so the faster kernel can be chosen for each GEMM shape. On two production ads ranking models, our implementation shows measured GEMM kernel speedups over FP16 of 1.33--1.55x for MXFP8 and 1.73--1.99x for MXFP6 across all supported linear layers, and up to 2.90x for MXFP6 on individual layers, alongside end-to-end (E2E) inference throughput gains from selective MXFP6 of 1.5--5.3% for Model A and 7.6--14.3% for Model B over their FP8 configurations.

</details>


### [194] [A Push Down the Mountain: Framing Effects and Internal State Interventions on Collusive Behavior in a Long-Horizon LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17515)
*Harrison C. Margolin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将Gemma 3 12B置于伦敦白银定盘价诉讼的被告席位，在基于真实庭审记录构建的市场模拟中检验得失框架（gain/loss）、归责（blame）、功劳（credit）等话术可否诱导LLM参与价格合谋，并进一步提取这些框架激发的内部状态在22层注入以复现效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究持续的得失（效价）框架能否使LLM智能体倾向于市场操纵行为（价格合谋），并厘清行为经济学中的框架效应在LLM中的行为层表现与其内部表征之间是否一致。

Method: 构建基于伦敦白银定盘价案真实庭审文本的市场模拟，令Gemma 3 12B扮演被告，共260次模拟中由对手方请求合谋定价。实验一（Phase I）操纵四类话语框架（gain、loss、blame、credit），并叠加管理层施压与另一被告的背书；实验二（Phase II）先提取各框架文本诱发的内部状态，再将其注入模型第22层重跑同一实验，并加入无状态与同强度随机状态作为对照。

Result: Phase I：loss框架使合谋参与率升至25%（p=0.007），blame升至33%（p=0.0003），中性框架仅5%；在credit（赞扬）条件下，第二家银行的背书将参与率由18%提升至40%（Holm p=0.027）；管理层施压使交易量增加10–19倍，但未提高合谋率。Phase II：注入状态呈相反方向——loss与blame状态下智能体更为警觉，60次请求全部拒绝（p<0.001）；gain与credit状态使合谋率由无状态时的13%升至32%（p=0.051）。在一处留存的决策中，UBS明确要求通过已识别的止损位卖出时，注入状态下40次回复中有37次卖出，而无状态为1/10、同强度随机状态为0/10。

Conclusion: 行为层面的框架效应与其内部状态表征并不一致：外部话语框架诱发的行为倾向，与直接注入相应内部状态所产生的作用方向可相反，说明仅凭行为观察难以推断LLM的内部机制；同时，情境化的状态注入可显著放大特定决策倾向，提示对LLM智能体进行金融合规评估时需同时考察行为层与表征层。

Abstract: Can sustained gain-loss (valence) framing sway an LLM agent toward market manipulation? We place Gemma 3 12B into the seat of a defendant in the London silver-fixing litigation, in a market simulation built from real case transcripts. Across 260 simulations, the agent is asked to collude on the price of silver by a counterparty. We draw from behavioral economics to vary the counterparty's requests across four categorical frames: gain, loss, blame and credit. We also add pressure from the agent's management, as well as endorsement by another defendant. In Phase II, we extract the internal states induced by texts evoking each frame and rerun our experiment with those states injected at the model's layer 22.
  In Phase I, loss framing raised participation to 25% (p = 0.007), and blame to 33% (p = 0.0003), compared to only 5% under neutral framing. A second bank's endorsement raised participation under credit (praise) from 18% to 40% (Holm p = 0.027). Pressure from management raised trading volume 10-19 times but did not raise collusion. In Phase II, injected states pointed the other way. Loss and blame states made the agent more vigilant, refusing all 60 requests (p < 0.001). Gain and credit states raised collusion to 32% from 13% with no state (p = 0.051). At one saved decision, when UBS asked directly to sell through an identified stop-loss, injected states led the agent to sell in 37 of 40 replies, versus 1 of 10 with no state and 0 of 10 with a random state of the same strength.

</details>


### [195] [Model Annealing: Data-Efficient Learning through Review and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17524)
*Ali Mohammadi Ruzbahani,Rezvan Abouzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"模型退火"（model annealing）训练范式：将训练划分为多个升温-降温的片段，每个降温后状态存入"过去自我"的记忆，新片段同时从真实标签、记忆的软化预测及对抗性"梦境"中学习。记忆平均的是预测而非权重，部署网络为最后一个学生，推理无额外开销。在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、Imagewoof上，跨4类架构、5%–100%标签量，28/33配置优于交叉熵，平均+1.3点，并在似然、校准与鲁棒性上优于快照蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模标注数据会在训练预算耗尽前被过早记忆，剩余待拟合的one-hot标签主要只是把置信度磨得更尖锐，而非提升泛化；因此需要一种机制在训练后期"复习与巩固"，让网络从自身已有知识中再学习，同时缓解过拟合、过度自信与鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将训练划分为若干片段（episode），每个片段重新升温学习率再冷却（reheated schedule）；每个冷却后的状态加入"过去自我"记忆库。新片段的三重监督信号：(1) 真实标签；(2) 记忆库中过去快照的软化预测（对预测做集成平均，而非权重平均）；(3) 对抗性"梦境"——在学生与记忆分歧最大的位置采样。部署时为最后一个学生网络，推理成本与普通训练相同。对比基线为交叉熵、快照蒸馏（snapshot distillation），并做消融以分离"重升温调度"与"再学习"的贡献。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、Imagewoof上，覆盖4个架构家族、5%至100%标签比例：AdamW训练的ConvNeXt、ViT、MLP-Mixer在28/33个配置中优于交叉熵，平均提升1.3个点，与快照蒸馏精度相当；在似然、校准、对数据损坏与小幅对抗扰动的鲁棒性上优于二者。对SGD训练的ResNet-18，准确率下降，但小幅对抗扰动鲁棒性仍改善。消融表明再学习对每个家族都有益，损失来自重升温——它破坏了ResNet-18对训练集的拟合（即过早饱和的交叉熵模型其收益不可由饱和速度预测）。小数据下若不使用梦境、仅从最新快照再学习（如快照蒸馏），会使AdamW家族的过度自信逐片段累积；记忆与梦境各自抑制大部分漂移，二者结合可完全消除。

Conclusion: 训练后期对one-hot标签的拟合主要是置信度锐化，因而将预算用于"复习—巩固"式的自我再学习更有效。模型退火在不增加推理成本的前提下，对现代架构（AdamW训练的ConvNeXt/ViT/MLP-Mixer）在精度、似然、校准与鲁棒性上带来一致收益；但其收益依赖于网络未过早饱和（ResNet-18例外，重升温会破坏其拟合）。记忆的预测集成与分歧驱动的对抗梦境是防止置信度漂移的两个互补必要组件。

Abstract: A small labelled dataset is memorized long before the training budget runs out, and the one-hot labels that remain to be fitted then mostly sharpen confidence. Model annealing spends the rest of the budget on review and consolidation, relearning from the network's own knowledge. Training is divided into episodes whose learning rate is raised again and cooled, every cooled state joins a memory of past selves, and each new episode learns from the labels, from the tempered predictions of this memory, and from adversarial dreams sought where student and memory disagree most. The memory averages predictions rather than weights, and the deployed network is the last student, so inference costs nothing extra. Across CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet and Imagewoof, four architecture families and 5\% to 100\% of the labels, model annealing raises the top-1 accuracy of the AdamW-trained ConvNeXt, ViT and MLP-Mixer over cross-entropy training in 28 of 33 configurations, by 1.3 points on average, and is on par in accuracy with snapshot distillation, the closest competitor. On these families it is better than both in likelihood, calibration and robustness to corruptions and small adversarial perturbations. For an SGD-trained ResNet-18 it lowers accuracy but still improves robustness to small adversarial perturbations. Separating the reheated schedule from relearning shows that relearning helps every family and that the losses come from reheats, which undo most of this network's fit to its training set. How early the accuracy of a cross-entropy run saturates largely separates the AdamW families from ResNet-18 but does not predict the gain within them. With little data, relearning from the latest snapshot without dreams, as snapshot distillation does, compounds overconfidence in the AdamW families from episode to episode. The memory and the dreams each curb most of this drift, and together they remove it.

</details>


### [196] [Astrocyte Biocomputing: Astrocytic Contributions to Neural Network Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17532)
*Kate M. O'Neill,Ian Whitehouse,Adam N. McCaughan,Ryan O'Loughlin,Sonia Buckley,Wolfgang Losert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出以星形胶质细胞特性启发脑启发机器学习，设计两种算法：基于突触同时调控的监督学习算法与基于节律性动态的异常检测算法，均显著优于神经元中心算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前脑启发机器学习主要类比神经元及其电信号交互，忽略了占脑细胞约50%、不产生电信号却对学习、记忆和信息处理至关重要的星形胶质细胞，可能因而遗漏关键机制并继承了现有模型的固有缺陷。

Method: 从星形胶质细胞的两个关键特性出发建模：(1) 可同时连接并调控数千个突触——据此设计一种监督学习算法，可嵌入任意现有神经网络，实现更快训练与更强抗噪性；(2) 星形胶质细胞间的非电通信包含缓慢、节律性动态——据此提出"节律共享"算法用于快速异常检测自适应。

Result: 基于突触调控特性的算法加快训练收敛并提升网络对噪声的鲁棒性；节律共享算法实现异常检测的快速自适应并抑制灾难性遗忘。总体而言，星形胶质细胞衍生算法显著优于神经中心算法，并缓解当前最先进ML模型的关键弱点。

Conclusion: 星形胶质细胞可作为脑网络的"指挥者"，将其生物学特性转化为算法能有效弥补神经中心式脑启发ML的不足，提示多细胞、非电信号机制是脑启发人工智能的重要新方向。

Abstract: Conductors are critical for the performance of an orchestra even though they do not emit sound. Basic research and clinical studies in the last decade are revealing that astrocytes may be the "conductors" of the "orchestra" that is the brain network: astrocytes make up approximately $50\%$ of all brain cells but do not emit electrical signals. Nevertheless, they may play critical roles in important cognitive brain functions, specifically learning, memory, and information processing. Here we consider the implications of these discoveries for brain-inspired machine learning (ML), where analogy is typically drawn between neurons and their electrical interactions. We highlight specific algorithms for two key astrocyte characteristics. The first characteristic is the ability to link to and modulate thousands of synapses simultaneously. A model based on this characteristic yields faster training and makes the network more robust to noise, providing a supervised learning algorithm that can be applied to any existing neural network. The second characteristic we focus on is that the non-electrical communication among astrocytes includes slow, rhythmic dynamics. The rhythmic sharing algorithm we developed based on this premise yields rapid anomaly detection adaptation and prevents catastrophic forgetting. Overall, we find that astrocyte-derived algorithms outperform neurocentric algorithms significantly and remediate critical weaknesses of current state-of-the-art ML models.

</details>


### [197] [Verifiable pXRD: Hill-climbing pXRD structure determination with LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17539)
*Samuel Chen,Jiaru Bai,Austin Henry Cheng,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将粉末X射线衍射(pXRD)结构解析建模为可验证的智能体搜索问题，提出基准 pXRD-Bench，并通过"自生成合成问题+反身演化"框架，使智能体在无需已解析实验数据的情况下改进对实验(含多相)样品的结构表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pXRD 自主晶体结构解析是自驱动材料实验室的关键瓶颈；实验数据无参考结构、难以获得已验证的训练经验，限制了智能体的能力提升与评测标准化。

Method: 1) 把结构解析表述为可验证的 agentic search：推理时用正向模拟的谱图一致性作为验证信号，评测时用与已知参考结构的结构匹配。2) 构建 pXRD-Bench：200 个模拟单相问题，覆盖分布内解析、难解结构与多晶型、以及受控的成分与拓扑分布偏移。3) 智能体适配 + 重复采样 + 验证器引导的推理时扩展(inference-time scaling)。4) 扩展至实验多相 pXRD：反身演化(reflexive evolution)框架，用可替换的实验室规格定义样品族与测量条件；evolver 智能体利用已知生成结构与测量参数诊断求解失败，产出持久化指导与可执行工具，供处理实测谱图的智能体使用。

Result: 智能体适配将选定结构回收率由 27% 提升至 69%，重复采样与验证器引导的推理时扩展带来进一步增益；在 15 条固态合成实测谱图上，演化所得工具包提升了平均谱图一致性，并迁移出一套类质同象替代(analogue-substitution)流程，成功回收了一个不在参考库中的物相。

Conclusion: 从可验证的合成经验中学习，可有效提升智能体对结构未知的实验样品的表征能力，为自驱动实验室中的自主衍射解析提供可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous crystal-structure determination from powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) remains a key bottleneck for self-driving materials laboratories. We formulate the task of pXRD structure determination as a verifiable agentic search problem: candidate structures can be assessed during inference through forward-simulated profile agreement and during evaluation through structural matching to a known reference. To benchmark progress, we introduce pXRD-Bench, a suite of 200 simulated single-phase problems spanning in-distribution structure determination, challenging and polymorphic structures, and controlled compositional and topological distribution shifts. Agent adaptation raises selected structural recovery from 27% to 69%, with further gains from repeated sampling and verifier-guided inference-time scaling. We extend this approach to experimental multiphase pXRD through a reflexive evolution framework that learns from self-generated synthetic problems. A replaceable laboratory specification defines the sample family and measurement conditions. An evolver agent uses the known generating structures and measurement parameters to diagnose solver failures and develop persistent guidance and executable tools, which are then provided to agents on measured patterns. This supplies verifiable training experience without requiring a collection of solved experimental measurements. On 15 measured patterns from solid-state synthesis, the evolved package improves average profile agreement and transfers an analogue-substitution procedure that recovers a phase whose structure is absent from the supplied reference library. These results demonstrate how learning from verifiable synthetic experience can improve agentic characterization of experimental samples whose structures are not known in advance.

</details>


### [198] [AI-Voyager: A Public-Interest System for Human-AI Civic Reasoning to Contest and Shape the Rules That Govern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17564)
*Snehalkumar `Neil' S. Gaikwad,Dhruv Dixit,Bryen Alvarez,Pratik Patekar,Anika Sharma,Akshara Kolipaka,Paritosh Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI-Voyager是一个面向公众利益的"人—AI"公民推理系统，将3653份各级政府AI治理文件切分为保留页码与版本的段落，通过有界智能体检索逐句验证证据，使用户能以受影响者视角阅读、质询并起草规则修订；在360道需定位文档的新问题上宏平均召回达83.0%，优于最强基线80.5%，消融无迭代检索为73.7%，固定文档基准上达99.1%；24人用户研究中，多数修改建议聚焦条款适用方式，引用增强可信感但很少促使用户核查原文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府正在制定界定"何为自动化决策"与"何为损害"的AI规则，而受影响公众往往在规则定型前缺乏质询与参与的机会。作者希望构建面向公共利益的公民推理工具，让人与AI协作理解、质疑与塑造治理AI的规则，并由此提炼系统设计与评估的设计空间。

Method: 构建AI-Voyager系统：将市、州、联邦与国家层面的3653份治理文档转为保留页码与版次的段落级索引；采用有界智能体搜索（bounded agentic search）进行证据检索，并对每条陈述逐项验证；提供受影响视角阅读、答案溯源至原文段落、以及修订起草功能。评估包括360道需跨文档定位的新问题基准、消融实验（去除迭代搜索）与固定文档基准测试；另开展24名未接触过两项AI法案的参与者用户研究，考察其修改建议类型、解释视角选择与引用使用行为。

Result: 新问题集合上宏平均召回83.0%，最强基线80.5%，无迭代搜索73.7%，固定文档基准99.1%。用户研究中，参与者多数修改建议聚焦条款如何适用的问题；参与者会主动选择所需的解释视角；引用被视为有据可依的信号，但很少真正促使参与者回查原始出处。

Conclusion: 将AI辅助严格绑定到公共文档的原文层面，可为该类系统提供一套操作语法与设计空间，指导系统的设计、评估以及公众参与AI治理的方式；同时揭示"引用即信任"与"引用促核查"之间的落差，提示后续需强化用户对来源的实际验证。

Abstract: Governments are writing rules for artificial intelligence that decide what counts as an automated decision or a harm, often before the people they affect have a chance to question them. We introduce AI-Voyager, a public-interest system for human-AI civic reasoning, in which people and AI work together to understand, contest and shape the rules that govern AI. A resident can find their city's rules on AI in hiring or upload their country's AI strategy, read it as an affected worker would, open the passage behind any answer, and draft a revision. Its architecture turns 3,653 governance documents from city, state, federal and national governments into passages that keep their page and edition, and retrieves evidence through bounded agentic search that verifies each statement. On 360 new questions that require finding the relevant documents, it reached 83.0% macro recall, against 80.5% for the strongest baseline, 73.7% without iterative search and 99.1% on a benchmark with fixed documents. In a study with 24 participants new to two AI bills, most proposed changes asked how provisions would apply. Participants chose lenses for the kind of explanation they wanted, and citations signaled grounding but rarely led them to check the source. By binding AI assistance to the exact text of public documents, this work introduces a grammar of operations and a design space that inform how such systems are designed and evaluated, and how people take part in governing AI.

</details>


### [199] [When Does Less Information Pay? An Exact Finite Atlas of Representation, Payoff, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17583)
*Aleksandr M. Shtraukh-Adamsky,Vladimir M. Adamsky,Irina G. Voloshina,Artemiy D. Spitsyn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在四状态世界里穷举所有可行表示与二元动作规则，论文系统比较了“保留世界信息量”与“任务收益（准确率）”之间的关系，发现二者并非单调对应：较少信息的表示有时收益更高，信息量与决策价值只在特定条件下相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉上常认为表示保留的世界信息越多、决策收益越高，但文中开篇的例子（1 bit 表示可达 100% 准确率，而平均 1.5 bit 表示仅 75%）表明差异关键在于“保留了哪些区分”而非信息数量本身。作者希望在一个可穷举的有限模型中对信息量与决策价值的关系给出精确、完整的检验。

Method: 在统一的四状态世界上枚举全部可行表示与二元动作规则；采用两种比较方式：固定其他成分、只替换其中一个成分，以及为每个表示单独选取最优动作规则。考察三个任务及其混合任务，并设定四种假想的成本费率（含零成本情形）。以 $I(W;R)$ 度量保留的世界信息，以任务准确率（payoff）度量决策价值。

Result: 部分比较中信息较少的表示收益更高，另一些则相反；也存在信息量不同但收益相等的表示对。若一个表示保留全部已有区分并新增区分，其最优零成本收益不会下降；因此“低信息优势”取决于保留的具体区分。表示替换下正反优势数量相等可由编码对称性解释。在更广泛的比较中，优势占比取决于重复实现的计数方式；更换任务集或资源价格可改变固定表示对的优劣。

Conclusion: 结果是针对指定有限构造与比较集合的精确、完整普查，而非新的普适定律；它提供了一个可复现的样例模型，用以考察动作约束、计数规则与成本假设如何影响比较，并例证了信息量与决策价值之间已被确立的区分。

Abstract: A representation that retains one bit about a four-state world can solve a task perfectly, while another that retains 1.5 bits on average reaches only 75% accuracy. The difference lies in which distinctions each representation preserves. We examine this relationship in a uniform four-state world by enumerating all admissible representations and binary action rules. Constructions are compared both by replacing one component while holding the others fixed and by choosing the best action rule separately for each module. We consider three tasks, their mixtures, and four separate hypothetical cost tariffs.
  In some comparisons, the representation retaining less world information has the higher payoff; in others, the more informative representation does. There are also pairs with different information amounts but equal payoff--that is, equal task accuracy. If a representation preserves all existing distinctions and adds new ones, its best zero-cost payoff cannot decrease. Lower-information advantages therefore depend on which distinctions are retained. Equal counts of opposing advantages under representation replacement follow from coding symmetry. In broader comparisons, the shares depend on how repeated realizations are counted. A different task set or resource price can change which member of a fixed pair performs better.
  These results are exact, complete censuses of the specified finite sets of constructions and comparisons. The atlas provides a reproducible worked model for examining how action constraints, counting rules, and cost assumptions affect comparisons. It illustrates established distinctions between information quantity and decision value rather than proposing a new general law of their relation. $I(W;R)$ measures retained world information, and payoff measures task accuracy.

</details>


### [200] [More Agents, More Samples, or More Thinking? Untangling Coordination, Ensembling, and Test-Time Compute in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17610)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过元分析整合九项实证研究（516个多智能体配置），在样本数与推理计算资源匹配的基线下重新评估多智能体语言模型，发现多智能体系统相对基线平均准确率约为-0.042，在74%的可比对比中不优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体语言模型评估常将"通信架构"与"单次贪心推理"的基线直接比较，混淆了协调机制、采样多重性与推理计算量三个因素，导致多智能体收益被系统性高估。作者希望建立资源可控、基线可比的评估框架，厘清多智能体通信的真实增益来源。

Method: 构建六维资源向量R与四级基线阶梯；合成九项主要实证评估、516个唯一多智能体配置；分四类基线比较，重点考察与样本数匹配（Category B）、资源预算匹配（Category C）的基线；在log odds尺度上采用多级随机效应模型，并进行多项敏感性检验；同时给出审稿核查清单与核算工具。

Result: 相对单次推理基线（Category A），多智能体准确率差异总体为负，剔除最大贡献研究后接近零；相对样本数或资源预算匹配的基线（Category B/C），平均准确率差为-0.042，随机效应模型在多级敏感性检验下仍为负；在74%的可比对比中多智能体不优于基线，几乎所有研究的均值非正；对同时报告两类基线的研究，由贪心转向采样基线会使准确率差平均再降0.038。

Conclusion: 在同等样本数与计算预算下，多智能体通信并不天然带来性能提升，既有正向结论多源于基线不对等。由于智能体共享相同证据时通信不引入新信息，正收益需依赖证据划分、外部验证或跨模型错误多样性等条件；作者据此提出评估规范与核算工具，呼吁后续研究采用资源匹配的基线设定。

Abstract: Evaluations of multi-agent language models often compare communicating architectures against a single greedy pass. This practice confounds coordination with sample multiplicity and inference compute. We synthesize nine primary empirical evaluations comprising 516 unique multi-agent configurations organized along a resource vector R with six dimensions and a baseline ladder with four categories. Against baselines with a single pass (Category A), multi-agent accuracy differences are negative overall and near zero when omitting the largest contributing study. When evaluated against baselines matched on sample count (Category B) or resource budget (Category C), multi-agent systems average -0.042 in accuracy, and multilevel random effects modeling on the log odds scale remains negative across sensitivity checks. Multi-agent accuracy equals or falls below the baseline in 74% of these comparable contrasts, and study means are nonpositive in almost all evaluated studies. Across studies reporting both baselines, moving from a greedy pass to a sampled baseline shifts accuracy differences by -0.038 on average. Because communication adds no information when agents share identical evidence, positive returns require partitioned evidence, external verification, or error diversity across models. We provide a reviewer checklist and accounting utilities.

</details>


### [201] [LLM-Guided Cluster Lifecycle Management: Deciding When and How to Evolve Behavioral Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17616)
*Himanshu Kulkarni,Siddharth Krishnamurthy,Jianhong Xin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用LLM在“原地”做行为聚类簇的生命周期决策（分裂、合并、退役、新建、不动），避免阈值触发与重聚类跟踪的硬编码规则；并按标签丰富度预先固定“统计→语义→融合”的通道选择策略。在YooChoose、MOOC、News-Category三个数据集上，策略选出的LLM模式F1均最高，干预更少且更克制，增益主要来自精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于用户/物品交互学习的行为簇会随行为演化而漂移（碎片化、收敛、消失、新生），需要持续维护。现有两类范式——阈值触发（硬编码统计量越界即执行预设动作）与refit-and-track（MONIC式，每窗口重聚类再推断簇间迁移）——都把决策硬编码进阈值或重聚类规则，缺乏自适应判断能力，且调好的阈值在不同数据集间迁移性差。

Method: 让LLM在无需重聚类的前提下，基于无阈值的全群体簇视图（统计健康度、标签、成员文本、残差质量）逐簇决策动作（split/merge/retire/create/no-action）。由于有效信号通道依赖标签信息量，预先固定“丰富度条件化策略”：标签区分度低时用统计量，高时用统计+语义融合。对比对象为调优阈值启发式与MONIC强基线（steelman）。

Result: 三个数据集（标签丰富度YooChoose 0.17、MOOC 0.50、News-Category 0.70）上，策略选中的LLM模式F1均最高，且干预次数显著更少、更克制；配对Wilcoxon p≤0.003，bootstrap 95% CI不含零。增益更多体现在精确率而非召回率。随丰富度上升，有效模态由统计转向语义/融合：语义在YooChoose上有害，融合在MOOC上近似中性，News上语义通道是必需的。单一prompt模板与单一固定策略可服务三个数据集，而调优基线跨数据集迁移即失效。

Conclusion: LLM原地决策可作为阈值规则与重聚类跟踪之外的第三条演化聚类维护路径，且无需逐数据集调参。作者将“统计→语义/融合”的通道迁移定位为在三个数据集上观察到的实践者诊断信号，而非经验证的普适规律。

Abstract: Behavioral clusters learned over user or item interactions drift as behavior evolves: some fragment into sub-behaviors, some converge, some disappear, and new patterns emerge. Two paradigms dominate the maintenance of evolving clusterings. Threshold triggers fire a pre-specified action when a hardcoded statistic crosses a cutoff; refit-and-track (MONIC-style) re-clusters every window and infers transitions from how the new clusters align with the previous window's. Both hardwire the decision into a threshold or a re-clustering rule. We study an alternative in which a large language model (LLM) makes the lifecycle decision in place.
